# Train — Feature Documentation

> **2026-09-24 — Üveg U5 (`mezo-me75u.5`).** The Train area's second half wears the dark glass: the **Terv** tab (the plan landing, the run's own page, a day and its editor, the weekly muscle review and one muscle's story), the **plan library** (Edzéstervek, Lezárt futamaid, the frozen report, the two-run compare), the **sablonok** (list, the template's read-first page, its day-plan editor), the **plan wizard**, the **saját edzés** composer, and the three **Terhelés sub-pages** (izomtérkép, minden izomjel, minden mozgásod — the Terhelés landing itself was already U4).
>
> The slice's one behavioural addition, owner-approved: „A heted" now separates **megvolt · ma · jön**. A day already trained reports what HAPPENED — logged sets, measured minutes (`actualDuration`), worked exercises — read off the week's completed instances through the SAME cached hook the Terhelés tab uses (`useWeekMuscleLog` → `logic/mesoWeekDone.ts`), never the plan's numbers under a „Megvolt" stamp. A record count is deliberately absent: it exists only on the frozen meso report, so the card states what the instance itself carries. Mock mode has no persisted instances, so every day there is honestly „Jön".
>
> Both surfaces that draw this week — the Terv landing and the run page — render ONE component (`components/MesoWeekDays.tsx` → `components/MesoDayCard.tsx`), so they cannot drift. The card's anatomy went through three owner rounds and settled as: a header band (eyebrow over title · state stamp · chevron on a shared right edge), a hairline, then a content zone of a fixed 62px body-map column beside three EQUAL fact boxes with the muscle row on the same left edge. Its `--c` is the day's dominant muscle hue, so Push / Legs / Pull read as three different cards. Three new sprite icons (`t-template`, `t-compare`, `t-trash`). Look: [`uveg-style-bible`](../design_2.0/2026-09-23-uveg-style-bible.md), parity reference [`uveg-edzes2.html`](../design_2.0/prototypes/uveg-edzes2.html).

> **2026-09-24 — Üveg U4 (`mezo-me75u.4`).** The Train area wears the dark glass: Mai (frameless today hero + glass CTA, glass session/energy/muscle cards), the in-workout screen (glass workout cards in the muscle colour, 3D set verdicts, the no-sheen dock whose resting ring fills around a 3D clock, glass dialogs, floating sheets), the two-step closing ceremony (3D stars; a separate records card with type + value chips; the stats card now also lists the session's **accepted challenges** with a round hit/miss icon — owner-approved content, fed by the existing `useChallenges` list via `ceremonyChallenges()`), the review, Terhelés, Sport + log (per-sport 3D icons via `Sport.art3d`), Futás + builder, Medálok (`medalLabels` tier → t-record/t-tick), Gyakorlatok + story. A second owner-approved addition: a **„A mai küldetések” row** at the top of the workout list opens the existing Küldetések glass until the first set is logged. Behavior otherwise unchanged. Look: [`uveg-style-bible`](../design_2.0/2026-09-23-uveg-style-bible.md) (U4 rules 26–33), parity reference [`uveg-edzes.html`](../design_2.0/prototypes/uveg-edzes.html).

> **2026-09-23 — Üveg U3 (`mezo-me75u.3`).** The sport capture sheet wears the U3 capture look (glass floating sheet, 3D header art, scale cells, the shared lit Mentés). Behavior unchanged. Look: [`uveg-style-bible`](../design_2.0/2026-09-23-uveg-style-bible.md), parity reference [`uveg-nap.html`](../design_2.0/prototypes/uveg-nap.html).

> The strength + conditioning area (plan, execute, log, analyze). **Status: ✅ done (FE mock + FE real + Spring Boot backend)** — the only gaps are clearly-flagged Phase-3 AI/analysis features. Lives under the **`Edzés`** domain in the docked bottom `TabBar` (`app/navModel.ts`'s `train` row) — **four owner-approved tabs (2026-09-12, Train Titanium T4, `mezo-88iwa.5`)**: `Mai` (`/train/mai`) · `Terv` (`/train/mesocycles`) · `Terhelés` (`/train/week`) · `Gyakorlatok` (`/train/exercises`). **The six-tile Mozaik hub is retired** — `/train` itself has no face of its own any more; it index-redirects to `Mai` (below). The nine-tab `SubNavDropdown` shell and its `tabs.ts` were already gone before this ([ADR 0032](../decisions/0032-five-tab-ia-dissolved-section-shells.md), [ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md)); Train Titanium T4 retired the hub that replaced them.

---

## 1. Summary

Train is the largest mezo domain: the area for planning and executing strength work and conditioning. It covers periodized **mesocycles** (`Mesociklus`), the active week's **gym split**, full-screen **workout execution** with per-set logging, an **exercise catalog + per-exercise records** explorer, **sport** (`Röplabda` — volleyball, cross, and TRX, 0..n scheduled slots per day, `mezo-05v6`) scheduling/logging, and structured **interval running** (`Futás`) plans. A `Mai` page surfaces all three rhythms (gym + sport + running) for **one selected day at a time** via a `DayStrip` navigator (default: today; `mezo-9bbc`), with the detailed Mon–Sun agenda on the `Heti` page.

**Mozaik 2.0 is the Nth render layer over the same unchanged model.** The Design 2.0 F2 wave (`mezo-d20.3.1`…`.3.9`) dissolved the Train section shell and re-faced most of these pages onto the tile-mosaic vocabulary — but it was *recompose, not reinvent*: **every data hook, mutation, contract and honest-state rule below survived it verbatim**. `useTrain`/`useRunning`, the workout-session model (`logic/workoutState.ts`), the recommendation/progression engines, the medal engine, the meso template/run split and the whole backend slice are exactly what they were before the redesign. What changed is the surface: the nine sub-tabs became one hub + six tiles, the sub-tabs became full-page siblings, emoji became clay SVG sprites, and the entrance choreography became a one-shot rise-stagger.

**Status per layer — all ✅ done:**

- **FE mock** (Phase 1): synchronous static fixtures, every tab renders without a backend.
- **FE real** (Phase 2): each read/write swapped to `/api/train/*` behind the same hooks; no signature change.
- **Backend** (Phase 2): full `feature/train/**` Spring Boot slice over Postgres — mesocycles, workout execution, sport, exercise catalog/records, running.

**Phase-3-planned (🟣, flagged throughout, do NOT assume live):** `niggleWarning`, the live **cross-load → volume recompute engine** (Sport AND Running cross-load are still static; since `mezo-ly27` the **static `sportMuscleLoad.ts` heuristic is its FE interim** on Heti's group glass (`GroupGlassBody`, formerly `MuscleWeekSheet`, T12) — this is a DIFFERENT thing from the weekly performance ramp below, which IS live), `shoulderLoadTrend`, sport `jumpCount`/`intensity`/`team`/`season` capture, gym-schedule **`duration`** (the gym-time **schedule is now live** — see §4 `GymScheduleSlot`; only per-day duration stays out of scope), copy-week, and the active-workout **Voice** tool (still no UI at all, Phase 3). **Progressive Overload Plan 2 SHIPPED** (design spec `2026-07-25-progressive-overload-design.md`, above; PR #80 + the Phase B arc surface, `mezo-hi9m`): the performance-driven **weekly volume ramp** is a real engine now — `VolumeProgressionService.rolloverIfDue` writes `MuscleGroupVolumeLogEntity.currentSets` + a `volumeRecompute` audit live, per calendar week (toward MRV only for a muscle explicitly marked **Emphasize**; **MAV** for the default **Grow** tier since `mezo-3m5m` — §4 `#### Volume progression`) — and the read-only **`Mezociklus áttekintő`** volume-arc surface + the Mai entry card + Heti's `Mezociklus áttekintő` chip are live too, §2/§4. **Progressive Overload Plan 3 SHIPPED too** (`mezo-gj42`) — completing the progressive-overload checklist alongside Plan 1 (`mezo-5pfe`, the RIR-aware, deload-aware intensity engine + the in-workout `ProgressionBanner`/prep-chip signal): a deterministic (non-LLM) daily **`overload` challenge** surfaces the day's biggest recommended jump as a proactive workout-challenge card — a guaranteed +1 alongside the existing LLM-generated PR/Depth/Volume ones, §2. Mechanism/evaluator/display live in [proactive.md §HBWI](proactive.md). **The cross-load → volume engine is a separate, still-Phase-3 gap** — the weekly ramp above reacts to logged performance (reps/RIR) within Train itself, NOT to Sport/Running load; do not conflate the two. Also worth knowing: the mock's rich `volumePerMuscle`/`volumeRecompute` **prose** provenance is still fuller than what the real engine writes — the engine's `volumeRecompute.changes[]` is a terse per-muscle change list (`"+2 sets"`/`"tartás"`/`"deload"` + a one-line HU reason), not narrative. **The old inert `90s` timer chip is gone, not deferred** — Napiv S5 (`mezo-8141`) replaced it with a real rest countdown (since `mezo-xt65` an **in-card CTA-morph bar**, §2), whose 150s/90s duration is a **hardcoded** per-exercise-type heuristic (`restTimer.ts`), not a data field; a configurable per-exercise rest duration would need a new backend field (Phase 2 backlog candidate, not Phase 3).

**Driving design docs (read before changing this area):**
- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-10-phase2-backend-design.md` — Phase-2 slice map.
- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-11-train-write-clean-slate-design.md` — the "T0 clean slate" write/ghost rule.
- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-12-exercise-catalog-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-12-exercise-records-design.md`.
- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-14-train-running-slice-design.md` — the `Futás` slice.
- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-25-progressive-overload-design.md` — Progressive Overload master design (mesocycle-aware intensity + volume + daily-challenge tie-in). **Plan 1 (RIR-aware intensity engine + in-workout signal, `mezo-5pfe`) and Plan 2 (weekly volume ramp — Phase A `VolumeProgressionService` PR #80 + Phase B `Mezociklus áttekintő` volume-arc surface, both `mezo-hi9m`; the ramp's ceiling became tier-resolved rather than always-MRV in `mezo-3m5m`) have shipped** — see §2/§4 below. **Only Plan 3** (the daily-challenge overload tie-in, `mezo-gj42`) is still design-only.

---

## 2. User-facing behavior

Recurring gym and sport schedules are edited at `/settings/train/gym` and `/settings/train/sport`. TrainWeek no longer carries the Időpontok chip; the sport empty-state setup CTA opens the canonical editor. Existing workout logging, plan and one-off event editing stay here. Schedule sheets await persistence, retain drafts on errors, and mock changes propagate through the shared cache. See [central settings](settings.md).

**Quick sport capture appearance (`mezo-62xey`).** `SportLogSheet` shares the capture header, its sport sculpture and two-row numeric selectors. (That capture family is one of the three surfaces the 2026-09-17 rollback did not reach — it still wears Titanium-era art; see the design_2.0 index.) Its three supported sport branches, editable duration/count controls, RPE, volleyball shoulder load, note and mutation payload remain unchanged. The fabricated volleyball-only advice block is removed. The full `/train/sport/log` flow and the workout-session routes are unchanged.


### The four-tab navigation (`app/navModel.ts`'s `train` row, `/train` redirect, Train Titanium T4, `mezo-88iwa.5`)

**The six-tile Mozaik hub (`pages/EdzesHubPage.tsx`, `/train`) is retired.** Its two bands (the today's-session hero + the six-tile mosaic) are gone outright, not moved wholesale onto another page — every route they pointed at was already its own full-page sibling before this, so nothing downstream of a tile lost a page. `/train` itself now has no face of its own: **`TrainIndex`** (`app/router.tsx`) unconditionally `<Navigate replace>`s it to `/train/mai`, forwarding the legacy Heti deep link verbatim — `/train?day={0..6}` becomes `/train/mai?day={n}` — because the day view, not a hub, is what a day deep-link has always meant. Callers that still `navigate('/train')` (`KalauzSheet`, assorted back-fallbacks) keep working through the redirect; none needed repointing.

**Four owner-approved tabs live in the global bottom `TabBar`** (`app/navModel.ts`'s `train` row, the same matrix-row idiom as every other domain — Fuel's row is the exemplar, `navModel.ts:63-76`), never an in-page tab strip:

| Tab | Route | `owns` (deep routes that still light this tab) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Mai** | `/train/mai` | `/train/session`, `/train/review`, `/train/sport`, `/train/custom` |
| **Terv** | `/train/mesocycles` | `/train/templates`, `/train/futas` |
| **Terhelés** | `/train/week` | `/train/gym` |
| **Gyakorlatok** | `/train/exercises` | `/train/medals` |

`owns` is mandatory for every one of these deep routes or the wrong tab lights up (the Fuel bug this idiom exists to prevent). The tab homes read straight off the owner's 2026-09-12 coverage decision: **logging lives on Mai** (session execution, the post-workout review, sport, and custom/meso-less workouts all forward to the day view that started them), **plans live on Terv** (the mesocycle library IS Terv's own root; template management and the `Futás` running block builder are its deep pages), and **history sits beside the weekly volume on Terhelés** (`Heti`'s own root; `Gym` is still the thin `TrainWeekPage` alias, below). `Gyakorlatok` is the exercise catalog; `Medálok` is a separate page, reachable from the Gyakorlatok face via its own entry row (added in the T4 final-review fix wave, below) and owned by that tab (`owns: ['/train/medals']`) — the two were never one page, only one tab. **No page face changed for this** — every route above is the same page it was under the hub; only what lights the tab bar and what `/train` resolves to moved.

**Only `/train/session` stays outside the tab bar** (`hideChrome` in `AppLayout.tsx`) — the active-workout flow is the domain's one genuinely chrome-free surface. Every other deep route listed above (`/train/review/:workoutId`, `/train/mesocycles/new`, `/train/mesocycles/:id` the run page, `/train/mesocycles/:id/days/:day`, `/train/mesocycles/:id/week` and `.../week/:muscle`, `/train/mesocycles/:id/overview` (redirects to `.../week`), `/train/mesocycles/:id/report`, `/train/mesocycles/compare`, `/train/mesocycles/templates/:id`, `/train/futas/:id`, `/train/custom/{new,:id}`, `/train/sport`, `/train/medals`) DOES show the `TabBar` — which is exactly why each of them needs (and, per the entry rows added below, now has) a reachable `owns`-covered entry point from its owning tab's face: a page a user can land on with the tab bar showing but no way back to it is a dead end, not a full-screen sibling.

**Titan-dark scope — GONE** (`mezo-ju4j6.3`, 2026-09-17). The Titanium period put a `titanDark` gate on `AppLayout.tsx` that forced the prototype's dark-graphite skin over every `/train/*` route (plus two Nap routes and all of Fuel). The design direction was reversed on 2026-09-17 and the scope class was stripped in Phase 2 of the rollback: the domain is **light-first and theme-aware** again, like every other surface. `AppLayout.titanDark.test.tsx` was replaced by its inverse, `AppLayout.lightFirst.test.tsx`, which asserts the class cannot come back. The `hideChrome` rule for `/train/session` above is unaffected — that is geometry, not skin.

**The whole domain is re-dressed to the restored Mozaik/Clay world** (`mezo-ju4j6.11`/`.12`/`.13`, 2026-09-19 → 09-20, then ranked app-wide by `mezo-ju4j6.19`): Mai + the in-workout list, Terv + Terhelés, and Gyakorlatok + the sport flow + both ceremonies. **Behaviour was frozen in every one of those slices** — they are skin, naming and CSS-ranking changes only, each closed with a reverse parity checklist on its bead. Everything this document says about what Train *does* therefore still holds; what it says about how Train *looks* is the style bible's business ([`docs/design_2.0/2026-09-17-restored-world-style-bible.md`](../design_2.0/2026-09-17-restored-world-style-bible.md), Appendix C + C.1 + C.2 cover this domain).

**`GymPage` is a thin alias, deliberately kept** (`pages/GymPage.tsx`, `mezo-d20.3.2` + `mezo-d20.9.1`). Gym's content folded into `Heti` (below); the file survives as `export function GymPage() { return <TrainWeekPage /> }` — rendering `TrainWeekPage` directly, **not** a client `navigate`, so the URL is untouched while the content is unified. It exists because `/train/gym` is still a **live navigation target** for three real callers — `MesoStartSheet`'s post-start redirect, `MesocyclePlannerPage`'s post-create redirect, and `CustomWorkoutBuilderPage`'s `useBackNav` fallback — plus any PWA bookmark. Removing the alias means rewriting those three call sites, which the F8 cleanup deliberately left alone.

**Page faces are not uniform, and that is honest.** The F2 wave re-faced the hub, `Heti`, `Medálok`, `Gyakorlatok`, the `Mezociklus` hub + its three full-screen siblings, and the whole session flow onto the Mozaik vocabulary (`MozaikPage`/`PageHead`/`PageHero`/`PageBody`/`StatStrip`/`Tile`/`Mosaic`/`CollapsibleStrip` from `@/shared/ui/mozaik`, clay sprites from `@/shared/ui/clay`, the `--mz-*` tokens in `styles/prototype.css`). `Sport`, `Futás`, `Sablonok`, the planner/builders and the template editor still wear their pre-redesign DS `.page-header`/`.pghead-np` faces — Sport and Futás got **load-bearing behavior fixes** in their own slices (`mezo-d20.3.4`/`.3.5`, below) rather than a re-face. Nothing about their data or contracts differs; only the chrome is older.

### The ⓘ explain layer — one primitive, thirteen placements, eight screens (`components/InfoButton.tsx`, Train parity P3, `mezo-b516k`)

**The smallest control the prototype has, and production had none of it.** A 22px icon-only button sits at the END of a heading (or inlined at the end of a hero sentence); tapping it opens the workout-style 3D glass with one short paragraph of plain-language explanation. Ported from the companion-titanium prototype's `info()` helper (`plan-pages.js:131`, `load-pages.js:11`, `gyak-pages.js:11` — the same two lines three times) and the `infoGlass()` surface it opens (`navigation.js:88-101`). The prototype's own comment there records the owner call of 2026-09-15: **„Every ⓘ opens the workout-style 3D glass, never the drawer"** — so `InfoButton` renders `GlassBox` (which IS that glass: it ports `.wo-glass`/`.wo-glass-card` from `session.css`), never a `Sheet`.

**The primitive owns exactly three things GlassBox leaves to its caller.** (1) The trigger: `.pl-info` (22px glyph, `opacity:.75`, the prototype's own `#i-info` sprite glyph, ported verbatim into `clay-icons.svg` — a clay icon, never an emoji), with the prototype's exact `aria-label`, **`"<title> — mit jelent?"`**. (2) The **44px touch target**, which is house rule, not prototype — and it is anchored to the glyph's **LEADING edge** (`.pl-info::after { left: 0; top: 50%; transform: translateY(-50%) }`), not centred on the glyph: every placement is the LAST child of its row, so the whole 22px overhang falls to the button's right, clear of the heading's own text. A centred box overlapped the last ~4px of the heading text and stole taps meant for the heading (measured with `elementFromPoint`; jsdom cannot model it, so the real guarantee is a **geometric probe in the Playwright layout suite** — `tests/layout/layout.spec.ts`, `/train/week`'s „Izomcsoportok ezen a héten": the heading text's right edge resolves to the `H3`, the glyph's centre resolves to the `BUTTON`). (3) **Route-change close** — the prototype auto-closes on `hashchange` (`navigation.js:100`); `GlassBox` listens for Escape/backdrop/✕ only, so without a `useLocation` effect an open glass would survive a client-side navigation and hang over the next screen.

**The glass head is GlassBox's own, not a second header.** `GlassBox` gained two optional props, **`art`** (leading clay icon) and **`eyebrow`** (the „MEZO · RÉSZLET" line), rendered INSIDE `.gl-head` before the `<strong>{label}</strong>` title — the prototype's one-row `icon | eyebrow-over-title | ✕` anatomy (`.wo-glass-head`, `session.css:219`). Both default to nothing, so every pre-existing caller renders exactly as before. Do NOT stack a second header block next to `GlassBox`; that was the first cut and it produced a two-row head the prototype does not have.

**The tint is FIXED at `#bca6f1` for every info glass** (`INFO_TINT`, the prototype's `--ex-color` in `infoGlass`). It is the explain layer's identity colour, deliberately NOT the section's own accent — do not tint it per screen.

**The copy is owner-iterated and ships WORD FOR WORD.** Every one of the thirteen paragraphs was written with the repo owner in the prototype; call sites pass the prototype's string verbatim. Never paraphrase, never re-wrap, never "improve" it while touching a page — a copy edit here is an owner decision, not an implementer's. The live parity walk (2026-09-17, both apps side by side) confirmed **all 13 blocks byte-identical**.

**The three adjudicated exceptions, and nothing else.**
1. **Closed-run report (`MesoReportPage`, anchored on the `Izmonként · indulás → elért csúcs / plafon` eyebrow).** The prototype's title is „Mit mutat a sáv?"; production's muscle-journey section renders TEXT ROWS, not bars (a documented P1 decision), so that title would point at a bar that is not on the screen. The **copy stays verbatim** (it describes the journey, not the bar); only the TITLE reads **„Hogyan olvasd?"**, and it **flips back when the surfaces slice (`mezo-fsz2r`) returns the bars** — tracked in the parity matrix §22 so the debt cannot go silent.
2. **Template story (`MesoTemplateStoryPage`).** Production used to print this explanation as a static paragraph under the heading; the prototype keeps it BEHIND the button. The paragraph was **deleted** in the same change — the sentence must be said once, in the glass. The same ruling was then applied to `MesoMusclePage`'s near-duplicate `.pl-foot-say`, which restated the muscle glass's own copy.
3. **Muscle page (`MesoMusclePage`).** The copy interpolates the muscle's **real MEV** (`A ${mev} alatt nincs elég inger…`) — always the page's own value, never a literal number.

**One recorded art divergence.** Terhelés's sport placement passes the prototype's `volley` art; `clay-icons.svg` has no volleyball glyph, so the sport card's own `i-sport` glyph — one line above the button — stands in.

**Where the thirteen live.** `MesoDayPage` ×2, `MesoMusclePage`, `MesoTemplateStoryPage`, `MesoReportPage`, `TrainWeekPage` ×3, `TrainWeekMapPage`, `TrainWeekMozgasPage` ×2, `ExerciseStoryPage` ×2. Two CSS classes carry it — `.pl-info` (the trigger) and `.pl-info-copy` (the paragraph, ported from `load.css:179`'s `info-glass-copy`) — both registered in `prototypeCssStructure.test.ts`. `.ld-h3` gained `.pl-h3`'s `display:flex; align-items:center; gap:7px` so the Terhelés headings can hold a trailing glyph. **Adding a fourteenth means: find the prototype's `info(...)` call, copy its title and copy string exactly, attach the button as the LAST child of the same heading/sentence.**

### `Mai` — the one-day view (`pages/TrainTodayPage.tsx`, `/train/mai`)
> **Route moved, page unchanged (`mezo-d20.3.1`).** Mai used to BE `/train`; the hub took that path and Mai moved to `/train/mai` with its whole day view (`DayStrip`, per-day retroactive logging, the morning-training card) intact. The hub's hero is a one-session *summary* of the same logic — the depth below still lives only here. **The Titanium face (Train Titanium T5, `mezo-88iwa.6`) replaced Mai's DS-migrated `.page-header`/`.trainhero` chrome with a poster-anatomy hero** — the mechanics below (URL-driven day selection, the three-state gym gate, the morning nudge) are unchanged; only the markup and two new honest-state cards are new.

**A one-day page over a `DayStrip` navigator (`mezo-9bbc`), Titanium-faced (`mezo-88iwa.6`, T5).** The legacy `.page-header` (`Eyebrow` + `PageTitle` + a `← Ma` back action) is **gone** — the poster below names the session and the `DayStrip` names the day, so the strip is the page's first element; today's own `DayStrip` chip took over `← Ma`'s job (it **clears** `?day=` instead of pinning today's index, so a reload tomorrow still opens on the real today). Render order: the horizontal **`DayStrip`** (`components/DayStrip.tsx` — `role="tablist"`, one `.daychip` `role="tab"` per weekday: `MA`/day-number + per-session `SessionTone`-coloured dots + a done-count/`pihenő` line, pre-derived by the pure `logic/dayStripItems.ts`) → the **shown day's** hero card(s) in time-of-day order — a rounded **`.tr-day` poster** for the gym slot **only while today is shown** (full-bleed until `mezo-fsz2r`, the surfaces slice, gave it the prototype's `26px` radius + hairline back and sat it on the page rail) (replaces the old `.trainhero`; the unchanged three-state gate, below, now drives `.tr-day`'s own status pill + in-poster CTA instead of a separate eyebrow/button pair), or a read-only-capable `.todaycard` gym/sport/run card on any other day (`components/TodaySessionCard.tsx`, **one card per scheduled sport slot**), plus — on any day, today included — one permanently-logged gym-tone `.todaycard` per **completed custom (saját) instance** of that day (`SAJÁT · MEGVAN` tag, no state chip, `DoneBar` → `/train/review/{id}`; `mezo-9bbc` final review, so a day whose only session was a saját workout is no longer mis-rendered as a rest day) → **today-only, the energy card and the muscle-impact card** (new in T5, see their own paragraphs below) → **the `.tr-alt` "Vagy inkább" quick pair**, after the two cards whenever it renders (a one-off workout / a sport log — the same two sheets the page already mounts) → the Mezociklus áttekintő + Sport entry rows (`.mesorow`, gutter-aligned to the poster's own 18px inset — see below) → a **today-only open-instance resume card**, gated to a day with no gym schedule slot (a started-but-unfinished custom/meso-less workout would otherwise have no resume affordance anywhere on Mai, since the poster above only covers a scheduled gym day — `mezo-ws2x` Finding 4; `todaySession.openWorkout` + `workout` from `/today`'s open-wins resolution) → a rest-day card when nothing is scheduled on the shown day **and** no such open instance is showing (today's copy offers "+ Saját edzés"; a non-today rest day is copy-only, "Ezen a napon nincs tervezett edzés.", no CTA; the two cards are mutually exclusive by construction) → a **today-only dashed `+ Saját edzés` footer**, rendered exactly when **neither** the rest-day card **nor** the poster's own `.tr-alt` pair is (exactly one of the three one-off-workout entries shows at a time; the `mezo-9bbc` rework had dropped the unconditional entry, restored by `mezo-eahv`; non-today selections stay read-only, no footer) → the **today-only** morning-training nudge. **The weekly agenda ("Heti") lives on the sibling `Heti` page** (`/train/week`, see the subsection right below) — Mai renders none of it. The `WeeklyDayRow` `.dayrow` day-card list and the `LoadTiles` weekly-load summary that used to sit there are both **gone** (Train Titanium T12's Terhelés reface retired both outright, `mezo-88iwa.13`); Heti's provenance note (gym/sport pacing) survives on the new face. `TrainTodaySkeleton` mirrors **this** shape (a 7-chip `.daychip`-geometry `DayStrip` placeholder → the poster block), not the weekly timeline that moved to Heti, so the loading→content swap does not reflow. Mai's no-meso ghost likewise promises no „Heti terv” section — only the hero ghost + the dashed "+ Saját edzés" row remain. One-shot entrance choreography (`mezo-d20.11`) stages every card in document order on mount and on every `DayStrip` swap (`EntranceGroup replayKey={shownDay}`).

**Selected-day state — the URL is the single source of truth (`mezo-9bbc`).** `selectedDay: string | null` (`null` ⇒ today) is **derived from `?day={0..6}` on every render** (a `DAY_ORDER` index — the Heti drill-in contract, below), never mirrored into component state; a `DayStrip` tap and today's own chip both write it back via `setSearchParams(…, { replace: true })` (day-hopping inside Mai is a view switch, not a history step the back button should unwind chip by chip). Deriving rather than seeding is what keeps URL and view in sync in **both** directions: a reload or a back step keeps the day you were on, and any navigation that drops the query **without remounting the route element** snaps the page back to today. Hostile input still resolves to today: `null`/`''` skip `Number()` entirely (`Number(null) === Number('') === 0` would silently pin every plain `/train/mai` visit to Monday), and non-integer/out-of-range values are rejected; an unparseable value is left in the URL untouched. `shownDay = selectedDay ? agenda.find(day===selectedDay) ?? todayRow : todayRow`. 

**Which day is "today" — one date-based derivation (`mezo-9bbc` final review).** `todayRow = agenda.find(isToday) ?? agenda.find(date === localDateString())`, and `todayIso = todayRow?.date ?? localDateString()` feeds **all** of the page's date math (`sessionState`, `loggedSportOn`, the retroactive `date` threading). The **flag wins** because mock mode's "today" is a fixture flag pinned to `Csü` (`data/train/train.ts`) while the clock is whatever day it really is — reading the clock for the date math while the agenda's today came from the flag made every card on that row compute against the wrong calendar day (on any real weekday but Thursday the mock run card merged in by `todayRuns` read `TERVEZETT`/`ELMARADT` and lost its log CTA; the frozen visual clock, a Thursday, was the one day this was invisible). The **calendar fallback** covers real mode, where `deriveGymSchedule`/`toSportSchedule` only stamp `isToday` on a day that also has a gym/sport slot (§9): a true rest day or a run-only day flags no row at all, and without the fallback today's own agenda row — its rest copy, its completed custom instances — was simply missing. `isTodayShown` is date-based for the same reason, `selectedDay === null || (shownDay?.date ?? todayIso) === todayIso`; the old flag test rendered today as "not today" whenever you tapped today's **own** chip on a rest/run-only day (or drilled in from Heti's today row), which showed the weekday instead of the poster, offered a pointless back action, suppressed the morning nudge, and — functionally — dropped both the `+ Saját edzés` CTA and the in-progress custom-workout resume card, leaving no way to start or resume a custom workout from Mai. Switching days is a pure client-side re-render over the already-fetched week agenda — **no refetch**. `todayRuns` (the running hero's date-based prescribed sessions, `runSessionsForDay(activeRunningBlock, todayIdx())`) only merges into the shown day while today itself is shown; a non-today selection uses the agenda's own day-of-week-matched `running` list instead — the mock fixture's `isToday` flag (pinned to Csü) and the real calendar date are intentionally two different clocks (§9).

**Today-gym poster anatomy (`.tr-day`, Train Titanium T5, `mezo-88iwa.6`; re-surfaced by `mezo-fsz2r`) — replaces the old `.trainhero`.** A **rounded, floating poster** (`border-radius: 26px`, a `#ffffff14` hairline, no shadow, `padding: 20px 20px 18px`, `margin-inline: 6px` → a 21px rail it shares with every section below it), only while today is shown and a gym plan exists. **It was a full-bleed band until the surfaces slice** (`mezo-fsz2r`, audit §20.2): the port had dropped the prototype's radius and border and added a `border-bottom` rule plus a `var(--shadow-sm)` lift, which read as a toolbar band rather than the prototype's poster. The prototype's own rail is 40px (its demo page is inset 20px and the card adds 20px more); production's is 21px, because the house scroller's `--screen-gutter` is 12px — an accepted, recorded deviation whose cause is the page container, not the card (matrix §Surfaces). Anatomy: an absolute-positioned **status pill** (`.tr-day-status`, top-right — `BETERVEZVE`/`FOLYAMATBAN`/`KÉSZ`, one condition drives both the pill and the CTA below, no second state) → an overline row (a clay `i-edzes` glyph + `MA {time} · {phase} · GYM`) → an `h2` session title (`workout.title`) → a sub-line (`{meso.shortTitle} · {week}. hét / {weeks}`) → a **muscle constellation** (`.tr-day-constellation`, one small `MuscleChip` per region today's plan actually loads — `dayImpact(workout.exercises)` filtered to `plannedSets > 0`, dropping silent regions) → **fact pills** (`.tr-pills`: exercise count · set count · `~{n} perc` via `estimateSessionMinutes`, held at 0 — no flash-then-swap — while the calibrated timing profile is still pending · the gym type) → the **in-poster three-state CTA** (`.tr-start`, full-width, replaces the old separate `Indítsuk`/`Folytassuk`/`Eredmény` buttons below a `.trainhero`): the same three-state gate as before (see the gym-hero gating paragraph below) now renders **inside** the poster as `.tr-start.is-go` (`Indítsuk` → `openSession`), `.tr-start.is-resume` (`Folytassuk` + the open instance's non-skipped set count), or `.tr-start.is-review` (`Eredmény` + the completed instance's set count → `/train/review/{id}`). After the energy card and the muscle-impact card, gated on the same `gymPosterShown` condition, the **`.tr-alt` "Vagy inkább" pair** offers the two other doors without leaving the page — `Egyedi edzés` (opens `CustomWorkoutSheet`) and `Sport naplózása` (opens `SportLogSheet` pre-selected to volleyball) — the same two sheets the dashed footer and the rest-day card already open, so exactly one of the three one-off-workout entries ever shows.

**Session-card anatomy off the poster (`mezo-lruy`).** The sport/run/non-today-gym cards keep the pre-Titanium rhythm — tag → time → title → fact pills → full-width CTA: **`TodaySessionCard`** (`.todaycard`, `.todaycard-{tone}` modifier, `logged` state class — since `mezo-jyua` a thin wrapper over the shared `shared/ui/ItemCard`, both now importing `ReactNode` the same way; the markup and every class name are unchanged, and the `train-*` visual goldens passing untouched are the proof the extraction was pixel-neutral, see [`_platform-design-system.md` §3](_platform-design-system.md)) covers the recurring/prescribed sport and run sessions AND, since `mezo-9bbc`, a **non-today gym day** too (`ctaLabel` omitted ⇒ read-only, e.g. a day with no `gymDayTarget`) — a `.todaycard-top` row opens with a 44px `.todaycard-icon` shield (the modality's clay glyph, swapped for a ✓ check glyph once `.logged`) beside a `.todaycard-head` block: a `.typetag-{tone}` tag (**five tones** — `gym`/`sport`/`cross`/`trx`/`run`, `SessionTone` in `logic/sportKinds.ts`; appends `· MEGVAN` once logged) + `.todaycard-time` (hidden once logged) + a display `.todaycard-title`; while still unlogged, a trailing `.todaycard-state` chip carries one of exactly **four** `sessionState` labels (`MOST`/`MA`/`ELMARADT`/`TERVEZETT`, `logic/sessionState.ts`'s `SESSION_STATE_LABEL`) — **a logged card shows none of them**, its eyebrow reading only `{tag} · MEGVAN` instead. Below the head: the slot's facts as `.metapill`s (sport: `perc` · role · location; running: RPE target · rounds; non-today gym: exercise count · duration), then a full-width `.todaycard-cta` (log / start) — or, once logged, the shared `DoneBar` in place of the pills+CTA entirely — accent-tinted per modality through the `--tc-accent`/`--tc-wash` custom-property pair. **Heti no longer renders a per-session day-card list at all** — the `WeeklyDayRow` `.dayrow`/`.s` markup this paragraph used to describe on Heti retired with the Train Titanium T12 Terhelés reface (`mezo-88iwa.13`, see that subsection); Heti's own cards are per-MUSCLE-GROUP now, not per-session.

**Energy card (`.tr-energy`, Train Titanium T5, `mezo-88iwa.6`) — today's movement kcal, honestly split.** Today-only, rendered below the hero cards whenever the shown day carries at least one training block (gym/sport/running) and, on a gym day, the timing profile isn't still pending (no "+0 kcal" flash before the calibrated minutes land). Pure module **`logic/trainDayEnergy.ts`** (`trainDayEnergy(blocks, restPerHour)`, zero UI imports, table-tested): the **net** activity-energy model (`mezo-32m82`) — `kcal = (MET − 1) × restPerHour × hours` per block, via the shared FE mirror `data/train/activityEnergy.ts` (`netKcal`/`blockEnergyKind`, bound to the backend's `ActivityEnergyModel` by the shared golden vectors) — summed into `plannedKcal` and the done blocks' own `earnedKcal` share; a done block with a persisted `loggedKcal` shows that verbatim instead of re-estimating. `restPerHour` (`restKcalPerHour(bmr, weightKg)` — BMR/24, else 1 kcal/kg/h, else `null`) reads `goalResponse.tdeeBootstrap?.bmr` plus `weightKg` off `useGoal()` — the **same** hook Fuel's own served target reads — falling back through `goal.currentWeight ?? goalResponse.startWeightKg ?? 0`, never a static default, so "no weight/BMR on file" renders the identical honest fallback Fuel already has. **Surface (`mezo-fsz2r`): neither this section nor `.tr-mus` below it is a boxed card any more** — the prototype gives them no background, border, radius, shadow or padding of their own (they sit inside a `.tr-card` wrapper production never ported, which is Mai's own content slice `mezo-edg42`, not a surface fix), so the ports' `var(--surface-card)` box was removed and both sections now read as transparent stretches of the page. „Card” below names the anatomy, not a painted box. Anatomy: overline `A MAI KERETEDHEZ` → `h3` `Amit a mozgásod hozzáad` → **known weight**: a big `+{plannedKcal} kcal` headline plus a two-line split (`{earnedKcal} kcal már megszolgálva` · `{plannedKcal - earnedKcal} kcal a tervben`) → **unknown weight** (`dayEnergy.known === false`): the headline and split are replaced entirely by one honest sentence ("Ha megadod a súlyod, kiszámoljuk, mennyit ad a mai mozgásod a keretedhez.") rather than a fabricated number → a permanent `Becslés, nem mérés.` footnote either way. A run block with no plan-level duration (`RunPrescribedSession` carries none) stands in at the shared `DEFAULT_RUN_MIN` (45′, `data/train/activityEnergy.ts`) — the same default Fuel's own planned previews use for a duration-less run block; the old, mismatched 40′ constants are gone from every surface. **A deliberate three-state asymmetry with the muscle-impact card below:** the energy card's gym block only flips to "earned" on `completedTodayWorkout` — a workout still open (`FOLYAMATBAN`) contributes zero earned kcal — while the impact card's `doneByMuscle` reads the open instance's logged working sets too (`completedTodayWorkout ?? todaySession?.openWorkout`), so a mid-workout day can show a partially-lit muscle bar next to an energy split that still reads "0 kcal already earned"; kcal is only honest once the session is over, load is honest the moment a set lands.

**Muscle-impact card (`.tr-mus`, Train Titanium T5, `mezo-88iwa.6`) — what today's movement loads, in words.** Today-only, rendered whenever at least one region carries `plannedSets > 0` — never an all-zero table on a genuine rest day. Two sources, in priority order: **(1) a gym day** reads the pure module **`logic/dayImpact.ts`** (`dayImpact(exercises, doneByMuscle)`, zero UI imports, table-tested) off today's plan — per-region sums of planned working sets translated into a plain-language ladder (`enyhe`/`közepes`/`erős`; `ma nem kap` drops the row), joined against `doneByMuscle` (the open/just-completed instance's own logged working sets, mapped exercise→muscle) so the track bar's lighter fill never overflows the darker planned width even when more sets were logged than planned; **(2) a rest day carrying a sport slot instead** falls back to `logic/sportMuscleLoad.ts`'s static per-sport-kind heuristic table (`sportLoadForWeek`, aggregated per region, max load across today's slots — a **product heuristic**, not physiology, honestly labeled as such) so a röplabda-only rest day still gets a card. Surface: boxless since `mezo-fsz2r`, exactly as `.tr-energy` above. Anatomy: overline `HATÁS AZ IZOMZATODRA` → `h3` `Mit terhel a mai mozgásod` → one `.tr-mus-row` per region (a `MuscleChip` + the HU region label + a two-layer track bar, planned width behind a done-width overlay, both against the region's own rail color + the ladder word) → a footer note that flips between the gym-table note ("A halvány sáv a tervezett terhelés, a világos a már megszolgált.") and the sport-estimate note ("A sáv a sport becsült terhelése — nem mért adat.") depending on which source fed the table, always closing with `Becslés, nem mérés.`. **Honest-state addendum (sweep finding f, `mezo-88iwa.6`):** when a gym day **also** carries a sport slot, source (1) wins and the sport's load is silently **not** counted at all (the branches are `if`/`else if`) — the heading "Mit terhel a mai mozgásod" would otherwise over-promise coverage it never computed. The footer note gets one appended sentence in exactly that combination, disclosing that the gym plan is what's shown and the sport's own load is estimated separately, so the card never claims more than it actually measured.

**Convergence, ordering & done-state.** Where gym, sport (volleyball/cross/TRX), and running converge, on **Mai** — **Heti stopped rendering a per-session agenda at all with the Train Titanium T12 reface** (`mezo-88iwa.13`; the `WeeklyDayRow` weekly-row surface this paragraph used to describe there is deleted, along with the type it used to own — `WeeklyAgendaDay` moved to `agenda.ts` itself, since `weeklyLoad.ts`/`weekAgenda.ts`/`dayStripItems.ts` still needed it). **Mai's hero cards** (for the shown day) order **by time-of-day** rather than a fixed gym→sport→running modality order — a morning run renders above an evening gym; untimed sessions sort last — through the pure helper `daySessions(day)` (`features/train/logic/agenda.ts`), which flattens a day's `{gym, sport[], running[], custom[]}` — `WeeklyAgendaDay.sport: VolleyballSession[]` (now `logic/agenda.ts`) holds **0..n slots per day**, each carrying its own `sport: volleyball|cross|trx` (resolved via `sportOf(slot)`, `logic/sportKinds.ts`) — into typed `AgendaItem`s carrying `timeOfDay` and sorts ascending (null/`''` → `'99:99'` last, then stable by modality). The union's **fourth member, `kind: 'custom'`** (a completed saját instance; untimed, so it always sorts last) was added in the `mezo-9bbc` final review so Mai's one-day view stops losing an already-shipped session type: it drives Mai's `SAJÁT · MEGVAN` card and a gym-tone `DayStrip` dot (`toneOf` maps `custom → 'gym'`, and Mai's `isDone` predicate returns `true` for it by construction). The Mon–Sun agenda itself is built once by the shared pure `buildWeekAgenda` (`features/train/logic/weekAgenda.ts`, lifted out of `TrainTodayPage` unchanged, `mezo-9bbc`) and consumed by `TrainTodayPage` (and, outside Train, Fuel's protocol/orb-fill/recap logic — §4/§6). Gym time comes from the standalone gym schedule (§4 `GymScheduleSlot`); running time from each session's `timeOfDay`. On **Mai** you can log a sport (volleyball/cross/TRX) or running session inline via the shared `SportLogSheet`/`RunLogSheet` — since Task 9 (`mezo-9bbc`) this works on **today or any past day** of the shown week, not just today: each card's CTA is driven by the same three-way `sessionState` read as its `stateLabel` (above) — `planned` (a future day) ⇒ no CTA, read-only; `missed` (a past, unlogged day) ⇒ **`Pótold`**; `now`/`today` ⇒ the original copy (`Logold a session-t`/`Naplózd a futást`). Both sheets declare an optional `date?: string` prop, but **only `SportLogSheet`'s Mai wiring actually omits it on today** — `date={shownIso === todayIso ? undefined : shownIso}` — because `SportSessionCreateRequest.date` is optional and the server defaults to now when the body has no date (`sheets/SportLogSheet.tsx`); `RunLogSheet` (`sheets/RunLogSheet.tsx`) is wired to **always** receive `shownIso` from Mai (`date={shownIso}`, never omitted there) because its contract has no server-side default — `date` is resolved client-side regardless of whether the prop was passed, `date ?? localDateString()`, never the old UTC-shifting `new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)` (which mis-logged a late-evening/early-morning run onto the wrong day in CET). The omission semantics matter at other call sites too: `SportPage`'s own log CTA and `RunningPage`'s own log CTA both omit `date` entirely (today-only, no retroactive path there), relying on the same optional-prop/internal-default behavior each sheet already has — Mai is simply the one call site that supplies a non-today value. A logged past session's `DoneBar` stays tappable, re-opening the sheet against that same past date. **Mock mode honours the same `date`** (`data/train/trainHooks.ts` — `logSportMutation`'s mock branch appends `huMonthDayDow(req.date ?? localDateString())`, mirroring `runningHooks`' already-correct spread of the body): it used to hardcode today, so in the PWA demo a `Pótold` appended the session to **today** — the past day kept its `ELMARADT`/`Pótold` and today's card wrongly flipped to `MEGVAN` (`mezo-9bbc` final review). The sport CTA also **pre-selects the tapped slot's sport** (`SportLogSheet`'s `initialSport` prop, driven by `setSportLogSport(k)`). **Heti has no per-session tap of its own to drill from any more** (T12 retired that alongside `WeeklyDayRow`) — the day-drill contract itself is unchanged and still lives on Mai's own `DayStrip` (`toMai(day) = navigate('/train?day=' + index)`, covered by `app/router.trainIndexRedirect.test.tsx`), Heti simply has no caller into it left; Heti still has no retroactive-logging affordance of its own. **Once a session is logged, its hero/row flips to a `done` state — matched by date AND sport**, so a mixed day (e.g. a noon TRX slot + an evening volleyball match) flips each slot independently (`loggedSportOn(iso, k)`/`sportDoneOn`, day-parameterized since `mezo-9bbc` — the shown day's `iso`, not always today): the `.todaycard-state` label chip disappears and the type tag gains a `· MEGVAN` suffix instead, and the fact-pills + CTA are swapped out entirely for the shared `DoneBar`'s sage summary of the logged effort (volleyball: `RPE {n} · {n}p · váll {n}`; cross/TRX: `RPE {n} · {n}p` — no `váll`; running: `RPE {n}` (+ ` · {n} kör` when the round count is known); **gym**: a **three-state gate**, today only — see the gym-hero gating paragraph below). **Sport summaries append `· {n} kcal` when the wire carries one** (T8 Task 6 — `SportSessionResponse.kcal`, read straight through `sport.sessions`, no re-derivation): the em dash rule applies, so an old session logged before this wiring (or any `kcal: null`) simply omits the suffix entirely rather than showing a fabricated `0 kcal`. The `DayStrip` chips carry the same done-state, pre-computed per session by `dayStripItems(agenda, isDone)` into a `doneCount`/`dots` pair. Ghosts entirely when there is no active mesocycle ("+ Tervezz mesociklust") on **both** Mai and Heti — Mai's ghost still offers a dashed "+ Saját edzés" row below its own placeholder, since a custom workout needs no meso at all (`mezo-ws2x`, see the `Saját edzés` subsection below).

**Mezociklus áttekintő + Sport entry rows (Progressive Overload Plan 2 Phase B, `mezo-hi9m`; the Sport row since `mezo-88iwa.5`).** Below the poster and its `.tr-alt` quick pair, two `.mesorow` nav rows (secondary navigation, so they sit under the hero now that the legacy page-header is gone): an active meso renders a `{shortTitle} · {phase} · W{n}/{N} →` entry card that opens `/train/mesocycles/:id/overview` — since mesocycle pages v2 (Task 4) that route redirects to `Heti vizsgálat` (`/week`, see §2's `Heti vizsgálat` subsection) — hidden entirely without an active mesocycle, unconditional on which day is shown; and a **`Sportjaid és szezonod →`** row to `/train/sport` (relabeled from `Sport naplózása és szezonod` in the `mezo-88iwa.6` sweep — the old copy collided with the `.tr-alt` pair's own `Sport naplózása` quick chip sitting a few rows above it). **Gutter (sweep finding b, `mezo-88iwa.6`):** both rows' wrapper padding is `0 6px 12px`, not a bare `24px` — the scroller (`.screen-content`) already contributes `--screen-gutter` (12px), so `+6px` lands on the same **21px rail** the poster itself sits on since `mezo-fsz2r` (it was an 18px inset while the poster was full-bleed) and the `.tr-alt` wrapper above it already matches, instead of drifting 18px wider than everything else on the page.

**Morning-training nudge (`mezo-67rb`, `MorningTrainingCard`) — today only.** When a gym slot falls **after** the wake-derived window end, a gentle sage `.mtr` card offers moving the offending slot(s) into the morning window. The window is `morningWindow(useSleepGoal().goal.wakeTime)` = **[wake + 60′, wake + 6h]** (`features/train/logic/morningWindow.ts` — a Train-only circadian window; **since `mezo-u6jx`** it has no backend mirror, the habit engine's `training_done_today` tick is date-presence, see [habit.md](habit.md)); a slot *earlier* than the start is still morning training, so only `time > end` offends. „Áthelyezés a reggeli ablakba" is a **one-tap `saveGymSchedule` replace-all** (offenders land on the window start, the rest pass through; mock also `setQueryData(['train','gymSchedule'])` for parity), and „Maradjon így" **content-key-snoozes** it in `localStorage` (`mezo-morning-training-snooze`, keyed on wake + the offending slots so any wake/schedule change re-arms the card). Since `mezo-9bbc` the card only mounts while `isTodayShown` — selecting another day hides it. No red, no streak pressure — the ADR 0010 tone. See [`_platform-design-system.md`](_platform-design-system.md) §3 for the `.mtr` family.

**Gym-hero done-day gating (`mezo-cd8s`) — today's three-state gate, unchanged mechanics, Titanium-faced markup (`mezo-88iwa.6`, T5).** The gym hero is a **three-state gate** driven by today's instance, not by `weekDoneDates`, and only ever renders while today is shown: (1) **no instance** → the poster's `.tr-start.is-go` start CTA (`Indítsuk`, `openSession` → `/train/session`); (2) an **open** (started, unfinished) instance (`todaySession.openWorkout`) → the status pill flips to `FOLYAMATBAN` and the CTA becomes `.tr-start.is-resume` (`Folytassuk → · {n} szett kész`, `n` = the non-skipped set count); (3) a **completed** instance (`completedTodayWorkout` — since the cross-day start (`mezo-p7rp`) the resolved day's most recent finished instance **of the current Mon–Sun week**, not just today's) → the pill reads `KÉSZ` and the CTA becomes `.tr-start.is-review` (`Eredmény · {n} szett · megnézem`) opening the read-only review `/train/review/{completedTodayWorkout.id}` (`WorkoutReviewPage`, renders the shared `WorkoutSummary` in `closed` mode). Both `todaySession`/`completedTodayWorkout` come from `/today` and are **real-mode only** (null in mock → the poster is always `Indítsuk`, byte-identical to Phase 1). **Since `mezo-p7rp` the `/today` day resolution is `open instance > ?day= param > weekday label`** (§4) and the session route accepts **`/train/session?day={templateDayId}`** (`ActiveWorkoutPage` threads `useSearchParams` → `useTrain({ workoutDay })`) — this is how a non-today direct-start (Heti weekly row / Gym day-card, `mezo-bxpg`) pins its template day; the open instance winning resolution means the poster's `Folytassuk` and a hard reload resume a cross-day session too. **A non-today gym day on Mai** (`mezo-9bbc`) is a plain read-only-capable `TodaySessionCard`, not the poster: title/exercise-count come from the meso template (`activeMeso.days`, since `MesoDay` has no `title` — its human label is `type`) rather than `/today` (which only describes today), `logged` is **template-keyed** — `weekWorkouts` holds a completed instance of that day's template this week (`mezo-z9kft`; it used to read the performed-date-keyed `gymDoneDates`, so yesterday's plan finished today left yesterday `ELMARADT` and flipped TODAY's strip chip to done; the DayStrip's gym chip reads the same `gymDayDone`, mock keeps the date-keyed signal) — and the CTA (`Kezdjük el`, omitted when `gymDayTarget` resolves to `null`, e.g. a rest template day) routes via the same `gymDayTarget` helper Heti uses. **When the open instance belongs to that day's template** (`todaySession.templateSessionId === md.id` — the param-less `/today` names the open instance's template) the card reads `FOLYAMATBAN` and the CTA becomes **`Folytassuk`** → `/train/session?day={id}` (`mezo-z9kft`: leaving a cross-day session used to leave only `ELMARADT · Kezdjük el` on the day it was started from). `gymDayTarget` always pins `?day={id}` when the day has one — `MesoDay.current` is the backend's template-row `status == 'active'` (demo seed: Csü), **not** "today", and routing it to the plain session started today's template instead. **`ActiveWorkoutPage` pins the resolved day (`mezo-z9kft`)**: every resume path (floating button, poster, resume card) opens `/train/session` without `?day=`, and once `finishWorkout` refetches the param-less `/today` nothing is open, so it falls back to TODAY's weekday template — the ceremony then scored/listed today's muscles (and `mergePlan` folded today's exercises in), or bounced to `/train` on a rest day. The page latches the first resolved `templateSessionId` into `useTrain({ workoutDay })`, seeding the pinned key from the param-less cache so the switch neither refetches into a skeleton nor remounts the session (`ActiveWorkoutPage.crossDay.test.tsx`). **Heti has no per-day gym row of its own any more** — the direct-start/review resolution this paragraph used to describe on `WeeklyDayRow`'s weekly gym rows (`onReviewGym`/`onStartGym`/`onOpenGymDay`, keyed off `useWeekWorkouts()`'s date→id map) retired with the component in the Train Titanium T12 sweep (`mezo-88iwa.13`) — Heti's group cards are per-muscle-group now, not per-day, and open the glass detail (see the `Heti` subsection), never a session. The mechanics live on **only via Mai's non-today `TodaySessionCard`** (the `gymDayTarget` helper, previous paragraph) and, since the direct-start flow (`mezo-bxpg`), via that same tap handler resolving done/not-done up front (`weekWorkouts.find` by `templateSessionId`) and routing straight to `/train/review/{id}` instead of `/train/session` — no intermediate sheet mediates the choice (`GymDaySheet` is deleted). The `/train/session` route itself is still **gated**: when today is completed with nothing open, `ActiveWorkoutPage` redirects to the review (`<Navigate to={/train/review/{id}} replace />`, after the pending/no-workout guards) so the prep screen (challenges included) is unreachable until next week.

**App-wide resume FAB (`mezo-78sd`).** Beyond the hero, an open instance also surfaces a shell-level coral resume FAB (done-set badge → `/train/session`) on every route via `app/FloatingReturnLayer.tsx` — plus a lavender chat bubble that stays available ON `/train/session` itself (the tab-bar-less screen), so a mid-workout Mezo chat is one tap out and one tap back. The layer, its `useOpenWorkout()` data hook and the route rules are documented in the [_platform-design-system.md](_platform-design-system.md) integration table; nothing in the Train pages changed for it (the hook reuses `useTrain`'s param-less `['train','workoutToday', null]` key and the TrainTodayPage `completedWorkout` gate).

### `Heti` — the Terhelés face: drawn week, body map, group glass (`pages/TrainWeekPage.tsx`, `/train/week`)
**Train Titanium T12 re-face (`mezo-88iwa.13`) — replaces the earlier Mozaik-2.0 Gym fold-in wholesale.** Source of truth: `docs/design_2.0/prototypes/companion-titanium/load-pages.js` (`loadHero`/`mapCard`/`groupCards`/`groupGlass`/`sportCard`/`movementCard`) + `load.css`, ported onto tokens as the `.ld-` section in `styles/prototype.css`. The page tells ONE story top to bottom: a full-bleed hero with a **DRAWN** percent (big numeral + growing bar, never a bare text percentage) says how deep into the week you are and carries the `Időpontok` chip (`GymScheduleSheet`), the `Mezociklus áttekintő` chip (→ `/train/mesocycles/{id}/overview`, redirects to `Heti vizsgálat`) and a medál count — then a tappable **body-map doorway** ("A tested térképe" → `/train/week/terkep`, `TrainWeekMapPage`) — then **group cards**, one per budget group, each opening a `GlassBox` with its own per-muscle detail (`GroupGlassBody`, below) — then, only while the week actually has sport/running minutes, a sport summary card — then a **movement doorway** ("Minden mozgásod a héten" → `/train/week/mozgas`, `TrainWeekMozgasPage`) — then the `+ Saját edzés` dashed CTA and the gym/sport provenance note. Without an active meso the page ghosts to the `+ Tervezz mesociklust` placeholder inside the same scaffold.

**The day-strip function inventory (owner-approved) — nothing dropped, only moved.** The old Napiv-era `WeeklyDayRow` `.dayrow` list left this page entirely; every function it carried has a named, still-tested home: drill-to-day (`toMai`, `/train?day=N`) → Mai's own `DayStrip` (the `/train` redirect contract stays covered by `app/router.trainIndexRedirect.test.tsx`); `Időpontok`/`GymScheduleSheet` → kept here, as a hero-row chip; non-today gym → session routing and done-day → review routing → Mai's poster CTA / past-day handling; the `heti-terheles` kalauz anchor → re-anchored to the hero (was `heti-napok` on the day list); medál/`StatStrip` facts → the hero (medál chip via `useMedals`) and the group glass (per-muscle detail + XP). The old `LoadTiles`/`ZoneMiniGrid`/`MuscleWeekSheet` surfaces retire with this face too — their data is what the hero, the groups and the glass now draw; `WeeklyDayRow`, `LoadTiles` and `ZoneMiniGrid` are **deleted outright** (components + tests, T12 sweep) once the grep confirmed zero production consumers. `WeeklyDayRow`'s `WeeklyAgendaDay` type had real consumers beyond the component (`weeklyLoad.ts`/`weekAgenda.ts`/`dayStripItems.ts`) and moved to `logic/agenda.ts` — the module that was always its real logic home — rather than dying with the component.

**Two row sets, deliberately (not a duplicated read) — `logic/loadWeek.ts`'s header note.** `doneRows` is the week as LOGGED (`weekZoneRows({ plannedDays, completed })`) — the hero totals, the group numbers and the group WORDS all read this, so nothing ever credits a session that hasn't happened yet. `heatRows` adds TODAY's plan on top (`todayPlan`) — only the body map needs it, since without it the 'entering' status ("today's session crosses the floor") is unreachable. `mapWeekHeat(doneRows, heatRows)` folds the two back together honestly: an 'over' status is kept **only** when `doneRows` alone already crosses the budget — the map's own caption ("ami már dolgozott") must never be inflated by tonight's still-unlogged plan.

**Group cards + glass.** `loadGroups(doneRows)` sorts groups by done-contribution first; each card shows `{done}/{planned} szett`, a `shareOf` bar, the group's own honest word (`wordFor` — "ez a hét itt már megvan" / "erre a hét második fele épül" / "még N szett van hátra", never a bare percentage) and a `sok` flag (house amber, never red) when the week's PLAN exceeds the fatigue budget (`planBudget > 1` — verbatim the meaning the retired `ZoneMiniGrid`'s `⚠` carried). Tapping a card opens `GroupGlassBody` in a `GlassBox`: the migrated `MuscleWeekSheet` content, scoped to that one group — the group's own muscle heads with their planned week (sets/rep-range/frequency), the sport/run `▲` stimulus chips (`sportLoadForWeek`, a static heuristic estimate that never blends into the set counts) and an XP forecast (`growthForecast`, honestly silent — "XP-előrejelzés ehhez a csoporthoz még nincs" — when the plan carries no weight anchor to forecast from).

**THREE subscreens, own routes, own full page (T12 Task 4 + Train parity P2 Task 1) — never a drawer.** `TrainWeekMapPage.tsx` (`/train/week/terkep`, "Izomtérkép") draws the full front+back `BodyMap` off the SAME `doneRows`/`heatRows` this page derives — a mode toggle re-renders the same heat (`mapWeekHeat` for "Eddig megvolt" vs `mapHeat(heatRows, 'planned')` for "A heti terv"), never re-derives it — plus the untouched-muscle list and the sport-reach note. `TrainWeekMozgasPage.tsx` (`/train/week/mozgas`, "Minden mozgásod a héten") draws gym and sport/run minutes side by side but NEVER mixed into one number (`movementWeek`, `logic/loadWeek.ts`): the gym side is an ESTIMATE (minutes from `estimateSessionMinutes` off the plan, kcal from `trainDayEnergy`'s MET math, both needing a weight on file), the sport side is what was actually LOGGED this week (volleyball sessions + run logs, real minutes). **Sport-side kcal is now wire-carried too (T8 Task 6):** each logged sport session's `kcal`/`kcalIsEstimate` (`SportSessionResponse`) and each run log's `kcal` (`RunSessionLogResponse`) come straight off the BE-owned estimate/override, no FE re-derivation. `movementWeek` keeps an **all-or-null gate** on the sport sum (`sport.every(s => s.kcal !== null)`): the sum shows only once **every** sport/run entry logged this week carries a kcal — one old session logged before this wiring (or any session from a weight-less athlete, where the BE itself has no estimate to give) still reads `null` and hides the whole sum rather than under-reporting it; the gap self-heals as weeks roll off. It also lists every planned sport/run event this week with its region-load `▲` chips (`sportLoadForWeek(...).events`).

**The third subscreen: „Minden izomjel" (`TrainWeekJelekPage.tsx`, `/train/week/jelek`, `mezo-lf3cv` P2 Task 1).** The prototype's own `train/2/jelek` screen, and the one the Izomtérkép's quiet foot row (`.pl-row.is-quiet`, „Minden izomjel · A 21 izom, saját jellel, régiónként ›") has always pointed at — the doorway existed in the design and the screen did not, so a reader who followed it landed on the router's catch-all. It is a **legend, not a chart**: all six regions (`REGION_MUSCLES`, the same source the region chips and the authoring picker read), each region block naming its own count (`Mell 3 izom`, `Hát 4 izom`, `Váll 3 izom`, `Kar 6 izom`, `Láb 4 izom`, `Core 1 izom`), each of the 21 cells drawn by the shipped `MuscleChip` silhouette — never a second geometry path and never an emoji. **A LIT cell means that muscle was worked this week**, read off the real log (`useWeekMuscleLog().details` → `workedMusclesThisWeek` in `logic/loadWeek.ts`), not off a fixture: in mock mode no workout instance is ever persisted, so nothing is lit and nothing is guessed lit, and one closing line („…még egy izmod sincs naplózva — amint egy edzés lezárul, a jele kigyullad.") makes an all-dark screen read as an empty week rather than a broken render. The back pill reads `‹ Izomtérkép` (it is a child of the map, not of Terhelés) and the head docks inside the slim `.ld-hero` like §13/§14's do. `Terhelés` `owns`s the route explicitly (`navModel.ts`) even though the `/train/week` prefix would already light the tab — an `owns` entry is the subpage SAYING where it belongs.

**The two-scales decision, written down.** This page and its subscreens deliberately stay on `weekZone.ts`'s fatigue scale (`budgetOf`/`GROUP_MEV`) rather than the planning-time `weeklyBands`/current→ceiling scale `WeeklyBandsCard`/`MesoEditor` use — that split predates T12 (`mezo-d20.16`, §4 `#### Weekly bands`) and this slice does not close it; `WeekZoneStatus` (`'below'|'entering'|'in'|'over'`) flows unchanged from `weekZoneRows` through `mapHeat`/`mapWeekHeat` to `BodyHeat['level']`.

**GymPage stays the alias.** `/train/gym` still resolves to `TrainWeekPage` (`GymPage.tsx`, `return <TrainWeekPage />`) — three in-app callers and PWA bookmarks still point there; the sameness test (`GymPage.test.tsx`) rides along unchanged.

### `Terv` — the running plan's own landing (`pages/MesoTervPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles`, Train Titanium T9 Task 3, `mezo-88iwa.10`)

**THE INVERSION.** The running block is no longer a card inside a library — it IS the page. Was `MesocycleLibraryPage` (`mezo-d20.3.6`'s 4-tile mosaic + clickable `ActiveMesoCard` hub hero); the library itself moved intact to `MesoKonyvtarPage` (Task 2, below) behind a quiet doorway, and the active run's hero grew into the whole landing. Ported from the prototype's `planHome` (`docs/design_2.0/prototypes/companion-titanium/plan-pages.js:60-129`):

- **`.pl-poster`** — the week numeral + „. hét / {weeks}", a progress ring, a phase pill (`PHASE_LABEL` maps `phaseChip`'s `Rámpa`/`Csúcs`/`Deload` onto the Hungarian `Emelkedés`/`Csúcshét`/`Pihenőhét`), the block's own name, one plain sentence and the week arc. The whole poster is a `<button>` door straight into the builder (`/train/mesocycles/:id`) — `ActiveMesoCard`'s whole-card idiom, carried over verbatim.
- **„A heted"** — one `.pl-day` card per TRAINING day (full weekday name, a `MA` chip on today, boxed szett/perc/gyakorlat facts, per-muscle mini bars off `dayTileData`, `wizard/dayTiles.ts`) opening that day's own page (`MesoDayPage`, below); rest/sport days render as slim rows instead.
- **`.pl-dests`** — two quiet doorways, not a header `+ Új` chip: „Melyik izmod hol tart" → `.../week` (the old `Heti vizsgálat` mosaic tile's reachability, kept) and „Edzéstervek" → `.../konyvtar`, which now owns `Új blokk` — one creation entry, not two.
- **the close row** — opens the *existing* `MesoCloseSheet` (the builder's own sheet, reused, not forked).

Derivations are the app's shared ones, never fixture math: `phaseChip`/`weekDots`/`runBands`/`nextRolloverChips` (`logic/mesoBands.ts`) and `dayTileData` — the same modules the builder and the week page read, so the landing, the builder and the week page can never disagree about the same block. Language: one plain Hungarian sentence, no jargon — „pihenőhét" never „deload" — and clay icons only, never emoji. **No active block:** the page says so honestly (`GhostState`: `"Még nincs mesociklusod — itt fognak élni a blokkjaid."`) and still shows the `Edzéstervek` doorway, since the `Tervezett` list lives there now, not here; the T0 ghost hint stays.

### `Edzéstervek` — the library LANDING, behind a doorway (`pages/MesoKonyvtarPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/konyvtar`, Train Titanium T9 Task 2 + T10, `mezo-88iwa.10`/`mezo-88iwa.11`)

**T10 (`mezo-88iwa.11`) turned this page into the library LANDING.** A `.pl-dhero.pl-lhero` hero — back pill docked inside the hero, `data-kalauz-anchor="konyvtar-hero"`, the line „Itt élnek a terveid — ami fut, ami jön, és ami már mögötted van." — over a `.pl-poster-foot` facts row that states only what the lists themselves carry: `N fut · N következik · N sablon · N lezárva` (`useTrain().mesocycles` + `useMesoTemplates()`). Below it, three blocks and two doorways:

- **„Most fut"** — the running plan as one `.pl-lib-card.is-now`, opening `/train/mesocycles` (the `Terv` landing, which IS that run's page). At most one; nothing at all when no run is active.
- **„Következnek"** — the queued (`planned`) runs, one `.pl-lib-card.is-queued` each: a `{startDate}-tól` head (`huDate`, `logic/mesoDates.ts`) and fact boxes that never repeat it — weeks, nap/hét (off the split string's own tail, `splitFrequency`; the box is dropped when the split carries no such tail) and the split name — plus a reassurance line: „Akkor indul, amikor a mostani terved lezárul." when something is running, „Nyisd meg, és onnan indíthatod." when nothing is. **A queued plan is never started from here.** The card body opens the plan's OWN page (`/train/mesocycles/{id}`, the builder), whose dated `Aktiválás · {date}` CTA is the deliberate path — `activateMesocycle` silently archives whatever is currently running (`TrainService.archiveActiveMesos`) with no close ceremony and no frozen report, so a one-tap chip on a list row was a data-losing tap.
- **`.pl-lib-new`** — the one loud CTA, „Új terv összeállítása" → the planner. One creation entry on the page, never two.
- **the two `.pl-dest` doorways** — „Sablonjaid" → `/train/templates` and „Lezárt futamaid" → `/train/mesocycles/futamok` (both below). The closed runs are no longer a section on this page; they have a route of their own.

Loading renders the geometry-matched `MesocycleSkeleton` (`role="status"`). What T10 did **not** keep: the DS-era 4-tile `Mosaic` (its `Történet`/`Sablonok`/`Új blokk`/`Futóblokkok` entries are the two doorways + the one CTA now), the `PlannedMesoCard`/`ArchivedMesoCard` components (deleted in the T10 sweep, `mezo-88iwa.11`) and the `Történet` section itself, which moved verbatim to `MesoFutamokPage` before being refaced. Kalauz gained a `train-konyvtar` entry (T3, anchor `konyvtar-hero`).

**Superseded by Train Titanium T9 (above).** Through mesocycle pages v2 (`mezo-d20.3.6` Mozaik re-face + Task 2's `mezo-d20.15` clickable hero) this same page carried BOTH jobs at once: a **4-tile mosaic** (`Heti vizsgálat`/`Sablonok`/`Új blokk`/`Történet`, replacing the old `.mesorow` nav row) sitting above a clickable **`ActiveMesoCard`** hero (whole-card `<button>`, three derivations off `logic/mesoBands.ts` — week dots, a phase chip, up to five band chips — plus a `Ma · {day} · {type}` line) over the planned/history sections. T9 pulled the hero out into its own page (`MesoTervPage`'s `.pl-poster`, above) and kept the mosaic + planned/history sections here as `MesoKonyvtarPage` (`Edzéstervek`, above) — same `logic/mesoBands.ts` derivations, new poster face, split page.

**DS-migrated (`mezo-setx.6.8`) — the layer under it.** `.page-header` + `PageTitle`/`Eyebrow` head with a `.pgact` `+ Új`; the `ActiveMesoCard` hero takes `.todaycard`'s gradient formula and the primary rail (its coral was UI chrome, not data-viz); `MetaStat` becomes the **third** component on the shared StatStrip cell vocabulary, after `GymStat` (`.6.4`) and `SportStat` (`.6.5`). The planned/archived cards move to the DS type roles (18/600 and 16/600 titles, caption bodies, the notes line on `.text-meta-sm`) and their inline-sized `Chip`s drop the overrides. The `+ Új mesociklus tervezése` footer joined the shared `.dashedcta` (and **moved to the `Sablonok` tab as `+ Új sablon tervezése` in `mezo-tlwa`** — the library has no dashed footer any more). **Two carried-forward lessons:** `MetaStat` drops the ported `whiteSpace: nowrap` — a nowrap PHRASE in a three-across strip silently overflows at phone widths (the `.loadrow` finding, `.6.3`) — and its value sits at caption 14, not the strip's 20px numeral size, because `Pull / Push / Legs` broke over three lines at 20.
**Template/run split (`mezo-meyc.1`) — the library is RUNS-ONLY since `mezo-tlwa`.** Sections in this order: the 4-tile mosaic (above; it carries the `Sablonok · {n}` entry that used to be a `.mesorow` nav row — the whole template section, its cards and its dashed CTA moved to the dedicated `Sablonok` page, next subsection) → **`Aktív`** hero → **`Tervezett`** → **`Történet`** (was `Archív` — the closed runs; each `ArchivedMesoCard` carries an `Újrafuttatás` **and**, since `mezo-tlwa`, a `Sablonná` action). Header chip "+ Új" opens the planner — with the dashed CTA gone it is this page's only creation entry. Run cards open the builder. **Both start paths still funnel into one sheet:** the `Sablonok` tab's `Indítás` opens `MesoStartSheet` on the template directly, `Újrafuttatás` first calls `rerun(meso.id)` (which materializes a template for a legacy run, backend-side) and opens the same sheet on the returned `templateId` — **the sheet wiring stays on the library page** precisely because of that second path, even though no template card lives here any more. `ArchivedMesoCard` is no longer a whole-card `<button>` (buttons cannot nest) — the body is the tappable region, the rerun chip sits in a footer row; **since `mezo-meyc.2` the body opens the run's frozen report** (`/train/mesocycles/:id/report`), not the builder — a closed run has no builder to open. **Napiv S4 (`mezo-8141`) gave this page, the planner, and the builder a `.pghead-np` page-head only** (over `Edzés · Mesociklusok`) — their body content still runs on `--coral`. The planner and builder **kept that face through Design 2.0**: `mezo-d20.3.7` audited the 5-step wizard against the prototype and found only one real fidelity gap worth fixing (below), not a re-face; deeper Builder/Planner restyling stays deferred, see §9.
**Történet selection + report state (`mezo-meyc.4`).** Each `ArchivedMesoCard` footer now states whether the run HAS a frozen report — a `riport` chip when `meso.hasReport`, a muted `nincs riport` label otherwise (a plain `<span>`, not a button: the card body already goes to the report, and a nested button would fight the selection mode). The card's eyebrow date reads the actual `closedAt` (formatted like every other date on the card), falling back to the plan's `endDate` only for a legacy run closed before `closedAt` existed. The section head gained an **„Összevetés" toggle chip** (`aria-pressed`, rendered only when there are ≥2 closed runs — nothing to compare otherwise) that flips the section into **selection mode**: a card tap now SELECTS the run instead of opening its report (the card becomes an `aria-pressed` toggle with a ✓/○ marker, full opacity + coral border when selected, and **both** footer actions — `Újrafuttatás` and the `mezo-tlwa` `Sablonná` — step aside so the only meaningful tap is the selection), max **two** runs, kept in **tap order** — which is exactly what makes the taps the `a`/`b` choice. With two picked, an „Összevetés megnyitása" `CtaGhost` navigates to `/train/mesocycles/compare?a={first}&b={second}`; below two, a `Válassz két lezárt futamot (n/2)` line stands in its place. Toggling the mode off clears the selection (a pick surviving an invisible mode would fire out of nowhere the next time it is switched on). Outside selection mode every existing behavior is untouched: tap → report, `Újrafuttatás` → rerun, `Sablonná` → fork (below).

**Run report + close flow (`mezo-meyc.2`, spec §2).** Closing a run is no longer a bare status flip: the builder's `Meso lezárása` button opens **`MesoCloseSheet`** (`sheets/MesoCloseSheet.tsx`, chrome mirrors `MesoStartSheet`) — a short "the report freezes at this moment" summary line + an optional „Saját értékelés" textarea → confirm → `closeMesocycle(id, selfEval?)` → on success navigates straight to the freshly-written **report page**; on failure the sheet stays open and retryable (the mutation-cache toast carries the message, §7a). `MesocycleBuilderPage` itself now redirects an **archived** run away on load — `<Navigate to=".../report" replace>` (`MesocycleBuilderPage.tsx`) — since a closed run's plan is history, not something to edit.
- **Report page** (`pages/MesoReportPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/:id/report`) — the FROZEN end-of-mesocycle report, reached from a `Történet` card tap, `MesoCloseSheet`'s post-close redirect, and the builder's archived-run redirect above. Full-screen sibling route, **on the `MozaikPage`/`PageHead`/`PageHero` scaffold since `mezo-d20.3.6`** (its volume section also took the prototype's name, `Heti szettek · a blokk íve`); the composition below is otherwise unchanged: back breadcrumb → header (title, `Lezárva · {date}` eyebrow, start–end date range + weeks, a `Sablon megnyitása` chip when `templateId` is set) → an **adherence** `StatStrip` (`Edzés {done}/{planned}`, `Hét {done}/{planned}`, `Teljesítés {pct}%`) → (**until `mezo-e1ii9` Task 5 removed it, see the tail bullet below**) the **frozen volume arc** rendered by the shared **`MuscleArcSwitch`** (`components/MuscleArcSwitch.tsx`, lifted verbatim out of `MesoOverviewPage` — presentational + local pill-selection state over a `MuscleVolumeArc[]` prop, so a live arc and a close-time snapshot render through the exact same component and can never visually drift) → **strength** cards (per exercise: the `{firstTopKg} → {lastTopKg} kg · {reps} rep` movement line, a `{±kg}` LOAD-delta pill and a separate `{±%} e1RM` pill — each hidden individually at exactly 0, and a weightless exercise shows reps only) → **records** (medal count + top highlights) → an **`Életmód-kontextus` block** (`mezo-meyc.3`, rendered only when `report.context != null` — a planned/archived-but-uncomputed report shows nothing, not an empty shell) — a wrapping row of compact `Chip` pills over the totals (`😴`/`🍽`/`⚡`/`😰`/`⚖️`/`🏐`/`🏃`, each individually omitted when its metric is null — never shown as a fabricated 0; the kcal pill's "vs cél" is the mean of the weeks' own `kcalTargetAvg`, since `MesoContextTotals` carries none of its own) over a horizontally-scrolling (`overflow-x` on its own inner wrapper, DS wide-content rule) per-week table (`W`/`Alvás`/`Kcal`/`Energia`/`Stressz`/`Súly Δ`/`Sport`/`Futás`, null cells render `–`; the `Sport` cell's RPE sub-line is explicitly labelled **"Sport/futás RPE"**, not "gym" — `gymRpeAvg` is actually the sport+running RPE average despite its name) → an **`Ezt akartad`** block (mesocycle pages v2 Task 5) — rendered only when `meso.notes` is set (the wizard's freeform goal text, read back once the run is closed): an `Ezt akartad` eyebrow over the quote `„{meso.notes}"`, with an optional `— és ez lett: {meso.summary}` trailer when the report also carries a summary → an **`Izmonként · indulás → elért csúcs / plafon`** card — one row per muscle from `peakBands(arcs)`, each `{start} → {peak} / {ceiling}` → the owner's **read-only self-eval** (rendered only when non-empty) → an **AI section that does not render at all while `aiEvalEnabled` is false** (the `mezo.feature.meso-review.enabled` switch; since S3 it is on by default, so the section renders and the `pending` poll below is live). Three AI states inside the card: `pending` ⇒ a `Spinner` + "Az értékelés készül…"; `ready` (with a non-null `aiEval`) ⇒ the narrative split into `<p>`s on blank-line separators (no markdown lib) + a `Generálva · {date}` caption + an **`Újragenerálás`** `CtaGhost`; `failed` **or** `ready` with a null `aiEval` (a defensive fallback — the backend should never produce this) ⇒ "Nem sikerült az AI-kiértékelés." + an **`Újrapróbálás`** `CtaGhost` — both buttons fire the SAME `regenerate()` the page's bottom `Riport újragenerálása` chip does. Below that: `Újrafuttatás` (the shared rerun-into-`MesoStartSheet` flow, `mezo-meyc.1`), a second ghost CTA **`Sablon mentése ebből a futamból`** (`mezo-tlwa` — `createTemplate(runToTemplate(meso))` then straight into the new template's editor; rendered only once the meso list has resolved the RUN, since the fork copies its day plan and the frozen report DTO carries none) and the `Riport újragenerálása` chip. Three page-level states beyond the happy path: `pending` (loading ghost), a genuine `error` (fetch failure → `Újrapróbálás`), and `notFound` (the run exists but carries no report yet) — which itself branches on the run: still running ⇒ "a riport a lezárás pillanatában készül el", archived with none ⇒ **„Riport generálása"**, the legacy-backfill CTA that calls the same regenerate endpoint. Data via `useMesoReport(id)` (`data/train/mesoReportHooks.ts`, §4), which polls every 3s once a report has landed only while `aiEvalStatus === 'pending' && aiEvalEnabled` — which since S3 is exactly the window in which the companion's async generator is running — plus a short post-regenerate window while the row doesn't exist yet.
  - **The star hero (Train Titanium T10 Task 4, `mezo-88iwa.11`).** A run that HAS a report now opens on a `.pl-lhero.is-closed` hero instead of the plain `PageHero` (the loading / `notFound` / error branches keep it): five clay stars (`i-termes`, lit / half / dim), one adherence-neutral sentence, and the completion share DRAWN rather than stated — the `.ld-hero-pct` numeral + `.ld-hero-bar` idiom under the label „A teljesített edzések aránya".
