The clay set has no half-star or empty-star glyph, so a half or empty star is ONE glyph dimmed and desaturated, exactly the way the ceremony already draws its own (`.cer-stars`). **The stars are FE-derived, and their only input is the FROZEN `adherence.completionPct`** — `runStars(completionPct)` in `logic/libraryStory.ts` clamps the percentage to a 0..1 share and halves it through the SHARED `starsFor` (`logic/cerScore.ts`, the same function the workout ceremony and `sportScore` use), so a run's rating can never disagree with the ceremony's; `null` passes straight through as `null`. There is no `stars` field on the wire — the prototype's fixture `stars`/`say` strings are demo prose — so the `say` ladder is new copy, written adherence-neutral („Végigvitted." ≥95% · „Erős futam volt." ≥75% · „A nagyobb fele megvan." ≥50% · „Elindult, aztán másfelé vitt az élet." >0 · „Ez a futam nem indult el." at 0). The adherence `StatStrip`'s `Teljesítés` cell was **dropped**: the hero draws that number, and two copies of it read as two measurements.
  - **„A mostani tervedhez képest" — the versus block (T10 Task 4).** One `.pl-versus` pair per shared muscle between this closed run's **peak planned week** and the ACTIVE run's **current-week planned sets**, read from `useMesocycleVolumeArc(active.id)` — the same arc source `MesoMusclePage` builds its own versus rows from — through the exported pure `versusPairs()`. **Presence rules:** it renders only when there IS an active run **and** the two share at least one muscle; otherwise the block is absent entirely, never an empty shell. The peak-then vs current-week-now asymmetry is deliberate (a finished run has no "now", a running one has no peak yet) and spec-compliant, but it is an owner-visible product call rather than a neutral fact. Muscle labels come from `BUDGET_GROUP_LABELS` (so „Hamstring"), matching the report's own band block rather than the arc switch above it — a pre-existing label-set inconsistency, untouched.
  - **The tail is gone — the report keeps the story, drops the spreadsheet (`mezo-e1ii9`, Train parity P1 Task 5).** Under the Titanium closed-run story (star hero → drawn delivery share → muscle journeys → versus) T10 had left a pre-Titanium tail stacked. The claim made here at the time — that each item was merely a SECOND surface for data that already had a first one — was **not true of everything in them**, and the fix wave (`mezo-e1ii9`) corrected it: see the two recorded deaths in the bullet below. Removed: **`HETI SZETTEK · A BLOKK ÍVE`** (`MuscleArcSwitch` + `VolumeArcChart`) and its **MEV/MAV/MRV/Deload** legend — the muscle-journey card directly above says the same arc in plain words (indulás → csúcs / plafon); and the **`ÉLETMÓD-KONTEXTUS`** emoji totals row (`😴`/`🍽`/`⚡`/`😰`/`⚖️`/`🏐`/`🏃`) with its horizontally-scrolling **W1–W8** per-week table. `components/{MuscleArcSwitch,VolumeArcChart}.tsx` lost their last consumer and are deleted. The page now ENDS on the versus block, exactly as the prototype does. Everything in the paragraph above about those two blocks is history, not current behaviour.
  - **The AI evaluation is NOT a leftover — it is demoted, not deleted (same task).** It is a real backend feature the prototype simply has no counterpart for, so it keeps its data and all three live states (`pending` / `ready` / `failed`), moved into a **closed `<details>` at the foot of the story**: „Mit olvas ki ebből a gép?", captioned honestly as a guess written from the numbers above rather than a measurement.
  - **The run-window lifestyle context lives in that same collapsed foot (fix round 1).** Task 5 had claimed the lifestyle averages stayed reachable through `MesoComparePage`'s `contextDiff` — true only with **two or more** closed runs, since `MesoFutamokPage` gates the compare entry behind `archived.length >= 2`. A first closed run (or any run reviewed alone) had no UI path to its own sleep/kcal/energy/stress/weight-delta/sport averages at all. The `<details>` therefore folds the run-window context in beside the AI narrative as **plain prose-and-number rows** — one per `CONTEXT_METRICS` field (now **exported** from `logic/mesoCompare.ts` and reused, not re-declared): `Alvás`, `Kcal`, `Energia`, `Stressz`, `Súlyváltozás`, `Sport`. No emoji, no table. A metric the run never measured renders `–`, never `0`, and an averaged-vs-summed figure says which it is in its own wording. The block renders whenever EITHER half has something to show, and the `<details>` summary adapts when the AI evaluation itself is switched off.
  - **The fix wave finished the accounting — three more totals got a home, two capabilities are recorded deaths (`mezo-e1ii9`).** The six shared `CONTEXT_METRICS` were not everything the removed `ÉLETMÓD-KONTEXTUS` block carried. **Rehomed** into the same collapsed rows: `totals.sportSessions` and `totals.runSessions` — plain run-window counts exactly like the six, rendered as two further rows (`REPORT_ONLY_ROWS`, report-LOCAL: `CONTEXT_METRICS` is `MesoComparePage`'s contract and widening it would silently add two columns there) — and `kcalTargetAvg`, which now gives the `Kcal` row something to sit against: `kcalTargetMean(weeks)` averages the weeks' own targets (`MesoContextTotals` carries none) and `kcalTargetNote` renders one sentence („A cél {n} kcal volt — {±n} kcal a célhoz képest.") **only when both the measured average AND a real target exist** — a fabricated target would read as a verdict about the owner's eating. **Recorded deaths, deliberate:** `context.weeks[]` (the whole per-week granularity — sleep / kcal / energy / stress / weight-delta / sport / run, W1–W8) and `MesoContextWeek.gymRpeAvg` (despite the name, the sport+running RPE average) have **no renderer anywhere in the app** any more. The prototype's closed-run story has no week-by-week view, so no screen was invented for them. **The backend still computes and ships both on every report**, so a later slice can surface them with no server work — that is exactly why they are written down here and as a row in the parity matrix (§21) rather than left to be rediscovered by grep. Defensive detail: the report's `contextRows` **skips** a metric `CONTEXT_ROW_COPY` does not know instead of throwing — a seventh field added to `CONTEXT_METRICS` for the compare page must never blank a frozen report.
- **Compare view** (`pages/MesoComparePage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/compare?a=&b=`, `mezo-meyc.4`, spec §4) — two closed runs side by side, reached from the library's „Összevetés" selection mode above. Full-screen sibling route, **on the `MozaikPage` scaffold since `mezo-d20.3.6`** (the `MesoReportPage` idiom, which is now the Mozaik one). **There is no compare endpoint** — the page runs **two `useMesoReport` reads** and composes client-side through PURE helpers in `logic/mesoCompare.ts` (`alignVolumeWeeks` / `sharedStrengthDeltas` / `contextDiff` / `peakVolumeRows` / `focusDiff`, §4). Blocks: a **two-column header** (each column `A`/`B` + title + `{start} → {end}` + `{weeks} hét`) → **`Fókusz-különbség`** (mesocycle pages v2 Task 5) — `focusDiff(run)` per side: a `régi modell` note (`isLegacyPlan`, below) when the run's plan predates the current tier model, plus its non-`grow` `musclePriorities` chips (emphasize before maintain) so a shared-exercise delta can be read against each side's actual focus → **`Adherencia`** (each side's `completionPct` big, over `{done}/{planned} edzés` and `{done}/{planned} hét`) → **`Csúcs-volumen · szet/hét`** (mesocycle pages v2 Task 5) — `peakVolumeRows(a, b)`: one row per muscle in either run's arc, `aPeak`/`bPeak` (the max planned value across that run's own weeks) against `aCeiling` (A's MRV), sorted by A's ceiling then A's peak → **`Volumen · szet/hét`** — the muscle-pill switch (a local re-style of the then-shared `MuscleArcSwitch` pill row — `MesoComparePage` owns the only surviving copy since Task 5 deleted that component; the chart itself can NOT be reused, it draws one run's arc) over a per-week `Hét / A terv / A tény / B terv / B tény` table in its own `overflow-x` container → **`Közös gyakorlatok`** — one card per exercise trained in BOTH runs (name + muscle, then each side's `{±kg}` LOAD line and `{±%}` e1RM figure), the **better side's percentage** in `--sage-deep` and bold, the weaker side plain secondary — deliberately **no red**: this compares two finished blocks, it does not condemn one → **`Kontextus-átlagok`** (the `contextDiff` rows, `–` where a run has no such measurement) → chips to open either run's own report. **States:** missing/blank/identical `?a=&b=` ⇒ a Hungarian „Válassz két lezárt futamot…" ghost + a `Történet megnyitása` CTA (a self-comparison is treated as an unusable link, not a state); per column, `notFound` ⇒ **„Előbb generálj riportot"** + a `Riport megnyitása` chip pointing at that run's report page (where the generate CTA lives), `error` ⇒ a per-column `Újrapróbálás`, otherwise a `Riport betöltése…` line — the other column keeps rendering either way, and the comparison body simply waits until BOTH reports exist.
- **Start sheet** (`sheets/MesoStartSheet.tsx`) — the single "stamp a run from a template" surface: start date (today by default, `localDateString()`) + an `Aktív`/`Tervezett` `.segtab` pick → `startTemplate(id, {startDate, status})`. On success `active` navigates to `/train/gym`, `planned` just closes (the run shows up under `Tervezett`); on failure the sheet stays open and retryable (the mutation-cache toast carries the message, §7a of the FE conventions).
- **Template editor** (`pages/MesoTemplateEditorPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/templates/:id`) — full-screen sibling route, now on the **shared `MesoWeekEditor`** (`components/MesoWeekEditor.tsx`, `mezo-yty6`) in `template` mode — the very same editor the planner's third step renders, replacing the old `.pghead-np` + `MesoEditor` accordion face. `TemplateDayEditor` resolves the template, seeds local day state (authoritative, updates synchronously) and mounts the editor once the list query has resolved; every add/remove/change/reorder — plus a name edit and a day rename — calls a shared `toUpsert(template, days, goalPreset, musclePriorities, title)` and fires a background **full-template `updateTemplate` PUT** (a template has no per-day endpoint; `toUpsert` gained the `title` fifth parameter for the in-place rename). The response is deliberately **not** re-seeded into state — the server regenerates every exercise id on a full write, so re-seeding would swap ids out from under an in-progress edit. **Renames are debounced** (`RENAME_DEBOUNCE_MS = 400`, refs hold the latest days/priorities/title so the debounced write or an unmount flush always sends the current draft, never a stale snapshot) — a per-keystroke `onRename`/`onRenameDay` would otherwise PUT the whole document once per character. **The tier (`Fókusz`) picker is gone from this page** — `musclePriorities` is still read off the template and still rides every PUT (dropping it would reset the map to all-grow), but there is no UI here to change it any more; tier editing now lives only in the wizard interview (follow-up filed, see §9).
- **Planner** (`MesocyclePlannerPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/new`) — **rewritten wizard v3 (`mezo-yty6`).** The 3-step wizard (`Mikor és miért` / `Fókusz` / `Program`, its own progress strip, `StepWhen.tsx`/`StepFocus.tsx`/`StepProgram.tsx`, and the page-state `ProgramDayView.tsx`) is **retired outright** — all four files are deleted — for a **single scrollable interview screen** (`wizard/InterviewStep.tsx`) that folds the old Step 1 + Step 2 into one page, followed by generation straight into the **same `MesoWeekEditor`** the template editor uses, in `draft` mode. One task, one editor UI: the old three-step "Program" step and its bespoke day-mosaic/`ProgramDayView` pair are gone, along with the `.mz-wprog` progress strip and `STEP_LABELS`.
  - **The interview asks exactly the old Step 1 + Step 2 questions, on one page:** a day-count/weekday picker (2–6 training days via `COUNTS`/`DAY_CHIPS`, `canGenerate(state)` gates the CTA), an optional free-text goal (`goalText`, `maxLength=400`), a `Hossz` segmented control (4–8 weeks, moved with the `Program` step onto the interview — it no longer waits for a generated proposal to be visible), the unchanged `MusclePriorityPicker` (≤2 Emphasize groups) with a live week-1/peak `StatStrip` off `logic/mesoPlan.ts`'s `weekTotals(priorities)`, and a static "Ami magától megy" `StatStrip` (ramp+deload weeks, `+2` sets/week/muscle, `~8` set/session cap). `✨ Program generálása` is disabled until 2–6 days are picked.
  - **Generation is a one-shot page transition, not a step.** `onGenerate` calls `useMesoPlanGenerate().generate(generateInput(state))` (`POST /api/train/meso-plans/generate`, §4 `#### Plan generator`) and, on success, dispatches `{ type: 'step', step: 'editor' }` — `wizardState`'s `step` is now `'interview' | 'editor'` (was a numeric step index). A failed **first** generation leaves `step` on `'interview'` and shows a full-body `↺ Újrapróbálom` retry strip above the interview; there is no in-between "empty program" state to protect, because the editor never mounts before a proposal exists.
  - **Once in `'editor'`, the wizard renders `MesoWeekEditor` in `draft` mode** with the generator's `rationale` as its `note` line — the old hero's `coachsub`/`llmUsed` provenance line (`Gemini · a determinisztikus kereteken belül` vs `alap gyakorlat-kiosztás`) and the `DayTile` mosaic/`WeeklyBandsCard`/`peakWeekFit` strip are gone from this screen; the equivalent load information now lives on the editor's own `LoadTile`/`WeekLoadPanel` (§2 below), and the peak-week check moved into `WeekLoadPanel` itself. Opening a day is the editor's own page-state (`state.activeDay`, `MesoDayEditor`) — there is no separate `ProgramDayView` any more; the reducer gained a **`renameDay`** action for the day-rename input, and the old signature-diffing **`inputChanged(state)`** guard (and its `proposalInput`/hero coach note) was **deleted as unreachable**: the one-screen interview asks every generator input before the CTA is even enabled, so there is no post-generation path back to change days/weeks/priorities without also re-confirming the dirty program (below).
  - **Regenerating after a manual edit is still guarded the same way:** any `editProgram` dispatch sets `state.dirty`; the editor's `↺ Újragenerálás` footer button calls `regenerate()`, which opens an inline `mz-confirm` strip ("Kézzel szerkesztett napjaid vannak — az újragenerálás felülírja őket.") when dirty, or calls the generator directly when not. A **failed regeneration** keeps the standing (possibly hand-edited) program and raises its own `mz-confirm` strip ("Nem sikerült az újragenerálás — a korábbi program megmaradt." + `Újra`/`Mégse`) — its `Újra` button calls `runGenerate(state)` directly, **bypassing the dirty check** a second time (follow-up filed, see §9).
  - **Save dropped the start-date suffix:** the two footer buttons are `✓ Mentés + indítás` (creates the template, then chains `startTemplate(id, { startDate: todayIso, status: 'active' })` and lands on `/train/gym`) and `Mentés sablonként` (stops at the template, lands on the library) — the old `· {startDate}` label on the primary CTA is gone (follow-up filed, see §9). Failure paths stay honest: a failed create keeps the wizard mounted; a created-but-failed-start lands on the library, never Gym. `toUpsert` (`wizardState.ts`) is otherwise unchanged in shape — `phaseCurve: phaseCurve(s.weeks)` derived from the CURRENT `weeks` at save time, `musclePriorities`/`days` the live `state.priorities`/`state.program`.
  - **The wizard still never touches the standing gym-time schedule** — no `saveGymSchedule` call anywhere in the flow, no invented weekday time; `deriveGymSchedule` tolerates a day with no matching slot rather than fabricating one.
**The unified editor's anatomy (`mezo-yty6`, `components/MesoWeekEditor.tsx` + siblings) — the surface both the planner (draft mode) and the template editor (template mode) render.** Hero (editable mezo-name input, buffered like every renamed text field in this surface, + a one-line meta: weeks · split · run count) → a horizontally-scrolling row of **`DayStripTile`**s (one per day, name/type/sets/`~minutes`/a per-muscle mini-bar strip toned via `dayTone`, flagged with a warning corner when the day sits in an `adjacentDayConflicts` pair) → a compact **`LoadTile`** ("Heti terhelés · izmonként": total W1 sets + up to 3 per-muscle gauges against MRV, flagged when any adjacency conflict exists) → the `adjacentDayConflicts` lint lines rendered inline, passive advice that never blocks. Tapping a day tile opens **`MesoDayEditor`** as page-state (`activeDay`, not a route — the still-unsaved draft survives the round trip): a renameable day-name hero, a day-scoped `LoadTile` (against the session-cap, not MRV), and a list of **`ExerciseCard`**s — always expanded, no accordion — each showing every field (working sets, rep min/max, the nullable `Kiinduló kg` anchor, target RIR, a `🔥 Failure`/`🌿 Volume` toggle, warmup sets) as directly-writable inputs plus ▲▼ reorder and a ✕ delete, all buffered the same way as the hero name inputs. `MesoDayEditor`'s entrance choreography (`rise` stagger) fires **only on first mount** — a `data-entered` flag + a non-state `firstRender` ref keep every later edit (reorder, delete, add, a number field commit) from re-triggering the flash-on-edit the prototype review flagged.
- **Load pages (`mezo-yty6`).** Tapping the week `LoadTile` opens **`WeekLoadPanel`** — one poster card per muscle group (tier chip, frequency, `{sets} → {target}` with a direction arrow off the LIVE `WeekLoadRow.direction`, a **`ZoneBar`** drawing the MEV–MAV/MAV–MRV zones with labelled landmark lines, a target-tier line and a muscle-colored marker at the current value — no percentage is ever rendered as text) that expands on tap into a per-day contribution breakdown; below the cards, the `adjacentDayConflicts` lint rows AND a **`peakWeekFit`** check (flags a training day whose estimated session length would fall outside the 45–90 min band once every group actually reaches its tier's target) — both render a green "no issue" line when clean rather than disappearing silently. Tapping a day `LoadTile` opens **`DayLoadPanel`** — the session-cap analogue, one poster card per muscle group against the ~8 set/session cap with an amber near-cap/over-cap flag and its contributing exercises. Both panels are page-state (`weekLoadOpen`/`loadOpen`), not routes.
- **Derivations live in one module — `logic/mesoLoad.ts`** (`weekMuscleLoad`, `dayMuscleLoad`, `adjacentDayConflicts`, plus **`dayTone`/`DayTone`**, which MOVED here from `wizard/dayTiles.ts`; `dayTiles.ts` now just re-exports both for its existing callers). `weekMuscleLoad` reads each group's live weekly sets against its tier's landmark target (maintain→MEV, grow→MAV, emphasize→MRV) and returns, per group, a **live `direction`** (`'up'`/`'down'`/`'hold'`, computed straight off `sets` vs `target` — not carried over from a stale generator value) plus `toTarget`/`frequency`/a per-day `contributions` breakdown. `dayMuscleLoad` is the same shape against the per-session cap. `adjacentDayConflicts` flags a muscle group trained on two CALENDAR-adjacent training days (Hét→Kedd, not Hét→Sze) — passive advice, never a block, with no prior-art precedent in the surveyed apps (spec §4).
- **Run page** (`MesocycleBuilderPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/:id`, mesocycle pages v2 Task 3, `mezo-d20.15`) — **the old three-view switcher (`Áttekintés`/`Volumen`/`Gyakorlatok`) is gone**: `Volumen` moved to its own route (`Heti vizsgálat`, below), `Gyakorlatok` is now per-day (`MesoDayPage`, below), and there is **no in-cycle `Fókusz` picker any more** — `MusclePriorityPicker` is planning-time only (the wizard's interview screen), because changing tiers mid-run used to silently re-plan the remaining weeks. The page itself is now status-first: a `PageHero` (name + sub) → an **"A blokk íve"** card (phase chip + week dots off `logic/mesoBands.ts` + a one-line `arcLine()` textual arc, `W1 · W2 · W3 · most · …`) → a **decider sentence** card (`deciderSentence(meso)`, only rendered when non-null — it maps the backend `VolumeChange.reason` vocabulary, e.g. `'kezdő hét (MEV)'`/`'cél teljesítve, nincs grind'`/`'tartás'`/`'deload'`, to a Hungarian sentence) → **two tiles** (`Mosaic`): **`Heti vizsgálat`** (a mini bar cluster of the top-5 `runBands`, tapping opens `/week`) and **`Hétfőn jön`** (`nextRolloverChips(meso)` — deliberately **not** clickable, it is a forecast of next week's rollover, not a destination) → a **day mosaic** ("A heted", one `DayTile` per training day; tapping a tile opens `/days/:day`, below) → the lifecycle footer: planned → `Aktiválás`, active → **`Meso lezárása` opens `MesoCloseSheet`** (`mezo-meyc.2`, see the `Run report + close flow` paragraph above), never closes directly. "Heti terv másolása" is inert (deferred). **An archived run redirects away to its report** (`<Navigate>`, `mezo-meyc.2`) before any of this renders. A day tile's `status` is only ever `'now'` (today) or `null` — never `'done'`: `useTrain()` exposes this week's completed instance for TODAY alone, never a per-day list, so a `✓ kész` on another day's tile would be invented.
- **Day page** (`pages/MesoDayPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/:id/days/:day`, Task 3, re-faced Train Titanium T9 Task 4) — the per-day editor a run-page day tile opens (URL-encoded day token, e.g. `H%C3%A9t`). Since T9 the top of the page is the `.pl-dhero`/`.pl-mrows`/`.pl-exs` Titanium view described above (body-map poster, per-muscle bars, read-only exercise cells) — this replaced the older letter-tile `PageHero` + per-muscle `StatStrip` pair. Below that, unchanged: `<MesoExercises meso={meso} day={day.day} />`. **`MesoExercises`** (`components/MesoExercises.tsx`) lost its in-cycle `Fókusz` `<details>`/`MusclePriorityPicker` and the `PUT .../muscle-priorities` call it used to fire — `meso.musclePriorities` is read-only there now, feeding only the bands/lint math — and gained a **`day?: string`** prop that restricts the editor to that one day's exercises (`days.filter(d => d.day === day)`) while still passing the WHOLE week to `MesoEditor` as `weekDays`, so week-scope band/lint derivations keep judging the real week instead of one day. Persistence is unchanged: local day state is authoritative, each add/remove/change/reorder fires a background `saveDayExercises` PUT. While the block list is still in flight (real mode) the page holds a `role="status"` skeleton — the sibling week/muscle pages' pattern — and only a RESOLVED block without the day (or an unknown id) falls through to the "nincs ilyen nap/mesociklus" guard; rendering the guard during the fetch made every valid deep link flash as a dead one (`mezo-d20.15` fix wave). **Since the unified-editor merge (`mezo-7rdg`, spec `2026-08-01-set-budget-unified-editor-design.md`, superseding `mezo-n46i`'s `MesoDayTabsEditor`)** the day page **renders the shared `MesoEditor`** (`components/MesoEditor.tsx`; the wizard's now-deleted `ProgramDayView` used to render it too — the wizard renders `MesoDayEditor`/`ExerciseCard` since `mezo-yty6`, see above): day tabs (exercise-count badge, `current` day preselected, a **red session-cap warning dot** on any day with a `sessionCapWarnings` breach), a gradient **`MesoEditorHero`** (day focus stats — sets/exercise count, weekly total + training-day count — amber-shifted only via the session-cap `warningCount`, since the weekly-band % overage alarm retired with `SetBudgetCard`, `mezo-d20.14`), a **`WeeklyBandsCard`** (`current → ceiling` per tier, no percentages, `▲ +2 / hét` step chip — replaced the old percent-pill `SetBudgetCard`, §4 `#### Weekly bands` below), a **`PeakFitCard`** ("Csúcshét · időbecslés", `mezo-3m5m` — flags training days whose session length would fall outside the 45–90 min band once every group actually reaches its tier's target; distinct from the structural-lint session-length rule below, which reads the template's OWN minutes today, §4 `#### Weekly bands`), and a **single-expand `ExerciseAccordionRow` list** (`components/ExerciseAccordionRow.tsx`; one row open at a time): collapsed rows show name + muscle pill + a `🔥`/`🌿` style chip; the expanded row adds a **`🔥 Failure` / `🌿 Volume`** segmented toggle (writes `targetRIR` 0/2, D1) above a 2×2 stepper grid (working sets, rep range, the nullable **`Kiinduló kg`** anchor — `auto` when unset, `mezo-anm4` — and warmup sets) plus a **`Finomhangolás`** disclosure for the raw RIR/rep-min/rep-max knobs. `ExerciseAccordionRow` **replaces the always-open `ExerciseRecipeRow` in this surface** — `ExerciseRecipeRow.tsx` itself is **retained**, but only for `CustomWorkoutBuilderPage` (see the spec's §7 reality note). Off-days are still detected by **muscle** (`'' = rest`, `'sport'`), now via the shared `isOffDay` helper (`logic/offDay.ts`) rather than a per-component copy. Drag-reorder (`SortableList`) is preserved on collapsed rows. The exercise picker (`ExercisePickerSheet`) is still **multi-add**: stays open across picks (per-row `Hozzáadva ✓` flash + live counter), takes a `dayLabel` context prop, and closes via the header **`Kész`** chip / ✕ / backdrop / Escape; a newly added exercise is filled by `logic/exerciseDefaults.ts`'s `addExerciseWithDefaults`/`libraryToGymExercise` — a **single hypertrophy scheme** (`mezo-d20.14`; the multi-goal `goalPreset`-keyed `SCHEMES` table and its `planner.ts` picker are retired) crossed with the picked exercise's `type`: a `compound`/`isolation` pick gets the hypertrophy reps/RIR/working-sets plus a type-seeded `warmupSets` (compound 2, isolation 1), while a `plyo` pick always gets the fixed weightless `PLYO_SCHEME` (3×5, 0 warmup sets) and is exempt from the weekly bands the same way a generator-seeded plyo row is (`mezo-gbo7`). The seeded `warmupSets` is then immediately refined by `suggestedWarmupSets` once the exercise is in the day (position/anchor-weight aware — see the recommendation-engine subsection in §4), so it usually differs from the type-seeded value by the time the row renders; a **`cut-prep`/`recovery`** isolation pick lands on `repMax 15`, which trips `suggestedWarmupSets`'s bodyweight-ish rule (`anchorWeightKg == null && repMax ≥ 15`) straight to **0 warmup sets** — contract-consistent, but worth knowing if it looks surprising in the UI. Every pick lands **expanded**; added-exercise ids use `crypto.randomUUID()`.

### `Lezárt futamaid` — the closed-run list (`pages/MesoFutamokPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/futamok`, Train Titanium T10, `mezo-88iwa.11`)

Its own route since T10 Task 2, which moved the landing's whole `Történet` section here **verbatim** first — markup, handlers and tests unchanged, so nothing was unreachable at any commit (the T4 reachability lesson) — and Task 4 refaced it. `.pl-dhero.pl-lhero.is-slim` hero (docked back pill → the landing, a spinning `i-erme` clay spot, „Lezárt futamaid" / „Amit végigvittél", one plain sentence) over one `.pl-lib-card.is-closed` per archived run: name + the `{start} → {end}` window, `N hét`, the `mezo-meyc.4` report stamp (a `riport` chip when `meso.hasReport`, a muted `nincs riport` label otherwise) and the run's own summary line. The card is a plain box whose BODY is the button (`.pl-lib-open` → that run's frozen report) with `Újrafuttatás` and `Sablonná` in a `.pl-lib-foot` row — no nested buttons. The `mezo-meyc.4` „Összevetés" pairing mode (≥2 closed runs, max two picks in tap order, → `/train/mesocycles/compare?a=&b=`, `.is-picked` selection state, both footer actions stepping aside) and the `MesoStartSheet` rerun wiring came across unchanged. Loading renders `MesoFutamokSkeleton` (list geometry — it used to inherit the landing's).

**What this list honestly can NOT show — `mezo-z1mqg`.** The prototype draws a star row on every closed row and sums „N edzés" / „N rekord" into the hero. `Mesocycle` (`data/types.ts`) carries **no** `completionPct`, **no** `completedSessions`/`plannedSessions` and **no** record count; all three exist only on the frozen `MesocycleReportResponse` (`adherence`/`records`), which is one fetch per run. So the list draws **no stars** — the star row lives on the report page, which already holds the share — and states no session or record totals, not even em-dashed, because an em-dashed fact states nothing. The hero's facts row says only `N lezárt futam` and `N hét összesen` (Σ `weeks`). **`mezo-z1mqg`** tracks the honest fix, which is backend-side: put `completionPct`, `completedSessions`/`plannedSessions` and `recordCount` on the closed `Mesocycle` row, or add a closed-run summary endpoint.

### `Sablonjaid` — the template list (`pages/MesoTemplatesPage.tsx`, `/train/templates`, `mezo-tlwa`, refaced Train Titanium T10 `mezo-88iwa.11`)

**T10 (`mezo-88iwa.11`) refaced it into `Sablonjaid`.** Reached from the library landing's „Sablonjaid" doorway. A `.pl-dhero.pl-lhero.is-slim` hero with the back pill docked inside it (`.pl-lhero > .mz-backbtn`) → the landing; eyebrow „Sablonjaid", h2 „Amiből indíthatsz", one plain sentence, and a `.pl-poster-foot` carrying REAL facts („2 sablon · 1 futam indult belőlük" — the second summed off `runCount`). Then one `.pl-lib-card` per template, in served (created-date) order: name + split head, a `.pl-day-facts` box row (hét · nap hetente · ~perc, the minutes box dropped when the week carries no session yet), a `.pl-lib-mus` row of `MuscleChip` minis for every muscle group the template works, and a `.pl-lib-note` line from `templateUseLine` — „Ebből fut a mostani terved" / „N lezárt futam jött ki belőle" / „Még nem indítottál belőle". Tapping a card opens the template's own STORY page (`/train/templates/:id`, next), not the raw editor. The create affordance the DS page carried **twice** (a head chip and a dashed footer CTA) survives as the single `.pl-lib-new` → the planner. Real-mode loading renders the geometry-matched `MesoTemplatesSkeleton` (`role="status"`); an empty list adds a `.pl-foot-say` line — „Még nincs sablonod — az elsőt alább állíthatod össze." — not a `GhostState`. Data: `useMesoTemplates()` — no new endpoint, the whole tab is the existing CRUD. **Superseded here:** the DS page-header shell, the `StatStrip` shelf strip, the `MesoTemplateCard` poster (with `logic/templatePoster.ts` and the whole `.tpl-*` CSS family — all deleted in the T10 sweep) and the dashed `+ Új sablon tervezése` footer.

**"régi modell" legacy note (mesocycle pages v2 Task 5, `isLegacyPlan`, `logic/mesoPlan.ts`) — the CHIP retired with the card.** `isLegacyPlan(template)` is `(goalPreset != null && goalPreset !== 'hypertrophy') || !phaseCurve.includes('Deload')`: a template written under the retired multi-goal `SCHEMES` model, or one whose `phaseCurve` has no closing Deload week. A template with **no** `goalPreset` at all is not legacy — its absence is the current model's own steady state, not a signal. The dashed `régi modell` chip that `MesoTemplateCard` rendered is gone with that card (T10 sweep); the predicate itself is still live, read by `logic/mesoCompare.ts`'s `focusDiff` for the compare view's „régi modell" note.

**Why its own page:** a blueprint list answers "what can I run" while the library answers "how are my blocks going", and once a template gained four actions the two jobs stopped fitting in one scroll.

#### The template story page (`pages/MesoTemplateStoryPage.tsx`, `/train/templates/:id`, Train Titanium T10, `mezo-88iwa.11`)

The prototype's template preview page, built for real — a blueprint you can READ before you commit to it, which is what the DS card's four equal-weight buttons never offered. Route order matters: `train/templates/:id` sits right after `train/templates` in `src/app/router.tsx` and does not collide with the raw editor at `train/mesocycles/templates/:id`.

- **Hero** — `.pl-dhero.pl-lhero` in the gold tone: docked „‹ Sablonjaid" back pill, a `BodyMap` (`views="auto"`, heat flat at `'in'` over the template's own muscles — highlighted means *this template trains it*, not a graded weekly load), a `Sablon · {split}` eyebrow, the template name, the sentence „{weeks} hét, hetente {n} edzésnap.", the `MuscleChip` row, and a `.pl-poster-foot` with `~{n} perc egy edzés` + `{n} izomcsoport`.
- **„A hét felépítése"** — the week SPELLED OUT: one `.pl-lib-card.is-open` per TRAINING day with **every** exercise as a `.pl-tpl-ex` row (muscle chip, name, `szett×ismétlés` — or „N× tartás" when repMin and repMax are both 0 — and the starting kg, or „saját testsúly" at 0). Rest and sport days (`isOffDay`) render as slim `.pl-row.is-quiet` „Pihenő" rows; a training day with no exercises yet gets its own honest quiet row (day type + „még nincs gyakorlat") rather than being misread as a rest day.
- **„Heti szettek izmonként"** — `.pl-wload` bars over `templateWeekSets`, scaled to the week's biggest muscle, with the prototype's own one-line explainer as plain sub-text (not a tooltip): „Ennyi munkaszettet kap az izom egy héten, ha ebből a sablonból indítasz. A futam első hete indul ennyivel — onnan hétről hétre emelkedhet." The per-muscle sums run through the shared `daySessionBreakdown`, never a second inline volume rule.
- **„Futamok ebből a sablonból"** — `.pl-row` list over `templateRuns`: the running one → `/train/mesocycles`, a queued one → its own page `/train/mesocycles/:id` (never a one-tap start — the Task 2 lesson), a closed one → its frozen report. An em-dash line when none.
- **The actions** — „Futam indítása ebből" opens the ONE shared `MesoStartSheet` (the same `POST …/start` the DS card's `Indítás` fired), „Szerkesztés" → the raw editor, and — a judgment call, because the retired card's ⋯ menu had no other home and the raw editor carries neither — the lifecycle pair as quiet `.pl-row` actions: **Duplikálás** (re-sends the document as a fresh `createTemplate` titled „{title} (másolat)" → the copy's editor) and **Törlés** as a **two-tap confirm on the row itself** („Sablon törlése" → „Biztos? Törlés"), armed state carrying `.pl-row.is-danger` (the one deliberate exception to "down is never red") with a `Mégsem` escape beside it, auto-disarming on any other tap via a capture-phase document listener. Both disable while `createPending`/`deletePending` is in flight.
- Unknown id → the house dead-link idiom (`PageHead` + `GhostState`), and never before the queries resolve — the skeleton comes first.

Pure derivations added to `logic/libraryStory.ts` (table-tested): `templateRuns` (the matched runs; `templateStory` is now its count), `templateUseLine`, `trainingDayCount`, `splitLabel`, `templateSessionMinutes`, `templateWeekSets`. Both this page and the list pass `className="pl-lib pl-sub"` to `PageBody` — `.pl-row`'s 20px side margins assume the prototype's `.pl-sub` container.


The lifecycle pair itself is unchanged in behavior — only its home moved (the retired card's ⋯ popover → the story page's quiet rows, above):
- **`Duplikálás`** — re-sends the template's own document as a fresh `createTemplate` (days through the shared `toDayInputs`, `volumePerMuscle` baselines passed straight through) with a **`{title} (másolat)`** title, then navigates into the **copy's editor**: a duplicate exists to be changed, and the editor is also what proves it landed. `runCount` starts at 0 (server-computed — a copy has no runs).
- **`Törlés`** — a **two-tap confirm on the row itself** („Sablon törlése" → „Biztos? Törlés", the `CatalogExerciseSheet` idiom; row-local state, no modal), then `deleteTemplate(id)`. It is a **soft delete**: every run ever stamped from the template — and every frozen report — is untouched, which is exactly why a full confirm dialog would be over-ceremony here. **Known edge (`mezo-tlwa`, not fixed here — the bead is FE-only):** a run keeps its `templateId` after the template is deleted, and `MesoTemplateService.rerun` returns that id blindly while `start` resolves templates `…AndDeletedFalse` — so `Újrafuttatás` on such a run opens the start sheet and then fails with a `TRAIN_MESO_TEMPLATE_NOT_FOUND` toast. The FE workaround is the run's own `Sablonná` fork (which needs no surviving template); the real fix is backend-side (re-materialize on rerun when the linked template is gone, the legacy path already does exactly that).

**Run → template (the other direction, `mezo-tlwa`).** Two entry points fork a *run's* plan into a NEW template, both `createTemplate(runToTemplate(meso))` → the new template's editor: the report page's `Sablon mentése ebből a futamból` CTA (above) and a `Történet` card's compact **`Sablonná`** chip (beside `Újrafuttatás`, hidden in selection mode). `logic/runToTemplate.ts` is the pure mapping — title `{title} — sablon`, plan fields copied (an empty-string `goal`/`split`/`style`/`shortTitle` normalizes to `null`), days via `toDayInputs` (rest days included), and `volumePerMuscle` collapsed from each `VolumeProfile` onto its **provenance baseline** (`profile.source.baseline`), skipping any muscle whose baseline can't be resolved (nothing resolvable ⇒ `null`). The baseline — not the profile's live `mev/mav/mrv` — is deliberate: those numbers are that run's adjusted/ramped state, and freezing one run's fatigue into a reusable blueprint would be wrong. **Fork vs rerun:** `Újrafuttatás` reuses the run's ORIGINATING template (`rerun`, materializing one for a legacy run), `Sablonná` creates an independent copy you can diverge. In mock mode both paths work off `useMesoTemplates`' `setQueryData` cache mutators (the copy appears in the list, `runCount` 0) — note the mock's own archived fixtures carry no `days`, so a mock fork of one yields an empty day plan; a real run always has its plan.

### `Heti vizsgálat` + izom-részlet + day — the Titanium plan faces (`pages/MesoWeekPage.tsx` + `pages/MesoMusclePage.tsx` + `pages/MesoDayPage.tsx`, Train Titanium T9 Tasks 4–5, `mezo-88iwa.10`)

T9 refaced all three of the running block's drill-in pages onto the prototype's `.pl-*` vocabulary (`docs/design_2.0/prototypes/companion-titanium/plan-pages.js`), superseding mesocycle pages v2 Task 4's (`mezo-d20.15`) DS-era faces (the old `.mz-wtile` mosaic, the per-tile `VolumeBand` bar, `peakWeek`). Shared vocabulary across all three: **`tierLabel`** (`logic/tierLabel.ts`) is now the single Hungarian-only source for a muscle's volume tier (`Tartás`/`Építés`/`Hangsúly` for `maintain`/`grow`/`emphasize`) — hoisted off separate page-local English maps that used to live on the week page and duplicated, inline on the muscle page. Jargon ban across the family: „felső érték" never „plafon", „terv" never „blokk", „pihenőhét" never „deload".

- **`MesoDayPage`** (`/train/mesocycles/:id/days/:day`, Task 4) — opens on `.pl-dhero`, a full-bleed body-map poster (`BodyMap views="auto"`, heat = the day's own muscles, all at `'in'` — a single session isn't a fatigue read against a week-long budget, that's the muscle page's job) carrying the day's set count as the one numeral and pills (calibrated minutes, exercise count, week-share drawn as a mini bar + words, never a bare percent) — replacing the older letter-tile `PageHero`. Below the poster: `.pl-mrows` (one line per muscle worked, icon + name + a bar to the shared 8-set marker + the set count) → `.pl-exs` (read-only exercise VIEW cells: index + `MuscleChip` + name + a 4-cell szett×ismétlés/RIR/kg induló/bemelegítő grid, tinted by muscle color, rendered from the same `day.exercises` rows the editor route edits). BodyMap's first in-plan consumer. **The editor is no longer welded under the poster (`mezo-e1ii9`, Train parity P1 Task 4).** T9 refaced the top of this page and left an entire pre-Titanium screen scrolling below it (parity matrix §3): ⠿ drag handles, 🔥 per row, English `Grow`/`Maintain`/`Emphasize` tier words, the `HETI SZETEK` typo, `⚠ 1 jelzés`, `CSÚCSHÉT · IDŐBECSLÉS`, `STRUKTÚRA`, a duplicate exercise list and a duplicate add-CTA. Nothing was deleted — the editing **moved**: `MesoExercises` (the component that owns the PUT `.../days/{dayId}/exercises` save path) now renders on its OWN route, **`/train/mesocycles/:id/days/:day/edit`** (`pages/MesoDayEditPage.tsx`), exactly the way a TEMPLATE's day plan is edited at `/train/mesocycles/templates/:id`. A relocation, not a deletion, because the run's day plan had **no other editor route** — removing the welded one without this would have deleted the capability outright. The day page's tail is the prototype's own end-of-screen `.pl-add` („＋ Gyakorlat hozzáadása", landing on the editor with `?add=1` so the picker opens on arrival and the button still does what it says) plus a quiet „A nap szerkesztése" link for everything else the editor does. **The relocated editor is still pre-Titanium** — it was moved one route down, not refaced; that is a known, filed debt (bd `mezo-3dz0y`, §9, matrix §3).
- **`MesoWeekPage`** (`/train/mesocycles/:id/week`, Task 5) — `.pl-dhero` (full-bleed, `BodyMap views="both"`, the week's total as the one numeral, two sentences: how many muscles are growing/maxed/held, plus the week-over-week delta line) → `.pl-list`, one `.pl-item` row per muscle from `muscleTiles(arc, meso)` — ranked by ROOM TO THE CEILING, not raw ceiling — each carrying a verdict SENTENCE, not a number cluster (`Még {n} szett fér bele.` / `Ezt most szinten tartod.` / `Elérte a felső értéket ebben a tervben.`), prefixed by `tierLabel`. The live-rollover banner (`nextRolloverChips`) stays, unchanged — the one thing on the page that talks about the FUTURE. The old per-tile `VolumeBand` bar + 6-bar spark mosaic (`.mz-wtile`) is gone; the underlying math (`muscleTiles`, `logic/mesoWeek.ts`) is untouched, only the face is new. A **failed** arc fetch is still told apart from „nincs még ív": `Nem sikerült betölteni a heti vizsgálatot — próbáld újra.` plus an `Újra` retry (the arc hook's `refetch`).
- **`MesoMusclePage`** (`/train/mesocycles/:id/week/:muscle`, Task 5) — `.pl-dhero` (the muscle's own region tone, its body map, the weekly set count, a „say" sentence for what the number means + a „next" sentence for what Monday does to it) → `.pl-mstats` (sessions/week, week-one sets, the plan's own peak) → `.pl-scale`, THE GAUGE — a fill to the current number, landmark marks at the lower threshold and the ceiling, and a labelled pin, with the MERGED-LABEL rule (one caption when threshold==ceiling instead of two stacked captions) and `--nudge` edge-clamping — replacing the old `VolumeBand` bar, now **deleted outright**: it had no consumer left once this page and the week page were refaced (`components/VolumeBand.tsx`, T9 sweep) → `.pl-arc` (the plan's ramp, one bar per week, the pihenőhét hatched, per-week set counts under it) → `.pl-exs` (where it works, one row per training day, a door to that day's own page) → the unchanged 4-layer `DerivationSteps` provenance (`01 Baseline → 02 Rád szabva → 03 Eredő·most`) → **`.pl-versus`**, this plan against the previous one (`previousBlock(archived, group)`), captioned above the two bars with the previous plan's own title (**„Előző terved: {title}"**) so its identity stays legible, not just its numbers — NEVER red on a down move: the „Most" row keeps the muscle's own colour whatever the delta, and the sentence below calls a lower peak a shift of focus, not a failure. `logic/mesoWeek.ts`'s old `peakWeek` helper (the ramp's own last non-deload week, used for a hard-coded-`series[4]` bug fix) is **deleted** — nothing called it once this page's own „plan's own peak" derivation switched to a plain `Math.max` over the non-pihenőhét series.

### The former `Gym` tab — folded into `Heti`, then re-faced again by T12 (`pages/GymPage.tsx` is a thin alias)
**There is no Gym page any more** (`mezo-d20.3.2`). Its whole surface moved onto `Heti` (see that subsection); `/train/gym` still resolves — `GymPage` renders `TrainWeekPage` — because three in-app callers and PWA bookmarks still point there (`Terhelés` `owns`s it, navigation subsection above). `GymDayCard`/`GymStat`/`PhaseDots`/`GymSkeleton` were deleted in the F8 cleanup once the fold left them with zero references; `GymDaySheet` died with the direct-start flow (`mezo-bxpg`).

**Two generations of "what folded in," briefly.** Gen 1 (`mezo-d20.3.2`→`mezo-oyhy.7`) carried the meta card's live zone rows onto Heti's `StatStrip` (`Szetek`/`Gym napok`) plus a tappable group-level zone grid (`ZoneMiniGrid`) that opened a per-muscle detail sheet (`MuscleWeekSheet`: muscle heads, sport/run `▲` stimulus chips off `logic/sportMuscleLoad.ts`'s static heuristic, and a `growthForecast.ts` XP estimate) — the `Fázis`/`Split` meta stats and the prototype's `RPE átlag` stat were deliberately dropped (no cross-domain gym+sport RPE aggregate exists, and re-showing the phase is Mesociklus's job). **Gen 2 is the Train Titanium T12 Terhelés face** (`mezo-88iwa.13`, the `Heti` subsection above): `ZoneMiniGrid` and `MuscleWeekSheet` are themselves now retired — the same zone rows and the same per-muscle detail (heads, stimulus chips, XP forecast) live on, unchanged in substance, as the group cards and `GroupGlassBody`'s glass. The gym/sport day-tap routing (a completed instance → review, otherwise a direct start, a rest day a no-op) is unchanged across both generations — it now sits on `TrainWeekPage` directly rather than on the retired `WeeklyDayRow`. The `Időpontok` chip (`GymScheduleSheet`, §4 `GymScheduleSlot`, always visible incl. mock mode since `mezo-4t43`) and the `Mezociklus áttekintő` chip (→ `.../overview`, redirects to `Heti vizsgálat`) both rode straight through both generations onto the hero row. The `+ Saját` chip never survived as a chip either generation — `CustomWorkoutSheet` opens from Heti's `+ Saját edzés` CTA and Mai's own ghost/rest-day CTA instead (see the `Saját edzés` subsection below).

### `Saját edzés` — custom (meso-less) workout templates (`mezo-ws2x`)
A repeatable workout template that needs **no active mesocycle** — for ad-hoc/one-off days or for training with no plan running at all. Five entry points open the shared **`CustomWorkoutSheet`** (`sheets/CustomWorkoutSheet.tsx`, not mock-gated): the `Mai` rest-day card's dashed CTA, `Mai`'s today-only dashed footer (restored by `mezo-eahv` — the two are mutually exclusive, see §2), the `Mai` no-active-meso ghost's dashed row (all three `TrainTodayPage.tsx`), `Heti`'s dashed footer (`TrainWeekPage.tsx` — the old Mai weekly-plan footer that moved there with the list, `mezo-9bbc`), and — since the shell dissolution replaced Gym's `+ Saját` header chip — **Mai's own `＋ Saját edzés` ghost CTA** (`TrainTodayPage.tsx`, on the no-meso ghost and the rest-day card; this CTA outlived the hub's own retirement — it was always Mai's, the hub only ever *summarized* it). The sheet lists saved templates (tap a row → starts it; ✎ → edit) plus an **`Új összeállítása`** CTA into the composer **`CustomWorkoutBuilderPage`** — full-screen sibling routes `/train/custom/new` (create) / `/train/custom/:id` (edit), same idiom as the mesocycle/running builders, no sub-nav. Its own **"Indítsuk ma →"** CTA lands on `/train/session?day={templateId}` — the ordinary `ActiveWorkoutPage` flow, entirely unchanged (§4 has the backend contract: the D5 once-per-week guard is skipped for custom, D6 one-open-workout is kept). A completed custom instance surfaces as an extra **`SAJÁT`** card on its date via Mai's own `agenda.custom` list (`daySessions`'s `kind: 'custom'` member, tap → opens the review — see the Mai `Convergence` paragraph above; Heti's now-deleted `WeeklyDayRow` used to render the same list too, before the T12 reface) and counts toward the `Gym` load tile's count (`weeklyLoad`'s `gymCount = planned + custom`; the duration average stays planned-only) — but it is **plan-adherence-exempt** (see §5 for the full counting-semantics split). Data layer: `useCustomWorkouts`/`useCustomWorkoutActions` (`data/train/customWorkoutHooks.ts`, barrel-reexported from `@/data/hooks`); mock mode serves the `customWorkoutsMock` fixture and no-ops every write, byte-parity preserved like every other Train write. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-23-custom-workout-design.md`.

### Active workout (`ActiveWorkoutPage.tsx`, `/train/session`)
Full-screen takeover, **three phases** (`mezo-cd8s` replaced the old prep→active→auto-finish flow; the dead fourth `complete` phase — a settled read-back `setPhase` never actually reached — was retired in the T7 final fix wave, `mezo-88iwa.8`): **`prep`** (since `mezo-d20.3.8` the **6-tile mosaic hub** — breadcrumb → hero tile with the 4-cell forecast strip and the `⚡ Kezdjük el →` CTA above the fold → six tiles, each opening its own in-route page; see the dedicated paragraph below) → **`active`** (the **Titanium card list**, `mezo-88iwa.7` T6 — every exercise renders as one `.wo-card` poster in `.wo-list`, strictly-in-order per-card logging, "Múlt hét" comparison, a per-card records glass, medal chips, RP feedback debrief; see the dedicated paragraph below) → **`summary`** (the **two-act closing ceremony**, `WorkoutCeremony` — since Train Titanium T7 (`mezo-88iwa.8`) this is a POST-finish reward screen, not a pre-finish report, and it now carries the honest pending-sets line itself — see the dedicated paragraph below). A mid-workout hard reload **resumes** straight back into `active`. **Every phase flip resets the app scroller (`mezo-vad0`):** the three phases swap the whole screen *without* a route change, so `ScreenContent`'s navigation-keyed reset never fires — the long prep briefing's bottom-anchored "Kezdjük el" would otherwise hand its scroll offset straight to the card list (and to the closing summary). A `useEffect` on `phase` calls `scrollToTop(screenScroller())` (`shared/lib/screenScroll.ts` — the `.screen-content` lookup + the instant, never-smooth-animated top jump, now shared with `ScreenContent` itself). **Done-day gate (`mezo-cd8s`):** when today's instance is already **completed** and nothing is open, the outer `ActiveWorkoutPage` redirects to the review (`<Navigate to={/train/review/{completedTodayWorkout.id}} replace />`, placed **after** the `workoutPending`/no-workout guards) — the prep/session screen (challenges included) is unreachable until next week; mock mode has no `completedTodayWorkout`, so it never fires there. **Explicit finish (`mezo-cd8s`) + gamified level-up (`mezo-te8k`, P5).** The workout no longer auto-finishes — either the card list's own **3-state finish CTA** or the dock's **"Lezárás →"** (below) is the ONLY completion trigger, and both route through `finishAndCelebrate` → `finishWorkout(workoutId ?? 'mock', { onSuccess, onSettled })` (a `finishPending` flag disables the CTA while the POST is in flight; **`onSettled` re-enables it so a failed finish can be retried** — never stuck-disabled); on the response's `levelUp` it calls `showLevelUp(r.levelUp)` (the shared `LevelUpProvider` host, §5) and flips the phase to `summary` (T7 — every success path lands on the ceremony, the zero-pending shortcut included). The full-bleed `LevelUpScreen` overlay portals **over** the **ceremony** and its single `Tovább` CTA dismisses it to reveal the ceremony underneath. **Medal collection replaced the old demo PR path outright (`mezo-wp6n`)** — see the **`Medálok`** subsection of §2 and §4 below for the full engine: `PRToast.tsx`/`showPR`/`PR_DEMO_THRESHOLD_KG`/`hadPrFromSignal` are all deleted. Each `logSet` response now carries the set's real `medals[]`; a RECORD-tier medal (`WEIGHT`/`REPS_AT_WEIGHT`/`E1RM`) fires the real `MedalToast` right after the set is logged (typically during the rest window) and leaves a gold `MedalChip` on the set row's verdict cell, alongside its `✓`/`▼ cél alatt`/`▲ cél felett` rep-range status (see the card paragraph below), while a `TARGET_HIT` medal adds no glyph of its own — the verdict already conveys hit/miss. The old `· PR ✨` title suffix is gone; the ceremony instead names the session's RECORD-tier medals (incl. `SESSION_VOLUME`, which only ever resolves at finish) in its records strip. On a successful finish real mode also **invalidates the day's challenges** (`['challenges', templateSessionId, today]`) so the server-resolved outcomes land on the ceremony's challenge strip. When the progression switch is off the response carries no `levelUp` → no overlay, the ceremony shows directly. In **mock mode** `workoutId` is null (no instance), so the `?? 'mock'` sentinel still drives the no-op finish mutation, which returns a seeded `LevelUpResult` fixture so the prototype shows the overlay. **Mid-session plan growth is reconciled via `mergePlan`** (`mezo-ohvm`): the session is seeded once at mount, but the server can append template exercises while a workout is open (the fix-zárás closing block, §4) — an id missing from `session.planned` would read as 0 planned sets and count as done, silently auto-finishing the workout. A `useEffect` on `W.exercises` folds new plan exercises into `order`/`planned`/`prescribed` (appended at the end, progress untouched; identity-stable when nothing is new). **Challenges (`mezo-hbwi`, live) — proactive-owned, not in `WorkoutPlan`:** the prep-screen carousel is fed by `useChallenges(templateSessionId, localToday)` (not `W.challenges`, which stays `[]` in real), and `useChallengeActions.decide()` persists the L2 accept/dismiss. The `accepted` map derives from the server `status` (`accepted | hit | miss`) in live and from a local toggle in mock (byte-parity). Once a workout is decided each `ChallengeCard` renders its **outcome chip + line** (`hit → ✓ Megerősítve` / `miss → ◯ Nem igazolódott` / `inconclusive → ◌ Nem értékelhető`) with the accept/skip row hidden; live confidence-null reads „tanulom" and the `tools` chips are hidden (mock keeps `conf 72%` + chips). Type catalog is now **PR / Depth / Volume / `overload`** (`Tempo` still deferred — no tempo is logged, so it can't be honestly evaluated); `overload` is the deterministic daily-challenge tie-in (Progressive Overload Plan 3, `mezo-gj42`) — see the Progression-signal paragraph below and [proactive.md §HBWI](proactive.md). An accepted challenge targeting an exercise also earns that exercise's `.wo-card` its own accent line (below). The backend is proactive-owned: a lazy-on-prep `ChallengeGenerator` (structured targets, honest confidence/refs) + a NEW set-level `ChallengeOutcomeEvaluator` that judges hit/miss/inconclusive from the logged `exercise_set` rows (which FK straight back to the **template** exercise — `WorkoutService.java:204` — so no template→instance mapping) over the `challenge` table; the `/api/proactive/challenge` list+decide flow, the write path, and the full lifecycle live in [proactive.md](proactive.md) (§HBWI/§3/§4).

**Jutalomoldalak re-composition (2026-09-22, `mezo-p2777`; spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-22-jutalomoldalak-design.md`).** Step one of `WorkoutCeremony` is now `.cer-screen.is-staged`: ONE hero — five stars on an arc over the stone *fuse* (`.cer-bar`, the warm §5 radial), with **no star numeral** — then, beat by beat from one 2700 ms rAF pass (`is-b1` verdict → `is-b2` one `.cer-card` holding the record strip, the minutes + XP pair and the quiet `.cer-tally` of szett/ismétlés/kg×rep → `is-b3` the record stamp → `is-told` the CTA). Step two opens on a `.cer-recap-chip` (mini stars + verdict), then the kcal hero, then the muscle rows as one card with their `MuscleChip` crops. `SportCeremony` inherits the arc and the stone through the shared `.cer-stars`/`.cer-bar` rules; its own structure is unchanged. The paragraphs below describe behaviour that still holds; for the look, the spec wins.

**The two-act closing ceremony + the settled recap (Train Titanium T7, `mezo-88iwa.8`; design canon `docs/design_2.0/2026-09-15-ceremony-pattern.md`, prototype `companion-titanium/session.js`).** Closing a workout is a **reward**, not a report. `components/WorkoutCeremony.tsx` replaces `WorkoutSummary` on BOTH session phases; the shell below survives only on the review page. **The finish comes first:** the card list's own finish CTA (or the dock's `Lezárás →`) is still the only trigger, and the ceremony renders AFTER the finish POST resolved — so it shows what actually happened, and the closing note can no longer ride the finish body. **Act one** is the sky, five stars, the gold-stone bar and the three counters (`szett` · `ismétlés` · `kg × rep`), all driven by **ONE `requestAnimationFrame` pass** (cubic ease-out, 2400 ms) toward `cerScore(session, exercises).ratio` — `logic/cerScore.ts`, the pure, table-tested star math (per-metric done/planned ratios, meaned, rounded to halves). The pass writes `--p`, the counter `textContent`s and the star `is-lit`/`is-half` classes ITSELF rather than handing them to a CSS transition, because a throttled or hidden webview freezes a just-started transition at 0; a ref guards it so no re-render can restart it. **Act two** (`.is-told`, revealed when the pass lands) is the reading: the verdict sentence, the honest stat tiles, the records strip, **the challenge outcomes**, the muscle-star rows, the kcal estimate, the closing note and the single way out. **Reduced motion and the settled recap skip the pass entirely** and paint the final state on the first render — act two is told immediately, nothing animates. **Every number is a prop and nothing is fabricated: a tile whose input is unknown is not rendered, never a 0.** Minutes: the session screen has no honest measurement to show (see the `actualMin` decision record in §4) → no tile. XP: the finish response's real `levelUp.totalXp` or no tile — never a `sets × 10` count. **kcal** is the same `trainDayEnergy` estimate Mai's energy card uses (T5), over the same weight source Fuel's budget reads, so the two screens can never disagree — and it is **scaled by the done/planned set share**, so one set of twenty-two cannot earn the whole session's kcal; the tile says **`Becslés, nem mérés`** out loud and opens Fuel. **The challenge outcomes survive the shell's retirement** (Task 4): the same per-challenge mapping the old `Kihívások` strip read (dismissed/undecided → `skippelted`, accepted → the server's resolved outcome when there is one, else the FE preview over the logged sets) renders records-adjacent in act two as `.cer-record`-shaped `.cer-chal` rows — type · exercise, the resolved detail (falling back to the target), and the verdict word, toned sage/coral/neutral. **The way out is straight to Mai** (`Vissza a mai napra` → `/train/mai`): there is no intermediate read-only screen any more. **The closing note is guaranteed to save.** It lives inside the ceremony now, so it is written through `PUT /api/train/workouts/{id}/note` (`useWorkoutNote`) rather than the finish body, on BOTH CTAs (close and the kcal tile's jump to Fuel) AND on unmount — browser-back would otherwise drop it silently. The write never PUTs an empty draft (which would clear an existing note) and never re-sends a draft byte-identical to the last save, so a CTA tap followed by its own unmount cannot double-write. **The pending-sets line lives on this live ceremony itself** (T7 final fix wave, `mezo-88iwa.8`): one extra line when the close left work unticked, **`{N} szett kihagyott státusszal zárult.`** (`.cer-recap-note`), with `N` frozen at finish time (`pendingAtClose`) rather than re-derived from a session that can still change — `pendingSets` is passed unconditionally, and the component itself only renders the line when the count is above zero. `WorkoutCeremony`'s `settled` prop (paint the final state on the first render, no animation pass) is a component capability, not a live phase: nothing in `ActiveWorkoutPage` sets it any more (the dead `complete` phase that used to drive it is gone) — it is kept for a future slice (T13 candidate) to reuse on the review page's own read-back.

**Summary/review redesign — colorful pill/chip language (`mezo-w943`, design spec `2026-08-10-workout-summary-redesign-design.md`; supersedes the grey 2026-07-15 layout) — one shell, two modes.** ⚠️ **Since T7 (`mezo-88iwa.8`) this paragraph describes the REVIEW page only** — `WorkoutReviewPage` (`/train/review/:workoutId`, `closed` mode) is `WorkoutSummary`'s last caller; every sentence below about the `summary`/`complete` phases, the `closing` mode, the `Lezárva · ma` eyebrow and the `complete` phase's in-memory medal sourcing is superseded by the ceremony paragraph above. `WorkoutSummary.tsx` (`components/WorkoutSummary.tsx`) was the single component for both the `summary`/`complete` phases here AND the done-day review (`WorkoutReviewPage`, below) — frontend-only, no API/backend change. Every derivation is pure and table-tested in **`logic/summaryStats.ts`**'s `deriveSummaryStats(exercises, medals)` (below), keeping the component itself presentational. Top to bottom: a **hero** — eyebrow (`closing`: `EDZÉS VÉGE` coral; `closed`: `Lezárva · {huMonthDayDow}`, the `complete` phase inside `ActiveWorkoutPage` uses `Lezárva · ma`) + title + a **hero-number** (`{doneSets}/{plannedSets} szett`, Geist 200 tabular) + a fact subtitle (`{volumeT} t összvolumen · {doneEx}/{totalEx} gyakorlat`, `· ~{durationMin} perc` appended only when `durationMin` is truthy) in front of a decorative `aria-hidden` **halo** blob — `fire` (coral-amber) in `closing`, `calm` (sage-amber, slower) in `closed`; `prefers-reduced-motion` freezes it to a still image. Below the hero, **muscle-region pills** — `muscleRegion()` aggregation over each exercise's `muscle`, wash/deep family-toned, most-sets-first; a region whose every exercise logged 0 sets renders a struck-through, faded `off` pill; an unmapped/legacy muscle key (`muscleRegion() === null`) is excluded from the aggregation entirely. A **4-cell stat strip** — `Volumen {t} t` · `Rekord {n} 🏅` (amber only when `n > 0`) · `Célszett {n} ✓` (sage only when `n > 0`) · `Ø RIR {x,y}` (or `–` with 0 logged sets) — replaces the old three-box "Mai mérleg". **Medálok** (rendered only when `medals.length > 0`): RECORD-tier medals get their own amber celebration cards (🏅 + `MEDAL_TYPE_LABEL` + exercise name + `medalValueLabel()` + an `előző: {previousValue}` line); TARGET-tier medals no longer get individual cards — they collapse into one sage summary row (`✓ {n} célszett teljesítve` + a per-exercise `{name} ×{n}` chip row); the section label carries a `{n} rekord · {n} cél` mono counter. **Kihívások** (only when present) is layout-unchanged (status roundel + type/exercise + detail + outcome chip, `{n} megvan · {n} kimaradt` counter). **Gyakorlatonként — the set-chip map is the redesign's core**: every exercise gets a family-rail card (`muscleColor(muscle)`, the execution-card-v2 language, §2 Active workout) with a header (name + muscle tag pill + `n/m` set counter, warning-toned on a partial completion) and a wrapped row of `{weight} × {reps} @{rir}` chips — the matched **record set** (a RECORD medal's `exerciseName` + `setIndex`, falling back to a weight+reps value match when `setIndex` is absent or out-of-bounds) renders an amber 🏅 chip, the **heaviest set** (ties break to more reps, then the earlier set) renders a family-wash "top" chip, and any unfinished planned sets collapse into a single dashed ghost chip (`— kimaradt`); an exercise with 0 logged sets renders a faded card with a struck-through name, a `kihagyva` tag, and no chip row at all. **The chip map now renders identically in BOTH modes** — the old `showSetLines` prop, which limited `closing` to a top-set-only view, is **deleted**. The **note is real** since `mezo-d20.8.2.2` — see the dedicated paragraph below. (It was removed outright by F7.2 in between, because until then it accepted what you typed and dropped it; `mezo-s52z`, which tracked the same deferral, is superseded.) CTAs are unchanged (`Edzés lezárása ✓` + `← Vissza az edzéshez` in `closing`; `← Vissza` in `closed`). **`WorkoutReviewPage.tsx`** (the `/train/review/:workoutId` consumer, `closed` mode) previously rendered no medals at all — since this redesign it sources the session's medals from the existing **`useMedals()`** cabinet (`GET /api/train/medals`, dual-mode) filtered to `m.workoutSessionId === detail.id` (an empty filter result still renders no medal section, same honesty rule as everywhere else); the `complete` phase inside `ActiveWorkoutPage` keeps sourcing medals from the in-memory `sessionMedals` earned during the just-finished POST, so the two call sites source medals differently but render identically. `SummaryExercise` gained a required `muscle: string` (both call sites map it straight off `WorkoutDetailExercise.muscle` / `LoggedWorkoutExercise.muscle`) and `WorkoutSummary` gained an optional `durationMin?: number | null` (review: `detail.durationEst ?? null`; active: `W.durationEst`).

**The workout-level closing note (`mezo-d20.8.2.2`, spec [`2026-09-01-edzes-jegyzet-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-09-01-edzes-jegyzet-design.md)).** One sentence about the WHOLE session — distinct from the per-exercise note (`PUT /api/train/exercises/{id}/note`, durable on the template) and the per-set note (`ExerciseSetResponse.note`), both of which were already real. **Storage** is a NEW `workout_session.closing_note` column, deliberately not a reuse of `workout_session.note`: that one carries the TEMPLATE day's plan note (written by `TrainService` on mesocycle creation, published as `MesoDay.note`, snapshotted into `MesoDayJson`), and the table holds both template and instance rows — one column with two meanings could leak an instance's closing note onto the mesocycle-plan surface. **Two write paths, one schema** (`WorkoutNoteRequest`, `maxLength: 1000`, blank clears): the finish endpoint gained an **optional** request body (`requestBody: required: false`, the `MesocycleCloseRequest` precedent — a bodyless finish still works), and a new **`PUT /api/train/workouts/{id}/note`** mirrors `saveExerciseNote`. Their semantics differ on purpose: the finish body is **fill-if-empty** (finishing is contractually idempotent, so a re-finish or a bodyless retry after a failed one must never erase what was written), while the PUT is **last-write-wins**. `WorkoutDetailResponse.note` reads it back. **UI**: in `closing` the field sits ABOVE the CTA block — which is in flow, never `position: fixed`, so the mobile keyboard pushes the finish button instead of covering it — and is labelled as a question, **"Hogy ment?"**, because a bare box is the shape users skip. It is optional and skippable; nothing ever remarks on an empty one (ADR 0010). In `closed` the saved sentence returns as a Fraunces-italic block ("Amit aznap írtál"); **with no note, nothing renders** — except on `WorkoutReviewPage`, the one surface whose whole job is revisiting, where a `✎` edits in place and an absent note offers a quiet **`＋ Jegyzet ehhez az edzéshez`**. The draft and the writes are **page-owned props** (`note` / `draftNote` / `onDraftNote` / `noteEditing` / `onEditNote` / `onNoteSave` / `onNoteCancel`); `WorkoutSummary` stays presentational. **Since T7 (`mezo-88iwa.8`) the session screen's field moved into the closing ceremony**, which renders AFTER the finish POST — so there the note no longer rides the finish body at all: it goes through the `PUT` path on both CTAs and on unmount (ceremony paragraph above). The finish body's fill-if-empty arm stays for the retry case and for any other caller. Data layer: `trainApi.finishWorkout(id, note?)` + `trainApi.saveWorkoutNote(id, note)`, `useTrain().finishWorkout(id, { note })`, and `useWorkoutNote()` (`data/train/workoutNoteHooks.ts`) — whose mock arm writes the detail cache rather than no-oping, so a note the user just typed cannot vanish while the UI pretends it saved. Deliberately NOT included: a mood/effort chip row (RIR is already collected per set and consumed by the review's Ø RIR cell). **The note now also feeds the companion and the Memoár** (`mezo-d20.13`, spec [`2026-09-01-edzes-jegyzet-kontextus-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-09-01-edzes-jegyzet-kontextus-design.md)) — see [companion.md](companion.md) and [proactive.md](proactive.md); it rides the existing `companion → train` / `proactive → train` read edges, verbatim and merely truncated, never summarized. **Since `mezo-06o0.13` it also feeds the Emberek section** on that same read edge: `TrainingNoteMentionSweep` (`feature/companion/embedding`) name-matches `note` + `closingNote` — as ONE text under the row's single id, since the mention dedup key is the row — plus `sport_session.notes`, writing mentions under the new `workout_note`/`sport_note` `source_ref_kind`s; and `PersonExtractionService`'s day narrative reads the same text, so a person first named in a training note can become a candidate. Both are read-only and additive: nothing about writing or displaying the note changed. See [me.md §5.4](me.md).

**The review is a CONTEXT report (`mezo-d20.8.2.1`, F7.2, design spec [`2026-08-31-edzes-review-kontextus-riport.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-08-31-edzes-review-kontextus-riport.md)).** Until this round the `closed` mode was a colour swap of the closing report — it knew nothing the `closing` mode did not. The revisit is a different job (*mihez képest*, not *mi történt*), so everything `WorkoutReviewPage` now adds on top of the shared shell is context, and **all of it rides existing endpoints — no contract changed**.

- **The „Mihez képest" tile** (`.wr-cmp`, between hero and stat strip) names the **previous instance of the same template day** — the most recent earlier one, whatever the gap, because pretending to a weekly rhythm that was broken would be a lie — and gives three deltas mirroring the strip's own cells: volumen · célszett · Ø RIR, each with the reference's raw value beneath so reading the delta needs no mental arithmetic. The reference's age is the **distance between the two sessions** (`gapLabel`), not its age from today: stepping back onto the reference must not silently re-scope the phrase. **The tone rule is ADR 0010 in code** (`logic/workoutComparison.ts`, table-tested): the number is always signed and honest, the colour only ever rewards — sage upward, neutral graphite downward, and there is deliberately no `down` tone to reach for. **Ø RIR is hard-wired neutral**, because there less is harder, so an automatic tone would say the opposite of what happened. `frontend/src/features/train/workoutReviewTokens.test.ts` fails the build if a coral/terracotta/error token ever reaches the tile.
- **No reference → no tile.** A first instance of a template day, and any custom workout (no `templateSessionId`, hence no chain at all), renders neither the tile nor the stepping — not an empty state for something that legitimately does not exist.
- **The exercise inventory is a swimlane** (`.wr-lane`), replacing five near-identical stacked cards whose chip rows made the individual SET unreadable. One tile per exercise in its muscle family's wash: monogram disc, `REKORD` stamp, **one anchor number** (the heaviest working set, ties → more reps) and a set-bar row — solid logged · gold medal · faint warmup · dashed missed.
- **Depth is on the exercise's own view, not in the scroll** (`components/ExerciseReview.tsx`). Every set is its own tile — `90 kg × 8 · RIR 2 · CÉLSÁVBAN · 720 kg` — with the medal set in gold, missed slots as ghost tiles **numbered along the working sequence** (`B · 1 · 2 · 3 · 4`, never jumping over a logged warmup), and **the set note under its own set**: `ExerciseSetResponse.note` has always been on the wire and was displayed nowhere. Like the prep tile pages above, this is a **local view, not a route** — the closing report lives inside `ActiveWorkoutPage`'s phase machine and has no route of its own, so a routed variant would mean two mechanisms for one screen. Its stat strip's fourth cell (`Előzőleg`, the reference's top set) follows **the same gate as the comparison tile**, so comparison cannot enter the closing report by a side door.
- **Stepping runs on the template-day chain** (`useTemplateDayChain`, `data/train/workoutDetailHooks.ts`) — the same axis the comparison uses, so there is one mental model rather than two. Page-owned (passed to the shell as `footer`); the shell knows nothing about routes. At either end the button is **disabled, not hidden**, so the chain's existence stays visible.
- **The dead workout-note `<textarea>` is gone.** It had no `value`, no `onChange` and no field on the wire — it accepted what you typed and dropped it. Making it real is its own slice (`mezo-d20.8.2.2`: `finish` request body, column, `note` on `WorkoutDetailResponse`). Nothing is offered that cannot be kept.
- **Mozaik face**: the report gained a page tone (`.mz-p-coral` closing / `.mz-p-sage` closed) it never had, the `.mz-statstrip` floating cells in place of its own divider-seam grid, a clay `s-medal` disc instead of 🏅, and an `EntranceGroup` stagger — it was squarely in the F9 audit's "no entrance choreography at all" class. The challenge glyphs `✓ ◯ ⊘ ◌` stay: they are the designed geometric language, not leftover emoji.


**RETIRED — the workout opens in the card list (`mezo-e1ii9`, Train parity P1 Task 1).** There is no
`prep` phase any more: `/train/session` renders the Titanium `.wo-list` on its FIRST frame, exactly like
the approved prototype's `openSession()` (`docs/design_2.0/prototypes/companion-titanium/session.js`) —
Mai's `Kezdjük el` CTA opens the card list directly. `Phase` is `'active' | 'summary'`, and the old
"⚡ Kezdjük el →" tap became a **mount effect**: mock mode (no `todaySession`) keeps the Phase-1 local
behaviour, real mode POSTs `startWorkout` and binds the instance id, and a resumed session (`openWorkout`)
re-POSTs nothing. Both surviving paths are the SAME two the CTA carried, fired on entry.

What the retired 6-tile mosaic showed, and where each subject lives now: the **exercise/set counts ARE the
card list**; the **XP forecast** is answered honestly by the ceremony's real `+XP` (no pre-workout estimate
is offered any more); the **weekly zone** is the Terhelés tab; the **warmup** is the cards' amber B-rows
(the session-level 3-block protocol itself is a recorded death — `logic/warmupProtocol.ts` is deleted, see §9); the
**niggle** is the banner the card list already renders under its header; and the **challenges** moved into
the header `⋯` menu's **`Küldetések` glass** (`WorkoutChallengesGlass`, `components/WorkoutMenuGlass.tsx`)
— the same `ChallengeCard` stack, the same pending → honest-empty → list priority, the same accept/dismiss
`decide` contract and the same "passzolni ér" principle line the tile page carried, docked in a `GlassBox`
instead of a full page. The per-card accepted-challenge accent line is unchanged. The six
`pages/prep/*.tsx` tile pages are **deleted**, and `PrepExerciseTile`/`WeekZoneCard`/`ZoneTrack`/
`logic/prepBriefing.ts` followed them out of the tree in the same slice's sweep (Task 7, §9). The kalauz's `session-start` anchor and the chrome-less page's mini `?`
moved from the prep breadcrumb onto the card list's `.wk-top` header.

**The head of the card list — what the retirement put there (fix round 1, same slice).** Between the
`.wk-top` header and the first `.wo-card` the list now carries, in order: a **failed-start strip**, the
**niggle banner** and the **overload line**.
- **The failed start is LOUD.** Before the fix, a rejected `startWorkout` POST was *silent*: `workoutId`
  stayed `null` for the whole session while the card list looked perfectly functional, and every
  `logSet(workoutId ?? 'mock', …)` then POSTed against a bogus id. `startFailed` now both SAYS so
  („Nem sikerült elindítani az edzést" + „A szerver nem vette fel a mai edzést…" + an `Újra` retry that
  re-runs the start) and **blocks logging** (`logBlocked = startFailed && !workoutId`) until a retry binds
  a real id — a set the server cannot store must never look stored. Mock mode has no `todaySession`, never
  starts anything, and therefore never blocks.
- **The niggle banner carries the backend's own prose**, not a generic warning: `⚠ {muscleLabel} aktív ·
  {detail}`, where `detail` is `W.niggleWarning.detail` (the per-niggle sentence that had no reader at all
  once the prep mosaic's niggle page went; the hard-coded fallback only fires when the wire carries none).
  The prep page's `Értem · jó így` acknowledgement did **not** return — it was a *prep* gesture (confirm
  the flag before you start) with nothing left to confirm once the workout opens in the list (§9).
- **The day-level overload tally** (`components/WorkoutOverloadLine.tsx`, the `overloadSummary` wire
  field, `mezo-88iwa.4`) sits at the head of the list — its ONLY surface since the prep hero retired.
  Honest-empty: nothing to say, nothing rendered.

**Two header affordances the prototype's session header does not have** (recorded so the parity matrix
stays truthful, `docs/design_2.0/2026-09-16-train-parity-matrix.md` §21): the mini **`?`** (the kalauz
entry — this route is chrome-less, so the global header's `?` does not exist here) and the **`⋯`**
„Gyakorlat műveletek" chip (the per-exercise menu glass, which also hosts `Küldetések`). Both are real
capabilities with no prototype counterpart, kept deliberately rather than dropped to match the drawing.

**Superseded, still true as history — the mission-briefing prep (`mezo-bxpg`) and its 6-tile mosaic
re-face (`mezo-d20.3.8`).** The mosaic composed a breadcrumb + a coral hero tile (`várható XP` / `szett` /
`idő` / `izomcsoport` `StatStrip` + the `⚡ Kezdjük el →` CTA) + a `Mosaic` of up to six tiles
(`Gyakorlatok` / `Fejlődés` / `Heti zóna` / `Küldetések` / `Bemelegítés` / `Niggle`), each opening its own
local `MozaikPage` via `prepTile` state rather than a route. Before the mosaic, the same prep screen had
composed, top to bottom: **`PrepHero`** (`components/PrepHero.tsx`) — a centered overline `{huWeekdayFull()} · {weekLabel}` + `W.title`, a bordered XP ring **"+{totalXp} VÁRHATÓ XP"** beside up to 3 top skill progress bars (amber when `willLevelUp`, else sage; a **"⚡ szintlépés-esély!"** micro-badge appears under a bar whose forecast XP crosses `xpThreshold(level+1)`), and a stat pill **"{szett} szett · ~{rep} rep · ~{perc} perc · {n} izomcsoport"** — → **`WeekZoneCard`** (`components/WeekZoneCard.tsx`, live zone unification, `mezo-oyhy.7`, below `PrepHero`) — rows = the muscle groups trained **today** (`selectPrepRows`, biggest `todaySets` first), each with a `✓ zónában` / `▲ a mai edzéssel zónába érsz` / `⚠ a mai edzéssel túlmennél a kereten` status hint (the four `WeekZoneStatus` values) — where **`below` splits in two by `setsToZone` (`mezo-zr6p`)**: `→ a héten még +N szett jön a terv szerint` (neutral — the week's plan still covers MEV, `setsToZone === 0`, N = `remainingPlanSets`) vs `↓ a heti terv is a zóna alatt — még N szett kellene` (the whole week's plan misses MEV, i.e. the planner card's own `under` level). Before that split every `below` row read `még N szett hiányzik a héten`, which contradicted `SetBudgetCard`'s `✓ optimális zónában` for the same group on day 1 of the week (the prep card scores done+today only, the planner card scores the full week's plan); renders nothing while `useWeekMuscleLog().pending` or with no active meso — → the **niggle pre-flag** → **`ChallengesCarousel`** (below) → the **warmup block** → **muscle-region-sectioned** `PrepExerciseCard`s → the sticky **`⚡ Kezdjük el →`** CTA.
