Every one of those subjects still exists — the mosaic gave each its own page, and `mezo-e1ii9` then re-homed each one on the card list itself (the head of §2's `Active workout`, above): the counts ARE the list, the XP forecast is the ceremony's real +XP, the weekly zone is the `Terhelés` tab, the warmup is the cards' amber B-rows, the niggle is the list's banner (carrying the backend's own `detail` prose), the overload tally is the list's head strip (`WorkoutOverloadLine`), and the challenges are the header ⋯ menu's `Küldetések` glass. The one affordance that did NOT return is the niggle page's "Értem · jó így" acknowledgement — it was a *prep* gesture (confirm the flag before you start) with nothing to confirm once the workout opens in the list. `PrepHero` and `ChallengesCarousel` were the components the re-face left behind and have since been deleted; `PrepExerciseTile`, `WeekZoneCard` and `ZoneTrack` outlived the mosaic by one slice and were deleted by P1's own sweep (Task 7, §9), together with `logic/prepBriefing.ts` — so every module named in the rest of THIS paragraph is history, not tree.

The hero's numbers came from `logic/prepBriefing.ts` (**deleted** in P1's sweep, §9 — pure view-data adapters — no hooks, no fetching), fed by `useTrain`'s existing `activeMeso`/`exerciseRecords` and `useProgressionProfile().athletic`:
- **XP/skill forecast (D2)** — `prepForecast(day, athletic)` wraps the existing single-day `growthForecast({days:[day], slots:[], runSessions:[], athletic})` (gym-only estimate, `slots`/`runSessions` empty). `day` is **today's real `MesoDay`** (`activeMeso?.days?.find(d => d.current)`) **only when it's genuinely the plan on screen** — an **id-subset gate** (`isPlannedDay`: every meso-day exercise id is present in `W.exercises`) — else `pseudoDayFromPlan(W)` adapts the `WorkoutPlan` itself into a `MesoDay` shape, covering custom/saját templates, no-meso sessions, and a defensive fallback should the meso day ever drift from what's actually rendered. The ring/skill rows are **honestly null-hidden** (not zero-rendered) when the progression profile is empty AND every forecast skill's `xpEst` is 0 — never a fabricated ring from no real data. The stat pill (szett/rep/perc/izomcsoport) is a separate, always-live `prepStats(W)` sum over `W.exercises` — it renders even when `forecast` is null.
- **1RM badge (D3)** — `oneRmByIdentity(exerciseRecords)` maps each record to a `catalogId`-else-name identity key (`identityKeyOf`, the same records/challenge-grounding idiom as `ExerciseRecordService.findIdentityRowsIncludingDeleted`) → rounded `bestE1rm` kg; a record with no `bestE1rm` (bodyweight-only history) is simply **omitted**, never zero-stated.
- **Starting-weight pill (D4)** — `startWeightOf(e)` reads the exercise's first **working** `prescribedSets` entry's `targetWeightKg` (the engine's TODAY recommendation) — hidden (no pill) when null (plyo / first session with no anchor / hypertrophy-drive switch off); distinct from `e.anchorWeightKg`, the established anchor `pseudoDayFromPlan` forecasts FROM.
- **Exercise sectioning** — a local `groupExercisesByRegion(W.exercises)` (`ActiveWorkoutPage.tsx`, not the `muscleWeekFromMeso`/`muscleRegionGroups` helper — that pair now feeds `TrainWeekPage`'s `GroupGlassBody`, `mezo-88iwa.13`, since `MuscleWeekSheet` retired) groups by the `muscleColors` region family (`muscleRegion`/`REGION_LABELS`, the same 6-region system as Heti's group glass, `mezo-ly27`) in **first-appearance plan order** (not the sheet's fixed region order); a muscle with no mapped region (e.g. a custom/saját `'full'`-type exercise) falls into a single **`Egyéb`** catch-all bucket so nothing is ever silently dropped. Each exercise renders as a bigger **`PrepExerciseCard`** (`components/PrepExerciseCard.tsx`): a muscle-color rail + name + muscle pill, the optional top-right **🏆 {kg} kg / 1RM REKORD** badge, an accepted-challenge accent line (sparkle + `{typeLabel} · {target}`, ported from the old flat list) when an accepted challenge targets that exercise, and a pill row — 🔥 `{n} bemelegítő` (hidden at 0 warmup sets) · `{n} × working` · rep range · RIR · the `↑ {kg} kg-ról indul` starting-weight pill above.

**Challenge pending state (D5, an `mezo-hbwi` follow-up).** `useChallenges` (`data/train/challengeHooks.ts`) now returns a **`pending: boolean`** alongside `challenges`/`mode` — real mode: the list query's `isPending` (the lazy backend LLM generation in flight); mock: always `false`. **The three states survived BOTH re-faces** — the mosaic's `PrepKuldetesekPage` (`mezo-d20.3.8`) and, since `mezo-e1ii9`, the header `⋯` menu's `WorkoutChallengesGlass` — in the same priority order: **pending** → a `SkeletonText` placeholder — closing the old silent-gap bug where an empty-during-generation list looked identical to a resolved "no quests" state; **resolved empty** → the honest `"Ma nincs kihívás"` line; **else** the `ChallengeCard` list (vertical stack, not the old horizontal scroll-snap rail) with its `⚔️ Elfogadom`/`Passz` actions and the existing „tanulom"/outcome-chip states (unchanged, [proactive.md §HBWI](proactive.md)). The glass's eyebrow carries `A mai küldetések · {accepted}/{total} elfogadva` (the menu ROW's hint says the same, or `Ma nincs kihívás`), and the principle line says out loud that passing is fine — the ADR 0010 tone. The old `ChallengesCarousel` (its `⚔️ A mai küldetések · {n}` eyebrow, subline and dot-pager) is the component this replaced; it is now unreferenced (§9).

**Layout — the Titanium card list (`mezo-88iwa.7`, T6, ported from the prototype's `session.js`; replaces the S5/v4 execution-card+table single-exercise stepper without touching the underlying engine contracts).** The `active` phase is a full-bleed session screen: the route (`/train/session`) hides the bottom `TabBar` (`AppLayout.tsx` — `hideChrome` on the route string, the `.wk-top` header owns its own back affordance, §5). A sticky **`.wk-top`** header carries the back pill, the title + `n/m szett` live counter and the `⋯` "Gyakorlat műveletek" chip (opens the current exercise's own menu glass, below); under it a **`.wkx-progressbar`** strip (one flex segment per exercise, weighted by its planned set count, colored by `muscleColor(ex.muscle).rail` — pure derivation `sessionProgressSegments`, below — opacity 1 done / 0.45 current / 0.25 upcoming). Below that, `.wo-list` renders **every exercise of the session as its own `.wo-card` poster** — no viewed-exercise rail, no pager, no swipe, no jump-to sheet: the whole session is on screen, scrolled rather than stepped through.

**Card anatomy (prototype 1:1) — head → note/cue → rows.** ① the head: a `MuscleChip` art disc, the exercise name (+ a `KIHAGYVA` tag when skipped), a records button (opens the records glass, below) and the `⋮` menu button; a skipped card renders `.is-skipped` and shows no rows at all. ② an always-visible **`.wo-note` pill**, doubled: an accepted challenge targeting this exercise gets its own accent line (sparkle icon + target text — restored per-card after an earlier pass had removed the single-exercise metaline chip), and the durable per-exercise note (unchanged wire, `PUT /api/train/exercises/{id}/note`) gets its own pill below it, tap → the existing note editor. ③ a cue line: the engine's `ProgressionBanner` when a `progression` signal exists, else the plain rationale sentence (first-session/anchor exercise) — never both, same rule as before. ④ **`.wo-rows`** — a header row (`#`/`KG`/`ISM`/`RIR`/`✓`) then one `.wo-row` per effective slot (planned + `＋ Szett` extras). **Logging is strictly in cursor order per exercise** (the model's existing `nextSetIdx(session, id)`, unchanged) — there is no free navigation any more, one card owns one cursor:
- rows **before** the cursor are done — `.wo-row.is-done`, read-only, and tapping one opens the **same `SetEditSheet`** the pre-reface table used (`mezo-l3on`, below): a row whose logged entry has no server id yet AND no known write failure stays untappable (still in flight — the `mezo-l3on` no-id-and-not-failed gate, unchanged);
- the row **at** the cursor is the ONLY editable one — plain weight/reps number inputs (not `SetStepper` here; `SetStepper` now lives only inside `SetEditSheet`, below) plus, on a working set, the RIR pill row and an `L/B/R` side segment on isolation exercises, and the ✓ that submits;
- rows **after** the cursor show their prescribed target (weight/rep-range/RIR from `prescribedAt`), quiet and inert — no input, no tap target; after a weight swap they show the swapped weight at its equivalent reps instead (below).

**The per-card `⋮` menu is a `GlassBox` (`WorkoutMenuGlass`, T6 Task 4) — everything that is not "log this set" moved here, off the deleted `ExerciseActionSheet`.** Rows: **Videó** (only when the exercise carries a demo url — opens a second minimal glass, `WorkoutVideoGlass`, embedding the same `videoEmbed()` resolver `VideoDemo` already uses — `ExerciseRecordSheet` was the third user until `mezo-lf3cv` deleted it, §2 Gyakorlatok); **Jegyzet** (opens the existing note editor); **Szett hozzáadása** — unchanged `addExtraSet` + the `Csak ma` / `Minden hétre` prompt (`Minden hétre` still calls `writeExtraSetToTemplate`, bumping the meso day's `workingSets` by one via the existing `saveDayExercises` PUT; only add-set ever writes to the template); **Szett elvétele** — enabled ONLY for an unchecked TRAILING pending slot (`canRemoveTrailingSet`, the same one-slot-floor rule as the old table's delete, `mezo-l3on`); **Előrébb**/**Hátrébb** — a single-tap swap with the immediate neighbour in `session.order` (`movePosition`), replacing the deleted `ExerciseActionSheet`'s `SortableList` reorder sub-view; client-only/ephemeral, disabled at either end of the list; **Gyakorlat kihagyása** / **Visszavesszük** — `handleToggleSkip`: skipping still keeps already-logged sets (skip only abandons the remainder) and persists via `useTrain.skipExercise` (idempotent `POST .../{id}/skip`), while **un-skipping is purely client-local** (`unskipExerciseModel` — there is no server-side unskip endpoint; skip is a one-way audit signal, so reversing it never touches the network and does not survive a mid-workout reload).

**The per-card records glass (`WorkoutRecordsGlass`, T6 Task 5) replaces the deleted `ExerciseOverviewSheet`'s job with real per-exercise history instead of a session-wide jump list.** Ported off the prototype's `historyGlass`, on real data only: "A múltkori alkalom" shows `exercise.lastWeek` — the wire's top working set of the previous session (there is no full sibling-row list to show, so the label says exactly that; em dash on a first-ever workout); "Megdönthető rekordok" renders three bars — **BECSÜLT 1RM** (Epley, capped at `reps ≤ 12`), **LEGJOBB SZETT**, **LEGTÖBB VOLUMEN egy alkalmon** — each sourced from the matched `ExerciseRecordResponse` (`recordFor`, catalogId-else-name identity) with **today's** progress overlaid (`todayBest`/`barProgress`, `logic/recordFor.ts`) and a `MA MEGDÖNTVE` state once today's number passes the record; a field the wire doesn't carry (bodyweight, or never logged) renders an em dash, never a fabricated zero. `repRecords` render as a compact list. **There is deliberately NO trajectory chart** — a closing sentence says the longer-term arc is deferred to the `Gyakorlatok` page rebuild (T13), no promise language.

**The fixed session dock (`WorkoutDock`, T6 Task 6) replaces the old in-card `RestTimerBar`/`.donebtn` morph with one constant-height surface at the bottom of the screen** so the card list above it never jumps between the idle and resting looks. It is **portalled into `.phone-screen`** (the same idiom `GlassBox` uses) rather than rendered inside the scrolling `.screen-content`: `position: absolute; bottom: 0` then anchors to the phone FRAME, so the dock genuinely stays put at the bottom while the list scrolls under it — rendered in place it scrolled away with the cards. `.wo-list`'s bottom padding reserves the dock's constant height so the last card and the finish CTA are never hidden beneath it, and the dock unmounts with the active phase (it is rendered only there). Idle: a progress ring (`doneSets/plannedSets` share, **both counted over non-skipped exercises only** so the copy agrees with the finish CTA's `pendingSetCount`-driven state), "ELVÉGZETT MUNKA" + `n / m szett`, and a **3-state finish CTA** — `finishState = doneSets === 0 ? 'skip' : pendingTotal === 0 ? 'full' : 'partial'` (`pendingSetCount`, `logic/workoutState.ts`) — driving both its own icon/copy and the dock's own "Lezárás →". Resting: the SAME ring now reads the countdown share (**it fills as the rest elapses**, not drains — a deliberate prototype-matching deviation from the old draining `RestTimerBar` fill), "PIHENŐ · ‹EXERCISE›" + `m:ss`, and `+30s`/`Kész` actions — still backed by the unchanged `useRestTimer()` hook and `restSecondsFor` (150s compound / 90s else, hardcoded, §9). **Closing with pending sets asks first:** tapping either finish CTA with `pendingSetCount(session) > 0` opens **`FinishConfirmGlass`** (T6 Task 6) instead of finishing outright — it names every non-skipped exercise still carrying pending slots (`pendingByExercise`) and how many, then requires one more deliberate tap ("Befejezem így" / "Kihagyom a mai edzést" at zero logged) before those slots become `kihagyott`; a zero-pending finish skips the glass and finishes right away, since there is nothing to warn about.

**RIR is a 0–5 pill row (`logic/rir.ts`'s `RIR_VALUES`), not the old 0–3.** The picker's only source of truth is `RIR_VALUES = [0,1,2,3,4,5]` (mirroring the `train.yml` contract bound) — both the card's cursor-row picker and `SetEditSheet`'s edit picker render off the same array. **The pre-reface picker capping at 0–3 was a defect, not a design choice** (audit §A3) — RIR 4/5 (very light effort) were silently unloggable; the card idiom fixes this by construction, since there is now exactly one array both surfaces read. **Warmup rows never carry RIR (`mezo-eerq`, unchanged):** the cursor row hides the RIR pills entirely on a warmup slot, and the logged `SetLogRequest` payload omits `rir` for a `kind: 'warmup'` set — effort tracking stays working-set-only. **The per-set note has a wire gap the reface introduced (`mezo-azzcq`, open):** the old excard's collapsed "＋ megjegyzés a szetthez" input let a note ride the SAME POST as the set; the card's cursor row has no note field at all (`onLogSet` never carries one), so a note can now only reach a set through a SECOND round-trip — tap the just-logged done row, open `SetEditSheet`, type the note, save (which DOES send `note` on its `PUT`, unchanged). Logging at speed and annotating in the same motion is no longer possible; a follow-up to add the input back to the cursor row (or accept the two-tap cost as final) is open, not yet decided.

**Done rows are still editable via `SetEditSheet` (`mezo-l3on`, unchanged mechanics, new entry point).** Tapping a `.wo-row.is-done` opens the same sheet the old table's rows opened — `SetStepper` súly/ismétlés + a RIR row on a working set (from the same `RIR_VALUES`, above) + `L/B/R` Side on isolation exercises + the per-set note field. Logged: `Mentés ✓` + `Szett törlése`. Pending (a not-yet-logged slot, opened defensively — the card itself never surfaces one as tappable, but the sheet's own `mode: 'pending'` branch exists for it): the steppers show the target, disabled, delete-only. **Delete removes the slot too** (one fewer row, the ones after it shift up), floored at **one slot per exercise** — at the floor the button disables and the copy points to `Kihagyás` instead. Edit/delete work only on the **active** instance (`/train/review/:id` and the `complete` phase stay read-only); the slot count (`extra`/`removed`) is still **client-only** — a mid-workout reload restores the template's planned count, while logged sets' own edits/deletes are persistent. **The `TrainMapper.toSetResponse` `setIndex` MapStruct bug this slice's investigation found and fixed stays fixed** (`TrainMapper.java:77`, an explicit `@Mapping` — the implicit by-name match never wired the entity's `setIndex` onto the DTO's `index` builder method) — every real-mode `logSet`/`updateSet`/workout-detail response now carries it.

`makeSession`/`seedFromOpen` fold the recipe (`warmupSets + workingSets`) into `session.planned` + a typed `prescribed[]`, and the pure helper `prescribedAt(session, exerciseId, idx)` (`logic/workoutState.ts:56`) resolves each row's target; the cursor row's weight/reps inputs (and `SetEditSheet`'s `SetStepper` pair, on a done-row edit) **seed from the prescribed target** rather than from last week, and the old static "+2.5 kg" hint is **replaced by the engine `rationale`** (surfaced via `ProgressionBanner` when a progression signal exists, else the cue line's rationale sentence — both above). **The prefill inherits within the exercise (`mezo-eerq`, unchanged)**: a working set prefers the **just-logged working set's weight** over the static engine target (a mid-session deviation carries forward; the engine seeds only the *first* working set), and a **null target weight** (first session, no anchor) falls back to the previous logged set's weight instead of resetting to 0 — only the warmup ramp (per-set distinct targets) overrides inheritance. Reps/RIR still follow the target (goal-seeking), inheriting only in the no-prescription ad-hoc path — except that a carried-over *swapped* weight prefills its equivalent reps (below). **Only genuinely load-less (`plyo`) sets hide the kg input** (reps-only) — a first-workout null recommendation still shows the input for manual entry (the starting weight comes from the plan-time **`Kiinduló kg` anchor**, `mezo-anm4`), fixing the earlier bug where a null target also hid the input. When the switch is off, `prescribedSets`/`rationale` are null and the screen falls back to ad-hoc logging exactly as before (no pre-fill). The prescription is computed from **completed-instance history only** (with the anchor as the pre-history base), so it is stable within a live session and resume-safe (§9).

**Weight fields take the HU decimal comma (`mezo-py1i6`).** The cursor row's kg field and the plan editor's `Kiinduló súly (kg)` are `type="text"` + `inputMode="decimal"` inputs parsed by `logic/decimalInput.ts` (`parseDecimal`: comma or dot, 2-place float cleanup, null for an empty/garbage draft → the last value stands); the card keeps the live text in its own draft so a half-typed `97,` is never wiped, and shows the committed value with a comma (`formatDecimal`). Native `type="number"` rejected the Hungarian keypad's comma mid-entry (the browser reports `''`, which zeroed the draft), and the plan editor's weight field even opened the digits-only keypad. `useEditableNumber` (SetStepper tap-to-edit) now keeps 2 decimals instead of 1, so a 1,25 kg microplate (101,25) is no longer rounded to 101,3.

**Weight swap → equivalent reps (`mezo-l95v4`).** When the gym has no plate/pin for the prescribed weight (98 kg × 10 @ RIR 2, the machine stops at 95), typing the new kg on the cursor row recomputes its reps at equal effort via the pure helper `logic/repEquivalence.ts`: reps-to-failure (`targetReps + targetRIR`) → Epley e1RM (the backend `OneRepMax` formula) → reps-to-failure at the new weight → minus the same RIR, rounded, clamped 1–50 (98→95 gives 11). A swing of more than 20 % off the target leaves the reps alone (the round-trip is only trustworthy for small swaps); typed reps stand until the kg moves again; back to the target weight restores the target reps. While adjusted: a quiet `.wo-adjust` caption under the row (`95 kg-hoz igazítva · ajánlás 98 × 10`), the later pending rows show the swapped weight at its equivalent reps, the next cursor row prefills the carried weight with its equivalent reps, and a done row's rep-range verdict is shifted by the same rep delta (`adjustedRange`). **Owner decision (2026-09-24): the adjusted target is what gets logged** — `handleLogSet` sends `targetWeightKg`/`targetReps` = the adjusted pair, so TARGET_HIT and the character's `ProgressionAdherenceDetector` judge the effort actually asked for, not a phantom 3 kg undershoot; the original prescription is not stored. Frontend-only; no prescription (switch off / null target weight) → nothing changes. No per-machine equipment memory (deferred idea).

**Progression signal — RIR-aware, deload-aware (Progressive Overload Plan 1, `mezo-5pfe`).** The engine no longer just holds/bumps load off the rep count — it now also reads the last completed working set's **RIR** and the active meso's **deload-week** state, and emits a structured `TodayExercise.progression: ProgressionSignal | null` beside `prescribedSets`/`rationale` (the corrected double-progression matrix lives in §4; this paragraph is the UI it drives). When the viewed exercise carries a `progression`, the **`ProgressionBanner`** (`components/ProgressionBanner.tsx`) renders in the card's cue slot (③ above) instead of the plain rationale sentence — since the card reface (`mezo-88iwa.7`) directly in the card, not inside a `CollapsibleStrip` (that idiom retired with the excard/v4 table it belonged to): a `⚡ Progresszió` label + a **delta chip** (`+2,5 kg ↑` in coral for a weight bump, `+1 rep ↑` in sage for a rep build, `tartás` in amber for hold/deload), a two-cell **`Múlt hét → Ma a cél`** comparison (left cell from the exercise's existing `lastWeek`, right cell the new target weight×reps), and the engine's HU `rationale` line — three tones keyed off `progression.lever` (`po-weight`/`po-rep`/`po-hold` in `prototype.css`, deload sharing the hold/amber tone). When `progression` is null (first session / anchor-only, no real history yet) the exercise still falls back to the **unchanged** plain rationale line, exactly as before this change. The **prep screen** used to surface the same signal ahead of the workout, one tile-page down (`PrepGyakorlatokPage`'s `PrepExerciseTile`, both deleted with the prep mosaic — `mezo-e1ii9`): it rendered a compact **delta chip** in its `múlt héten → ma` footer row whenever `progression` carries a non-null `deltaKg`/`deltaReps` (hold/no-op progressions show no chip — both are null for `hold`), and the day-level **`⚡ Túlterhelés: n× +súly · m× +rep`** summary — sourced from the `TodayExercise`-sibling `overloadSummary`, hidden when both counts are 0 and each half omitted independently — sits on `PrepFejlodesPage` (`overload` prop); the summary splits the weight lever by SIGN (`mezo-88iwa.2`) — `weightDown` counts exercises whose WEIGHT-lever `ProgressionSignal` carries a NEGATIVE `deltaKg` (the grind back-off vs the last completed session), the FE renders them as a separate `n× −súly` chip, and a drops-only day titles the card `Visszavett súlyok` with no ⚡ Túlterhelés claim, while `WorkoutService` tallies every WEIGHT move into `weightUp`/`weightDown` on `deltaKg`'s sign. **Mock mode is not gated out**: `data/train/train.ts`'s `trainWorkout` fixture seeds representative `progression`/`overloadSummary` values (one weight-lever, one rep-lever exercise + a day-level `overloadSummary`), so the banner and both chips render in the prototype exactly like real mode — no special-casing needed in the hook. **The daily overload challenge (Progressive Overload Plan 3, `mezo-gj42`) is the last progressive-overload surface**: the day's biggest jump above doesn't just drive `ProgressionBanner`/the prep chips — it ALSO becomes a deterministic proactive **`⚡ Túlterhelés`** challenge card in the prep carousel (a guaranteed +1 alongside the LLM-generated PR/Depth/Volume challenges), completing the progressive-overload checklist (Plan 1 → Plan 2 → Plan 3). Generator/evaluator/display mechanism: [proactive.md §HBWI](proactive.md).

**The cursor row's ✓ is an UNLIT ring; resume seeds from a fresh read (owner bug report 2026-09-25).** The cursor row's submit (`.wo-check.is-submit`) used to be a filled accent disc holding the 3D `t-tick` — on every card's first row it read as "this set is already done" before anything was logged, and after a resume the next row looked like an extra ticked set. It is now a stronger hollow ring with the tick hidden (the prototype's `.tickc` vs `.tickc.on`); only a logged row (`.is-checked`) lights up. Separately, the session seeds ONCE from `openWorkout.sets`, so a resume (`Folytatás`, no `?day=`) that hit a cached today snapshot older than the last logged set started one set behind the server and a re-tick stored a duplicate row. `ActiveWorkoutPage`'s guard now waits (skeleton) on `useTrain().workoutFetching` **once at mount**; the `mountSyncedRef` latch closes on the first settled read so in-session refetches never unmount the session (`ActiveWorkoutPage.resumeFresh.test.tsx`).

**Rest timer hook is unchanged (`features/train/logic/useRestTimer.ts`, `restTimer.ts`) — only where it renders moved.** `Szett kész ✓` (the cursor row's ✓) still calls `rest.start(restSecondsFor(finishing.type))` whenever more sets remain on the SAME exercise — never on its last set, where the RP debrief takes over instead and no rest starts. Duration is still **hardcoded**: 150s for `compound`, 90s otherwise — there is no rest-duration data field anywhere in the model or DB (§9). `useRestTimer` still holds `idle | running {endsAt,total} | paused {pausedRemaining,total}`, self-ticks a 500ms interval, self-clears at 0. **The rendering surface is now the fixed `WorkoutDock`** (above), not an in-card CTA morph — `RestTimerBar.tsx` is deleted (T7 sweep, this slice): the dock's ring/label/actions read the same `rest.status`/`remaining`/`total` the old bar did, just from one session-level place instead of swapping inside whichever card was mid-rest. Because the dock is session-level, not per-card — and portalled to the phone frame (above), so scrolling never carries it off-screen — scrolling to another card during a rest needs no workaround any more: the rest stays visible regardless of which card is on screen; exercise skip (`handleSkip`), every exit path, and the summary/complete phases still call `rest.skip()` to clear it. A mid-rest reload still loses the rest (in-memory only, unchanged).

### `Gyakorlatok` — the catalogue + the exercise story (`pages/ExercisesPage.tsx` + `pages/ExerciseStoryPage.tsx`)

**Rebuilt whole in Train parity P2 (`mezo-lf3cv`, Tasks 4–5) — the pre-Titanium page and its record modal are gone.** What stood here before (a DS page-header, the „Top gyakorlatok · rekordjaid" **top-5** default ranked by `sessionCount`, dashed **ghost rows** for never-logged catalogue items, the `.excat*` variant-A three-zone record cards with their 3-cell stat strips, and the `⋯`/`▶` roundels that opened the authoring sheets) was replaced wholesale by the prototype's two screens (`prototypes/companion-titanium/gyak-pages.js` — `gyHome` + `gyDetail`). The **muscle-colour system survives untouched** (`logic/muscleColors.ts`: 21 live head/zone-specific muscle tokens since `mezo-wu1s` + the legacy coarse keys the volume log still emits → the six token families; `REGION_MUSCLES`/`LIVE_MUSCLES` remain the single source for the region chips and the authoring picker), and so does every join rule (`logic/exerciseLibrary.ts` builds one row per catalogue exercise, resolving records and medals through the shared catalogId-else-name `recordFor` identity, so the catalogue, the story and the workout glass can never disagree about whose record is whose).

**The catalogue (`/train/exercises`, Task 4).** A `.pl-dhero.pl-lhero` **poster** — `GYAKORLATOK` eyebrow, „A mozdulataid", the lead sentence and **three real foot facts** (`N gyakorlat · N rekorddal · N medál`, all three honest at zero, `libraryCounts`); the medal fact is also the vitrine's **only doorway** → `/train/medals`. Under it the `.gy-search` field — matching the exercise NAME **or its muscle label**, case- and **accent-blind** (`foldAccents`, so „bicepsz"/„hosszu" find their rows) — then a `Mind` chip plus one chip **per region the catalogue actually has rows in** (`libraryRegions`; a chip that would filter to nothing is never rendered). The list is one `.gy-card` per catalogue exercise (no top-5, no ghost variant): the `MuscleChip` anatomy art, the name, the muscle label, and either the best **becsült 1RM** with its medal count or the honest „még nincs naplózva" — an absent estimate is an em dash, never a 0. Tapping a card opens that exercise's own story (below). The list's **trailing `.pl-add` row** („＋ Új gyakorlat") is the one surviving door onto `CatalogExerciseSheet`'s CREATE mode. Real mode waits the catalogue, the records **and** the medals out behind the layout-matched `ExercisesSkeleton` — `useMedals()` answers `[]` while its fetch is in flight, and gating on the catalogue alone painted that empty array as an honest-looking „0 medál".

**The exercise story (`pages/ExerciseStoryPage.tsx`, `/train/exercises/:key`, Task 5)** — one exercise's whole history on one page, and the replacement for the deleted `ExerciseRecordSheet` (the modal that used to open from a record card). Top to bottom: the **hero poster** (muscle eyebrow, the name, the authorship stamp, and for a logged exercise three foot facts — `N alkalom · <mióta> · N t összsúly`, with a bodyweight row saying `N ismétlés` instead of faking 0 t; a never-logged exercise gets the prototype's own empty-state sentence and no record cards at all). **`Rekordjaid`** — three `.gy-rec` stat cards (becsült 1RM with its gain over the previous best, legjobb szett, legtöbb volumen); **every absent figure is an em dash**, and the 1RM card's bar and its „Becslés, nem mérés" caption belong to the FIGURE: no figure ⇒ no bar and no caption, and a figure with no series to compare against gets an **unfilled rail** rather than a full one that would claim „you are at your peak right now". **`Következő cél`** (`nextTarget`) is DERIVED, never forecast: the best set's own load with one rep more (a bodyweight best keeps the rep step and drops the kg), and the copy says „cél" because nothing models it. **`Az erőd íve`** draws Task 2's `e1rmSeries` through `components/StrengthCurve.tsx` — **solid only** (the prototype's dashed „projected" branch is omitted: production has no model that forecasts an e1RM), on a **time x-axis** (the wire omits a session with no e1RM-eligible set, so index spacing would redraw a three-week hole as one ordinary step), and **a real gap breaks the stroke**: an interval longer than `GAP_FACTOR` (1.75) × the series' own MEDIAN interval starts a new polyline, a segment left alone still gets its dot, and under two points the component says so in one sentence instead of faking a line. The rule is deliberately relative — a cadence is whatever that exercise's rhythm is — and it is **weak on very short series** (with three points the median of two intervals is their mean, so a hole must be roughly 7× the normal step before the line breaks); under-breaking is the safer direction and is chosen knowingly. **`Medáljaid`** filters `useMedals()` through the same identity rule (`medalsForExercise`), **`Hol szerepel`** is derived CLIENT-SIDE with no endpoint (`whereUsed` over the running plan's days and the shelf's templates, each row a door to that day or template), and **`Gyakorlat kezelése`** is the authoring row — `Szerkesztés` (→ `CatalogExerciseSheet` in edit mode, which hosts the two-tap delete) only when the server says `editable`, `Demó videó` (→ `VideoUrlSheet`, and the row itself says whether a video is attached) only on `mediaEditable`. Both flags are server-derived per viewer; an absent flag (the mock-mode static seed) means no, so the section renders **nothing at all** for a read-only row rather than promising an affordance that would come back 403. The hero's **`Saját` / `Közös · {név}` stamps** (`authoredByMe`/`authorName`) render here and nowhere else in the app.

**Dates on this page are stated as claims about the reader's own history, so two rules apply that `huMonthDay` alone breaks.** ① A date older than ~10 months carries its **year** (`huMonthDayAged`, `shared/lib/dates.ts` — added beside `huMonthDay`, which stays as-is for its many callers where the date is inherently recent): „Szep 3 óta" read on a September screen means a fortnight, and the truth was a year. ② The „óta" fact is only **absolute when the series covers the whole history**. The wire keeps only the newest 52 e1RM points (`E1rmSeries.MAX_POINTS`, mirrored FE-side as the single constant `E1RM_SERIES_MAX_POINTS` in `data/train/trainApi.ts` — `sinceFact`, the mock fixtures and their tests all read it from there), so the oldest date the row carries can be the WINDOW's start — when the series comes back **FULL at that cap** with more sessions behind it, the hero says **„ebből az utolsó N látszik"** instead of an „óta" date it cannot honour (`sinceFact`, table-tested both ways). **The cap is the tell, not `sessionCount > points`** (the P2 fix wave corrected this): points are also lost to ELIGIBILITY — a session with nothing e1RM-eligible is OMITTED from the series while still counting in `sessionCount` — and those sessions are scattered through the history rather than cut off the front, so the old rule fired „az utolsó N látszik" on rows where nothing had been capped and the N were not the last N of anything. Below the cap, and for a row with no series at all (bodyweight), the hero states the oldest date the row carries.

**The prototype's hero CUE PROSE („Talpak lent. Stabil lapockák…") is deliberately omitted** — no production field carries per-exercise cue text and inventing one would be writing coaching copy out of nothing — and so are the demo **stills**: the story has no image section, because the prototype's has none and a player/gallery would be a new section rather than a ported one. The still surface that remains is the mesocycle builder's `ExercisePickerSheet` (§5).

**Multi-user catalog (S5, `mezo-qw37.5`) — unchanged mechanics, new surfaces.** The catalog is one shared pool (every user's rows show for everyone) and each row arrives with the four server-derived flags (`editable`, `mediaEditable`, `authoredByMe`, `authorName`; the FE never reasons about roles). `CatalogExerciseSheet` (`sheets/CatalogExerciseSheet.tsx`) is still the authoring form — name / muscle (21-token region-grouped picker) / type (`compound|isolation|plyo`) / stim + fatigue decimal steppers / demo-video URL — creating via `createCatalogExercise`, editing via `updateCatalogExercise`, with **delete inside the sheet** as a two-tap-confirm soft delete (other users' plans that link the row keep resolving). Built-in (master) rows stay read-only for the full edit for everyone incl. the OWNER (server 409, §4); `VideoUrlSheet` (`sheets/VideoUrlSheet.tsx`) is the one-field URL editor (`setExerciseVideo`, `PUT /…/{id}/video`) with its `Eltávolítás` action, and a forbidden write that slips through a stale list is a 403 surfaced as the generic „Mentés sikertelen" toast. **Mock mode seeds the authorship stamps but NOT the edit flags** (`data/train/train.ts`): mock catalogue writes are deliberate no-ops, so an authoring row whose Save does nothing would be a worse lie than an absent affordance — one row carries `authoredByMe`, another an `authorName`, and the display-only half of the feature is walkable offline. An inline **demo video** (`VideoDemo`, a `▶ Demo` chip revealing a lazy youtube-nocookie iframe) now appears in **two** places: the active workout's video glass and the mesocycle builder's `ExercisePickerSheet` — the catalogue browser's third one went with `ExerciseRecordSheet`, and the story page's video row is the AUTHORING half, not a player.

### `Medálok` — the medal cabinet (`pages/MedalsPage.tsx`, `/train/medals`, `mezo-wp6n`)
A read-only, dated history of every medal derived from the owner's logged sets — reached from the Edzés
hub's **`Medálok`** tile (the `s-medal` clay spot). **Mozaik 2.0 re-face (`mezo-d20.3.2`):** a compact
`PageHero` (the `s-medal` spot + the total + an `ebből N e hónapban` sub) over `.mz-facttile` cabinet rows
carrying a `REKORD`/`CÉL` chip — the grouping, ordering and every data read are verbatim from before.
Nothing here is a life-domain badge (the 9
`Kitüntetések` on `/me/growth` are untouched, see [growth.md](growth.md)); medals stay train-domain.
Rows are grouped by date, newest first, within-day order exactly as `GET /api/train/medals` returns it
(§4) — each row names the exercise, the medal type, the achieved value, and what it beat
(`previousValue`/`previousDate`, always null on a `TARGET_HIT`). Because the
four RECORD types (`WEIGHT`/`REPS_AT_WEIGHT`/`E1RM`/`SESSION_VOLUME`) are **replayed over the entire
existing set history** rather than stored ([ADR 0016](../decisions/0016-medals-derived-not-materialized.md)), the cabinet is populated the first time it's
opened with medals the user never saw live — the cabinet copy says so honestly, calling the history
reconstructed rather than implying it was tracked all along. `TARGET_HIT` cannot be backfilled the same
way (its prescription snapshot didn't exist before this change, §4), so it only accrues from the ship
date forward. Medals also surface live during and right after a workout — see the `Active workout`
subsection below and §4 — this page is the durable, browsable record of all of them.

### `Sport naplózás` — the full-screen flow (`pages/SportLogPage.tsx`, `/train/sport/log`, `mezo-88iwa.9` T8 Task 4)

Sport logging as its own screen instead of a sheet, ported from the Titanium prototype's step machine (`docs/design_2.0/prototypes/companion-titanium/sport.js:12-115`) onto the `sp-` CSS section and the eleven-sport table (`features/train/logic/sports.ts`).

- **pick** — the eleven `.sp-tile`s (clay art + name + `~N perc` target). Ten open the form; **Futás routes to `/train/futas`** — running is its own feature and its own wire, never a sport session.
- **form** — only the fields that sport actually asks (`SPORTS[x].fields`, `onlyMode` siblings gated by the chosen mode), duration prefilled from `targetMinutes`, and the mandatory felt-effort slider **`Megélt terhelés (RPE)`** → the wire's required `rpe`. The prototype's túra had no effort slider (its MET came from the climb); ours does, because `SportSessionCreateRequest.rpe` is required and a silent default would be a number the athlete never said.
- **kcal — no logged-session preview, ever; the estimate is the backend's call.** A LOGGED session's burn is computed server-side by the shared `ActivityEnergyModel` (net-of-rest: `(MET−1) × BMR/24 × hours`, RPE-banded, `mezo-32m82`), off the athlete's own body — the frontend never re-derives a logged number, **one estimator, one owner**; the prototype's live in-form estimate is deliberately dropped. So `.sp-kcal` is purely the override affordance — *"Kalória: becslést mentünk"* + an **Aktív kalória (ha az órád mérte)** dialog (`GlassBox`) writing `kcalOverride`, whose helper text is explicit that this is the movement's net/active kcal only (*"Csak a mozgás többletét írd be — az órád »aktív« kalóriáját, ne az összeset."*), shown back with the `.sp-note` *"Saját értéket adtál meg — ezt mentjük, nem a becslést."* and clearable. The real figure comes from the response: `SportSessionResponse.kcal` (null when the athlete's rest energy is unknown server-side — never a fabricated 0) + `kcalIsEstimate` (true = the BE's net estimate, false = the athlete's own override won, stored verbatim). `RunSessionLogResponse.kcal` uses the SAME `ActivityEnergyModel` (RPE-banded, `mezo.train.energy` `run` row), not a pace-based assumption — `kcalIsEstimate` there is always `true` (the run wire has no override field). Unlogged/planned previews (Train today's energy card, the sport ceremony's pre-save estimate) DO show a number — the FE mirror `data/train/activityEnergy.ts` — but that preview is replaced the instant the session is saved, never re-estimated once logged.
- **save** — `useQuickLogSport().logSportSession`, then the `onSaved(response)` seam mounts **`SportCeremony`** (T8 Task 5, `components/SportCeremony.tsx`) — the same one-shot rAF choreography + gold-stone reward pattern as `WorkoutCeremony` (§2 Active workout, design canon `docs/design_2.0/2026-09-15-ceremony-pattern.md`), scaled to a sport log's smaller shape: minutes/RPE counters, a `.cer-kcal` tile fed **verbatim** by the saved response's `kcal`/`kcalIsEstimate` (never re-derived — absent kcal hides the tile entirely, never a fabricated 0) captioned `Becslés, nem mérés` or `Saját értéked` depending on `kcalIsEstimate`, and any `levelUp` XP. `Tovább` returns to `/train/mai`.
- **Field → wire folding.** `minutes→duration`, `intensity→rpe`, `sets→setsPlayed` (volleyball Meccs only), `rounds→rounds` (cross/TRX), `shoulder→shoulderStrain` (`enyhe`/`közepes`/`erős` → 3/6/9). Everything the table asks that has **no wire field at all** folds into `notes` as one short Hungarian line rather than being dropped: `Táv: 25 km` (bike/swim/hike distance), `Terep: dombos` (bike), `Úszásnem: gyors` (swim), `Szintemelkedés: 300 m` (hike), `Típus: Meccs|Egyes|…` (volleyball/foci/kosár/tenisz mode), `Nehézség: közepes` (Egyéb effort), and the Egyéb activity name verbatim at the front.
- **Entry points.** `TrainTodayPage`'s `.tr-alt` **Sport naplózása** chip and `SportPage`'s **+ Log** header chip. The day-card `Logold a session-t`/`Pótold` CTAs and the schedule slot's `Logold ›` still use `SportLogSheet` — they carry a preselected sport and (for Pótold) a **past date**, neither of which this flow takes yet. `SportLogSheet` therefore survives; `QuickLogSurface` also mounts it, and `NumberStep`/`ScaleRow` are imported from it by `RunLogSheet`, `SportEventSheet` and `SportScheduleSheet`.
- **Nav.** Sits under `/train/sport`, which the Mai tab already `owns` (`navModel.ts` `isPrefix`) — no new `owns` entry needed.

### `Sport` — volleyball · cross · TRX (`pages/SportPage.tsx`, `/train/sport`)

**Design 2.0 did NOT re-face this page** — it is a full-page sibling of the Edzés hub that keeps its own `.pghead-np` head (`Edzés · Sport`, h1 `Röplabda`, a `+ Log` chip). `mezo-d20.3.4` spent its budget on **load-bearing fixes + the designed additions** the prototype/README checklist called for instead: the `Napló` session cards' type tag was hardcoded to `stag-sport` (a cross or TRX session read `RÖPI`) and now grades kind-correctly through `sportOf`/`SPORT_TAGS`/`SPORT_TONE`, with the fact row branching `Setek (röpi)` vs `Körök (cross/TRX)` off a new `SportSession.rounds` field threaded through the API mapper and the mock cache; the big **RPE readout grades off RPE itself** (7+ coral, 8+ amber, **never red**) rather than falling back through `intensity`; today's `Heti terv` slot gained an inline **`Logold ›`** chip that pre-selects that slot's sport when opening `SportLogSheet`; and `SportLogSheet` gained the designed **notes textarea** — `SportSessionCreateRequest.notes` had existed on the contract with no UI surfacing it.

**DS-migrated (`mezo-setx.6.5`).** `.page-header` + `PageTitle`/`Eyebrow` head with a `.pgact` `+ Log`; the hero borrows `.todaycard`'s gradient formula/radius/padding/shadow; `SportStat` renders the shared **StatStrip cell** vocabulary (the same move `GymStat` made in `.6.4`), keeping its rose `highlight` because a stat VALUE is data-viz — the one band where the domain accents are still legal ([ADR 0018](../decisions/0018-adopt-exist-zen-design-system.md) D5). **The view switcher is the point of the bead:** it was three 10px uppercase buttons tinted in the sport rose, which D5 forbids on buttons. It becomes the new shared **`.segtabs`/`.segtab`** control, whose selected segment speaks **primary** like every other selected control in the app. Session rows move to the ListItem voice (16/600 time, 14px meta), the `MA`/`EGYSZERI` markers to DS Tag stamps, and the `+ Egyszeri esemény` footer to the shared `.dashedcta`.
Hero with week stats (Sessions/Idő/RPE/Váll) and a 3-button switcher: **`Heti terv`** (7-day recurring schedule, editable in real mode via `SportScheduleSheet`), **`Napló`** (logged sessions), **`Cross-load`** (impact rows). Header chip "+ Log" opens the full-screen sport-logging flow (`/train/sport/log`, `SportLogPage.tsx` — see below); the inline `Logold ›` on a scheduled slot still opens `SportLogSheet` in place, because it carries that slot's preselected sport. **`SportScheduleSheet` is a per-day slot-list editor** (`mezo-05v6`, rewritten from a one-slot-per-day draft): each of the 7 days holds an independent **list** of slots (`+ Sport hozzáadása` adds another), every slot carrying its own sport selector (Röpi | Cross | TRX, the same chip idiom as `SportLogSheet`) plus time/duration/location/intensity — so a day can hold any number of slots, including two of the same sport (e.g. a noon TRX + an evening volleyball match). **`kind` (training|match) stays volleyball-only**: the kind selector is hidden for cross/TRX slots, which always save `kind: 'training'` (no DB/contract change to `kind`). Saving emits the full slot list to `PUT /api/train/sport-schedule` (full-replace), unchanged. The switcher's selected segment is **sticky** (`useStickyTab('train.sport.view')`), so leaving and returning restores it rather than snapping to `Heti terv`. Re-skinned to the Napiv rose `--wash-sport`/`--tag-sport` vocabulary (S4, `mezo-8141`) — superseding the pre-S4 `--cat-tendency` (pink) accent. **`Heti terv` renders every slot of a day** (`SportWeekView`, `SportPage.tsx:247`): a day card stacks all of that day's slots (e.g. a Tue cross/TRX session + a Tue volleyball match) rather than only the first match, each slot's time/duration/MA-chip/role-location row tagged with a `.stag-sport` chip (`SPORT_TAGS[sportOf(session)]`) whenever it's not volleyball (volleyball, the implicit default, stays untagged); `isToday` is now `daySlots.some((s) => s.today)`. The hero eyebrow reads **`Heti ritmus · {weeklyHours}h`** (the `court` suffix was dropped since a mixed week's hours are no longer purely court time) — `weeklyHours` is a plain JS number with no `hu-HU` locale formatting, so e.g. `2.5` renders with a dot, not a comma. **One-off (non-recurring) events (`mezo-e1sp`):** below the week list (and below the no-schedule ghost too) the `Heti terv` view renders a **`SportEventsSection`** — the upcoming (today+future) one-off events with per-row delete, plus the single dashed **`+ Egyszeri esemény`** entry point opening **`SportEventSheet`** (`sheets/SportEventSheet.tsx`: date defaulting to today + sport selector + time/duration/location; volleyball defaults `kind: 'match'`, cross/TRX force `'training'` like the schedule editor). Saved events persist via `POST /api/train/sport-events` (§4) and the **current week's** events merge into `sport.schedule` in `trainHooks` (`mergeEventsIntoSchedule`), so a one-off lands on its day card here (tagged `EGYSZERI`), on `Mai`/`Heti`, and in the fuel day-plan/calorie budget with **zero extra wiring** — its `today` flag is **date-based**, and `weeklyHours` deliberately excludes one-offs (the eyebrow describes the recurring rhythm). Works in **both modes** (mock emulates the server in the client-owned `['train','sportEvents']` cache). `SportScheduleSheet`'s `initial` filters `oneOff` sessions out so the full-replace weekly editor can never convert an event into a recurring slot.

### `Futás` — running (`pages/RunningPage.tsx`, `/train/futas`)

**Same story as Sport: a full-page sibling on its pre-redesign `.pghead-np` face, with `mezo-d20.3.5` spent on load-bearing fixes rather than a re-face.** Four of them: the `E heti edzés` session cards got a real **three-way CTA** keyed on `blockId + week + sessionKey` — today ⇒ `Naplózd`, a past weekday ⇒ **`Pótold`** (retroactive, logging against that weekday's real calendar date via the new `dateForDayOfWeek`), a future one ⇒ a disabled grey `Naplózás ▸`, already-logged ⇒ a `KÉSZ ✓` badge — where the page previously collapsed all of that into a binary onLog/no-onLog toggle; **pyramid session pills** regained the honest derived-rest note (`pihenő = szakasz × 2`) beside the `15／30／45` work ladder, which had been silently dropped; `RunLogSheet`'s **`Teljesített körök` stepper now shows for pyramid sessions too**, not only sprint — the FE half of the `completedRounds` scoring bug (F6.3 owns the backend half), defaulting to the week's prescribed segment count when no explicit round count exists; and `Napló` gained the **pulzus-megnyugvás (HR-recovery) trend chart** off the already-existing `hrRecoverySec` field. The RPE-target chip's hot (sprint 9+) tint also moved onto `color-mix` over `--error` instead of a hardcoded rose rgba that never matched the token.

**DS-migrated (`mezo-setx.6.6`).** Same treatment as `Sport`, and deliberately the same shape: DS head, the shared **`.segtabs`** switcher (sky off the buttons, primary on the selected segment), stat labels on the StatStrip label voice, session titles at 16/600, the run-type and status markers on the DS Tag spec, and the Fraunces `.text-meta-sm` for the empty-state asides. The two pages ship in one change because `.6.6` consumes the `.segtabs` class `.6.5` introduces — splitting them would have meant a branch whose control does not exist yet.
3-segment switcher: **`E heti edzés`** (active block hero with `RunWeekStrip` + prescribed `RunSessionCard`s — each now shows its `timeOfDay` next to the weekday — + `RunCrossLoadCard`), **`Napló`** (logged runs newest-first), **`Tervek`** (Aktív/Tervezett/Archív library; "+ Új terv" → builder). Re-skinned to the Napiv sky `--wash-run`/`--tag-run` vocabulary (S4, `mezo-8141`) — superseding the pre-S4 `--info` (blue) accent; the builder (below), `WeekdayGrid`, and `RunWeekEditor` were migrated off the legacy `--info` alias onto the semantic `--sky` accent in the S8 cleanup (`mezo-mifi`) — they carry the mid-hue accent rather than the full `--tag-run`/`--wash-run` vocabulary. The switcher's selected segment is **sticky** (`useStickyTab('train.futas.view')`), so creating a plan from `Tervek` and pressing the builder's breadcrumb `← Futás` returns to `Tervek`, not the default `E heti edzés` (mezo-0h9). The **builder** (`RunningBlockBuilderPage.tsx`, `/train/futas/:id`) edits title/goal, a **1–8 week** add/remove row (`RunningBlockBuilderPage.tsx:153-177`), and the selected week via `RunWeekEditor`. Editing is **auto-saved** — no Save button; a debounced write (`:65-69`) persists ~600 ms after the last keystroke and flushes on back, with a `Mentés…`/`✓ Mentve`/`Nem mentve` status pill (`:126-128`). Lifecycle collapses to a **single status-dependent CTA** (planned→`Aktiválás`, active→`Lezárás`) plus a `⋯` **overflow menu** (`OverflowMenu`, `:206`) carrying `Duplikálás`/`Törlés`. Each `RunWeekEditor` session is a **two-zone card** (`RunWeekEditor.tsx:59-92`): **Menetrend** (`WeekdayGrid` single-select weekday + a `type="time"` time-of-day input — both plan-level) over **Terhelés** (week-level load: sprint rounds+rest steppers / pyramid work-second pills). The old hardcoded "Kedd · Sprint" / "Péntek · Piramis" labels are gone — weekday + time are user-editable.

---

## 3. Architecture & data flow

The whole area flows through **two hooks**, both re-exported from the single data boundary `frontend/src/data/hooks.ts:202-203`:

```
View (features/train/**)
  → useTrain()      (data/train/trainHooks.ts)   |   useRunning()  (data/train/runningHooks.ts)
    → isMockMode()  (@/data/_client/mode → VITE_USE_MOCK !== 'false')
       ├─ MOCK: TanStack Query initialData = static fixtures (data/train/train.ts, data/train/running.ts)
       │        writes no-op (useTrain) OR emulate the server via queryClient.setQueryData (useRunning)
       └─ REAL: api client (data/train/trainApi.ts, data/train/runningApi.ts) over apiFetch → /api/train/*
            → TrainController.java (implements generated TrainApi)
              → {Train,Workout,Sport,ExerciseCatalog,ExerciseRecord,Running}Service
                → repositories → Postgres
```

**Invariants of the seam:**
- **Mock mode is synchronous** (`initialData` set) so the first render equals the Phase-1 static return — preserved for the visual baselines and component tests. **Real mode has NO static fallback**: an empty backend surfaces as `null`/`[]` and every view ghost-guards (the "T0 clean slate" rule, `2026-06-11-train-write-clean-slate-design.md`). The full rationale is in the comment block at `frontend/src/data/train/trainHooks.ts:166-173`.
- **Mode is read inside hook bodies, never at module scope** (`frontend/src/data/_client/mode.ts`), so tests stub per-case with `vi.stubEnv`.
- Default mode is **mock** (`VITE_USE_MOCK !== 'false'`), but per `CLAUDE.md` the project runs FE in REAL mode by default in dev/test.
- Contract types are generated: `api/feature/train/train.yml` → merged `api/openapi.yml` → FE `frontend/src/data/_client/api.gen.ts` (re-exported as named types in `trainApi.ts`/`runningApi.ts`) and BE `io.mrkuhne.mezo.api.dto.*` + the `TrainApi` interface that `TrainController` implements.

**Real-mode mapping notes** (`trainHooks.ts`): `toWorkoutPlan` (`:51-94`) collapses the API response to the Phase-1 `WorkoutPlan` shape and sets `challenges: []` (live challenges are proactive-owned and sourced separately via `useChallenges` on `ActiveWorkoutPage`, not through `WorkoutPlan` — see §Active workout); since Progressive Overload Plan 1 (`mezo-5pfe`) it also null-coalesces each exercise's `progression` and the plan-level `overloadSummary` straight through from the wire (no shape change, just the two new optional fields); `deriveGymSchedule(meso, slots)` (`:100-116`) builds the gym week by joining the active meso's template days (WHAT) with the standalone weekly gym slots (WHEN) — `DAY_ORDER` index `==` `slot.dayOfWeek`, a gym day with no matching slot keeps `time: null`; `duration` stays `null` (no DB home, out of scope). The active-meso real fallback is `realActiveMeso ?? (mock ? activeMeso : null)` (`:565`).

---

## 4. Data model & API

All tables: UUID PKs (`gen_random_uuid()`), `created_by` ownership via `OwnedEntity`, soft delete via `@SQLDelete`/`@SQLRestriction`. Migrations live under `backend/src/main/resources/db/changelog/1.0.0/script/`. Generated DTOs live in `io.mrkuhne.mezo.api.dto.*`; never hand-write boundary DTOs. The API fragment is `api/feature/train/train.yml`.

### Mesocycles (planning)
- **Tables** (`202606111400_mezo-n5q_create_train.sql`; `mezo-7ot` adds `exercise.catalog_id` + plyo): `mesocycle`, `muscle_group_volume_log` (UNIQUE `(mesocycle_id, muscle)`, jsonb `source` = `ProvenanceEnvelope`), `workout_session` (template rows = `template_session_id IS NULL`), `exercise` (FK→session CASCADE, `catalog_id` FK→`exercise_catalog` SET NULL). **Template/run split (`mezo-meyc.1`, ADR [0027](../decisions/0027-mesocycle-template-run-history.md)):** `202608161200_mezo-meyc.1_create_meso_template.sql` adds **`meso_template`** — plan metadata + **`days jsonb`** (typed `MesoDayJson`/`GymExerciseJson` records) + **`volume_per_muscle jsonb`** (typed `VolumeBaselineJson`), no dates/status; `202608161210_mezo-meyc.1_mesocycle_template_link.sql` adds `mesocycle.template_id UUID NULL` (FK `fk_mesocycle_template_id_meso_template_id`, nullable — legacy runs stay `null`) and `mesocycle.closed_at timestamptz NULL`. **Run report (`mezo-meyc.2`):** `202608161600_mezo-meyc.2_create_mesocycle_report.sql` adds **`mesocycle_report`** — 1:1 via `mesocycle_id` FK CASCADE (`fk_mesocycle_report_mesocycle`) + `uq_mesocycle_report_mesocycle`, typed jsonb `report`/`context` (both nullable; **`context` is written by the companion's `MesoContextAssembler` since S3, `mezo-meyc.3`** — §5 *Meso AI review*), `self_eval text` (the owner's close-time note, takes over the dead `mesocycle.summary`'s role — the old column is untouched, nothing new writes it), `ai_eval text` + `ai_eval_status text` (`pending|ready|failed`, `ck_mesocycle_report_ai_status`, default `pending`) + `ai_eval_generated_at timestamptz` (all three written by the companion generator since S3). **Goal preset (`mezo-dq60`):** `202608242315_mezo-dq60_goal_preset.sql` adds `mesocycle.goal_preset text` and `meso_template.goal_preset text` — a machine-readable sibling to the free-text `goal` column (which stays the human-readable prose shown in the UI). The wizard's `Cél` step writes the chosen `GOAL_PRESETS[].id` into `goalPreset` on the template/run it creates (`MesocyclePlannerPage.tsx:196`); the template editor's `Cél` `<select>` (`MesoTemplateEditorPage.tsx`) lets it be changed later on an existing template. The migration backfills existing rows by matching their `goal` text against the exact `GOAL_PRESETS[].description` strings the wizard has always written (a point-in-time snapshot, same convention as the `mezo-gbo7` slug backfill); a row whose `goal` was hand-edited or predates the presets stays `goal_preset IS NULL` — never guessed. **The multi-preset picker this column originally served is gone (`mezo-d20.14`, hypertrophy-only wizard):** `addExerciseWithDefaults` (below) no longer branches on `goalPreset` at all — every new exercise gets the single hypertrophy scheme regardless — so `goalPreset` is now write-only from the FE's perspective: `TrainService.stampRun` still copies it straight through with no fallback and nothing server-side branches on it, but nothing reads it back either. The column and its `hypertrophy`-literal writes (`toUpsert` in `wizard/wizardState.ts`) are kept for schema/history continuity, not for any live behaviour. **Muscle priorities (`mezo-3m5m`, spec GD3/GD4):** `202608251200_mezo-3m5m_muscle_priorities.sql` adds `mesocycle.muscle_priorities jsonb` and `meso_template.muscle_priorities jsonb` — a **sparse** per-coarse-muscle map (`{"back": "emphasize", "glute": "maintain"}`; an absent key means **grow**, the default) with **no backfill**: existing rows land on `NULL`, which `PriorityTier.of` treats identically to an all-`grow` map, so live behaviour does not jump on deploy (GD3). Surfaced as `musclePriorities` on `MesocycleResponse`/`MesoTemplateResponse`/`MesoTemplateUpsertRequest` — a template save (wizard save, template editor) round-trips it through the existing full-upsert `PUT`/`POST` paths — and, for a mid-cycle change on a live run that shouldn't force a full day/template re-upsert, a dedicated `PUT /api/train/mesocycles/{id}/muscle-priorities` (`operationId updateMesocycleMusclePriorities`, body `MusclePrioritiesUpdateRequest`, `TrainService.updateMusclePriorities`) that replaces the map wholesale (an empty map is stored as `NULL`, matching the sparse convention) and returns the full assembled `MesocycleResponse`. **GD7:** the map is persisted immediately but only changes weekly-ramp behaviour at the **next** volume-progression rollover (§4 `#### Volume progression` below) — nothing about the current week is recomputed retroactively; the run editor's Fókusz picker used to surface this with a helper line ("A módosítás a következő heti görgetésnél lép életbe."). **Since mesocycle pages v2 (`mezo-d20.15`) there is no in-cycle Fókusz picker any more** — tiers are planning-time only (the wizard's Step 2) — so the endpoint's own GD7 lag is now moot for the FE: `PUT /api/train/mesocycles/{id}/muscle-priorities` stays live in the contract/backend but has **no caller at all**. That includes the template editor, which never used it: a template's tiers ride the full-upsert `PUT /api/train/meso-templates/{id}` like every other template field, and this endpoint only ever served a mid-cycle change on a live RUN. The FE keeps the whole chain — `trainApi.updateMusclePriorities` → the `updateMusclePriorities` mutation exposed on `useTrain()` — **wired but app-unused**, kept for contract parity (and covered by its own hook test), not because any screen calls it.
- **Entities:** `MesocycleEntity` (`phase_curve text[]` mapped as `List<String>` for Hibernate dirty-checking; `volume_recompute` jsonb = `VolumeRecomputeJson`), `MuscleGroupVolumeLogEntity` (first typed-jsonb persistence, `ProvenanceEnvelope`), `WorkoutSessionEntity`, `ExerciseEntity`. **`MesoTemplateEntity`** (`entity/MesoTemplateEntity.java`, `mezo-meyc.1`) is the plan-document twin — same soft-delete/`OwnedEntity` house rules, `days`/`volumePerMuscle` typed jsonb via `@JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)` (`entity/json/{MesoDayJson,GymExerciseJson,VolumeBaselineJson}.java`), `phaseCurve` the same `List<String>` dirty-checking trick as `MesocycleEntity`. **`MesocycleReportEntity`** (`entity/MesocycleReportEntity.java`, `mezo-meyc.2`) — the frozen report row, same soft-delete/`OwnedEntity` house rules; `report`/`context` are typed jsonb via `@JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)` onto the record shapes `MesoReportJson`/`MesoContextJson` (`entity/json/`), each mirroring its matching contract DTO field-for-field so freezing/reading is a straight MapStruct pass (`mapper/MesoReportMapper.java`).
- **Endpoints:** `GET /api/train/mesocycles` (batch-loads volume + days, stitches; response now carries `templateId`/`closedAt`/**`hasReport`** — one extra batched `MesocycleReportRepository` query over the page's run ids, `TrainService.java:110`, `mezo-meyc.2`), `POST .../{id}/activate`, **`POST .../{id}/close`** (`mezo-meyc.2`: optional body `{selfEval?}`; sets `status=archived` + `closed_at=now()` + computes/persists the frozen report **in the same transaction** — see the `MesocycleReportService` bullet below for the idempotency contract), `PUT .../{id}/days/{dayId}/exercises` (soft-delete + re-insert; unknown `catalogId` → 400, not FK 500), **`GET .../{id}/volume-arc`** (Progressive Overload Plan 2 Phase B, `mezo-hi9m` — read-only, not switch-gated; see `#### Volume progression` below). **`GET .../{id}/report`** (`mezo-meyc.2` — the full frozen report; 404 `TRAIN_MESO_REPORT_NOT_FOUND` when the run has none yet) and **`POST .../{id}/report/regenerate`** (**202**; archived-only, else 409 `TRAIN_MESO_NOT_CLOSED`; upserts the report and resets the AI half (narrative + `context`) to `pending` — serves both a stale-report refresh and the legacy-backfill „Riport generálása" path for pre-S2 archived runs). **`POST /api/train/mesocycles` (direct run create) is REMOVED from the contract (`mezo-meyc.1`)** — the wizard now saves a template and starts it; `TrainService`'s create method is gone, `MesocycleCreateRequest` deleted. Its replacement, all served by `MesoTemplateService` (`service/MesoTemplateService.java`) via `TrainController`: `GET /api/train/meso-templates` (owner's templates, created-date ascending, each with `runCount`), `POST /api/train/meso-templates` (create), `PUT .../{id}` (full-replace update — every exercise gets a fresh server id on every save, ids are NOT stable across writes), `DELETE .../{id}` (soft delete; runs already started from it are untouched), **`POST .../{id}/start` `{startDate, status: active|planned}`** (stamps a full run via the extracted `TrainService.stampRun` — metadata copied, `endDate`/`currentWeek`/`orderIndex` server-computed exactly as the old create did, `days` materialized into `workout_session`+`exercise` rows, unknown `catalogId` → 400 as before, and — only for an active start — `volume_per_muscle` seeded into `muscle_group_volume_log`; starting as active archives other actives, the existing single-active invariant), **`POST /api/train/mesocycles/{id}/rerun`** (`{templateId}` response — a run started from a template just returns its `templateId`; a legacy run with none gets one **materialized** from its own `workout_session`/`exercise`/`muscle_group_volume_log` rows on first call — including a fallback baseline name **"Örökölt kiindulás"** when a volume-log row carries no provenance name — then links it back onto the run so a second rerun of the same run is a plain lookup). Ownership: a missing/foreign template is a 404 with the new `TRAIN_MESO_TEMPLATE_NOT_FOUND` `SystemMessage`. **`POST /api/train/meso-plans/generate`** (`operationId generateMesoPlan`, mesocycle wizard redesign) — the wizard's Hypertrophy plan generator; nothing is persisted, the response's `template` is meant to be posted straight back to `POST /api/train/meso-templates` unchanged. See `#### Plan generator` below.
- **DTOs:** `MesocycleResponse` (gained `templateId?`/`closedAt?`/**`hasReport`**), `MesoDay`/`MesoDayInput`, `GymExercise`/`GymExerciseInput`, `VolumeProfile`/`VolumeSource`/`VolumeBaseline`/`VolumeAdjustment`/`VolumeRecompute`/`VolumeChange`/`VolumeUserOverride`, **`MesocycleVolumeArcResponse`/`MuscleVolumeArc`/`VolumeArcWeek`** (the volume-arc endpoint's response shape, below — also **reused verbatim** as the report's `volume` block). New template family (`mezo-meyc.1`): `MesoTemplateResponse` (adds `runCount`), `MesoTemplateUpsertRequest`, `MesoTemplateStartRequest` (`startDate` + `status: active|planned`), `MesoRerunResponse` (`{templateId}`). New report family (`mezo-meyc.2`): `MesocycleCloseRequest {selfEval?}`, **`MesocycleReportResponse`** (identity fields read live off `MesocycleEntity` — `mesocycleId`/`templateId`/`title`/dates/`weeks`/`closedAt` — plus the frozen `adherence: MesoReportAdherence`, `strength: MesoStrengthDelta[]`, `records: MesoReportRecords` (nested `MesoRecordHighlight[]`), the reused `volume: MesocycleVolumeArcResponse`, `context: MesoContext?` (nested `MesoContextWeek[]` + `MesoContextTotals`, populated since S3 — null only for a report whose context has not been assembled yet), and `selfEval`/`aiEval`/`aiEvalStatus`/`aiEvalGeneratedAt`/**`aiEvalEnabled`** — the last one now the presence of the `MesoReviewGate` bean, i.e. the `mezo.feature.meso-review.enabled` switch).
- **`volumePerMuscle`/`volumeRecompute` now have a real write path** (Progressive Overload Plan 2 Phase A, `mezo-hi9m`, PR #80): `VolumeProgressionService.rolloverIfDue` (see `#### Volume progression` below) writes `MuscleGroupVolumeLogEntity.currentSets` + a `volumeRecompute` audit **live, per calendar week**, behind `mezo.feature.volume-progression.enabled` (default **on**) — it is no longer seed-only. **Since `mezo-xlmp` the rows themselves have a real birth path too:** a wizard create-as-active / activate seeds them from the fixed `mezo.volume.baselines` RP table (see `#### Volume progression` below), so a user-created meso's weekly ramp, DA6 effective-set distribution and volume-arc are live without demo fixtures. The mock fixtures (`data/train/train.ts`) and the `demofixtures` seed (`TrainSeedData`) still carry richer **narrative prose** in their provenance than the real engine writes (the engine's per-week `changes[]` is a terse `{muscle, change, reason, deload}` list, not free text) — that gap is real and worth keeping in mind. The provenance envelope round-trips through the DB (proven by `ProvenanceRoundTripIT`).
- **`MesocycleReportService`** (`service/MesocycleReportService.java`, `mezo-meyc.2`, spec §2) computes the report ONCE, inside the close transaction, and never again drifts even if the data underneath later changes. **Adherence:** non-empty template days (`countNonEmptyTemplateDays`, distinct `workout_session`/`exercise` join) × weeks elapsed (`MesoWeeks.weekOf(startDate, windowEnd, weeks)`) vs the run's meso-origin instances actually `completed` in `[startDate, closedAt]` (`WorkoutSessionRepository.findCompletedMesoInstancesInWindow`). **Volume:** `VolumeArcService.arc` frozen verbatim, via a package-visible 3-arg overload (`VolumeArcService.java:65`, `arc(createdBy, mesoId, effectiveCurrentWeek)`) that substitutes `max(currentWeek, weeksElapsed)` for the stored (possibly stale — it only advances on the weekly rollover) `currentWeek`, so the frozen arc can never disagree with adherence about which week was reached; the public 2-arg `arc(createdBy, mesoId)` is unchanged. **Strength:** per exercise IDENTITY (catalog id else exact name, the `ExerciseHistoryResolver`/`MedalService` idiom, resolved over `findIdentityRowsIncludingDeleted` so a mid-run day-edit's soft-deleted rows still resolve history) over **non-skipped working sets only**, bucketed into meso-weeks, first-vs-last-bucket top set compared by e1RM via the shared `OneRepMax.estimate` (Epley `w×(30+reps)/30`, `feature/train/service/OneRepMax.java`, formula only — this call site doesn't filter through `OneRepMax.eligible`) — an identity needs **≥2 distinct trained weeks** to earn an entry; `deltaKg` is the top-set LOAD difference (null if either end was weightless), `deltaPct` is the e1RM-based relative gain, so "same weight, more reps" reads as real progress instead of a flat 0 kg. **A lift trained exclusively above `REP_CAP` (12) reps in a bucket now carries no e1RM for that bucket** (`OneRepMax.estimate` returns null past the cap, same as everywhere else) — the top-set LOAD (`deltaKg`) still compares fine, but `deltaPct` and the e1RM figure go missing for that identity/bucket; what that should mean for **ranking** (does the identity still earn its progress entry on load alone, or drop out entirely) is an open owner question, tracked as `mezo-aw7yb`. **Records:** replays the existing `MedalService.forSessions` over the run's instance ids (medal count + top 5 by date) — nothing is stored, there is no medal table, this is why the block has to be frozen here at all. `closeWindowEnd` falls back to `endDate` when `closedAt` is null (the legacy-backfill case). **`aiEvalEnabled` (S3, `mezo-meyc.3`)** now reports the presence of the **`MesoReviewGate`** marker bean (`feature/train/MesoReviewGate.java`, `@ConditionalOnProperty` on `mezo.feature.meso-review.enabled`, declared `true` in `application.yml`), read via `ObjectProvider<MesoReviewGate>` rather than a direct injection (the bean is conditional, so a plain field would fail to wire when the switch is off) — off ⇒ the FE hides the AI section and skips polling instead of waiting on a `pending` that will never resolve. The gate deliberately lives in `feature.train`, not `feature.companion`: `companion` already depends on `train`, so declaring it there and consuming it here would close a brand-new `train↔companion` package cycle (`ArchitectureTest.feature_slices_are_cycle_free`). Both entry points that reach `computeAndStore` — `TrainService.closeMesocycle`'s REAL-close branch (never an idempotent re-close, never the fill-if-null self-eval branch) and `regenerate` (every accepted call) — publish **`MesocycleClosed(userId, mesocycleId)`** (`feature/train/MesocycleClosed.java`) in the same transaction right after the row is persisted; the companion consumes it AFTER_COMMIT and writes `context` + `ai_eval` back (see §5 *Train ↔ Companion*). `computeAndStore` UPSERTs onto the run's single `mesocycle_report` row, **clears the ENTIRE AI half — `ai_eval`/`ai_eval_generated_at`/`ai_eval_status='pending'` AND `context`** (a lifestyle context computed against the old numbers/window must not survive beside fresh ones; with the meso-review switch off nothing would ever overwrite it, so the page would show last-window buckets next to this-window results — the companion's generator re-populates it from `pending`), and never touches `selfEval`; `TrainService.closeMesocycle` (`:256`) is the sole writer of `closed_at`/`status` and is fully IDEMPOTENT — re-closing an already-archived run neither recomputes the report nor overwrites an existing self-eval; the one thing a second close still does is **fill** a still-null self-eval (the auto-archived-by-starting-the-next-run case never otherwise gets a close call with a note).
- **FE (`mezo-meyc.2`):** `useMesoReport(id)` (`data/train/mesoReportHooks.ts`, exported `mesoReportQueryKey`) — dual-mode read that resolves a real-mode 404 to `notFound` rather than throwing (`{report, pending, notFound, error, refetch, regenerate, regenerating}`); `useTrain().closeMesocycle(id, selfEval?, opts?)` posts the extended close body. `apiFetch` (`data/_client/api.ts:35-41`) now tolerates an empty response body (reads text first, only `JSON.parse`s when non-empty) since the 202 regenerate response carries none and the old unconditional `res.json()` threw a `SyntaxError` on it. `MesoCloseSheet`/`MesoReportPage` are covered in §2 above (`MuscleArcSwitch` is deleted, §9). Mock parity: `mockClose` (`trainHooks.ts:294`) flips the mock run to `archived` and seeds an — honestly empty, since mock has no logged history to freeze — report at the report query key, deliberately kept at `aiEvalEnabled: false`/`context: null` even after S3 (a freshly closed demo run has nothing to evaluate yet, and flipping it to `true` would poll forever offline); `mesoReportMock` (`data/train/train.ts`) is the one pre-seeded report fixture with real derived numbers (`meso-rec-03`), its frozen arc built from the SAME `mockMuscleArc` helper the live mock volume arc uses so the two fixtures can never drift apart, and since `mezo-meyc.3` it also carries a `ready` `aiEval` (4 Hungarian paragraphs) plus a full 8-week `context` (a couple of deliberate null holes — missing sleep/kcal/run weeks — to exercise the report page's `–` cells) so the S3 demo shows both new blocks by default; `mockRegenerate` (`mesoReportHooks.ts:26`) also writes a report into the cache — a demo-only quirk is that it can "generate" one for a still-active run, since mock mode has no archived-only guard. The MSW `mesoReportFixture` (`test/msw/handlers.ts`) mirrors the same S3 shape (`ready` + a small 2-week `context`) but with its OWN short literal values rather than importing `mesoReportMock` — `data/train/train.ts` is one of the largest mock modules in the app, and `handlers.ts` is transitively imported by nearly every test file, so pulling it in measurably raised full-suite CPU load and surfaced as an unrelated flake (`MesoCloseSheet.test.tsx`) under full local parallelism.
- **FE (`mezo-meyc.4`) — the compare composition is client-side only.** No endpoint, no new hook: `MesoComparePage` calls `useMesoReport` twice and the three pure helpers in `features/train/logic/mesoCompare.ts` do the lining up. `alignVolumeWeeks(a, b)` — muscles are the **union** of both frozen arcs (a's order first, then b-only ones, so the pill row never reshuffles), weeks aligned by **week number** (`W1..max`), every cell nullable so the shorter run's tail is `–` and a null `actual` stays null (an unlogged week is not zero volume); a report with `volume: null` contributes nothing, both null ⇒ empty ⇒ the block disappears. `sharedStrengthDeltas(a, b)` — the heart of the view: an exercise is the same identity when **both** sides carry a `catalogId` and they match, else on an **exact** name match (a fuzzy match would put two different lifts on one row); only shared identities survive, sorted by the **larger |deltaPct| descending** (magnitude, so a big regression ranks as high as a big gain) with pct-less lifts last — note the ordering key (magnitude) and the sage highlight (**higher signed** pct) answer two different questions. `contextDiff(a, b)` — the six run-level totals (alvás h / kcal / energia / stressz / súlyváltozás kg / sport perc) only; a metric NEITHER run measured is dropped, one only ONE measured stays (`7,4 h` vs `–` is itself informative), and a null `context` contributes nulls, never zeros. Domain-type touch: `Mesocycle` gained the optional **`hasReport`** the contract already served (`data/types.ts`) — `toMesocycle` spread it through all along, it just was not typed — and mock `mockClose` now flips it to `true` alongside the status, since it seeds a report in the same breath. Mock parity needed a SECOND report: `mesoReportHyp03Mock` (`data/train/train.ts`) + the archived run `meso-hyp-03`, deliberately unlike `meso-rec-03` (6 vs 8 weeks, worse adherence, higher-volume arc with `triceps` but no `biceps`/`ham`, worse lifestyle averages, `energyAvg: null` in the totals to exercise the one-sided `–`) and sharing exactly **three** of its five exercises, so both the overlap and the non-overlap paths are live in the offline app; `mesoReportHooks`' `mockReportFor` resolves against a small `MOCK_REPORTS` table now instead of one id. The MSW handlers were left alone on purpose — see the CPU-flake note above.

### Workout execution
- **Tables** (`202606120900_mezo-tod_t2_workout_execution.sql`): adds `workout_session.template_session_id` (self-FK SET NULL), `exercise_set.workout_session_id` (FK→instance CASCADE), renames `voice_note`→`note`, creates `exercise_feedback` (UNIQUE `(workout_session_id, exercise_id)`; CHECKs pump 1-4 / joint_pain 1-3 / workload 1-3). `202606171200_mezo-an1_exercise_set_skipped.sql` adds `exercise_set.skipped` (F3 whole-exercise skip marker). `202606171210_mezo-an1_exercise_note.sql` adds `exercise.note` (F4 durable per-exercise note). **`202607072000_mezo-dhdr_prescribed_sets.sql` turns the template `exercise` row into a *recipe*** (hypertrophy drive): it replaces the single `sets`/`target_reps` pair with `warmup_sets` (default 0), `working_sets`, `rep_min`, `rep_max`, and adds a nullable `anchor_weight_kg NUMERIC(6,2)` (first-session load seed); `target_rir` is kept. Same migration adds `exercise_set.kind` (`warmup`|`working`, default `working`, `ck_exercise_set_kind` CHECK) so a logged set records which half of the recipe it belongs to.
- **Entities:** `ExerciseSetEntity` (`weight_kg NUMERIC(6,2)`, `side` CHECK L|B|R, `done_at`, `skipped`, **`kind` default `working`** — `ExerciseSetEntity.java:72`), `ExerciseEntity` (the template exercise — carries the durable `note` **and the recipe fields `warmupSets`/`workingSets`/`repMin`/`repMax`/`targetRir`/`anchorWeightKg`**, `ExerciseEntity.java:49-70`), `ExerciseFeedbackEntity`.
- **Endpoints:** `GET /api/train/workouts/today` (workout context — **since the cross-day start (`mezo-p7rp`) it takes an optional `?templateSessionId=` and day resolution is `open instance > param > today's weekday label`**: the user's open instance always wins (a deep link to another day while one runs resumes the running one), the param pins any owned template day (else 404), and only the param-less no-open case falls back to the weekday-label match; **`completedWorkout` is now the resolved day's most recent completed instance of the current Mon–Sun week**, not just today's — a day done any day this week reviews instead of restarting (D5). **Each `TodayExercise` also carries `prescribedSets: PrescribedSet[]` + a `rationale` string when `mezo.feature.hypertrophy-drive.enabled` is on, null when off** so the FE falls back to ad-hoc logging; **since Progressive Overload Plan 1 (`mezo-5pfe`) each `TodayExercise` additionally carries a structured `progression: ProgressionSignal` (null on a first session / anchor-only exercise with no real history — the RIR-aware engine has nothing to compare against yet), and `WorkoutTodayResponse` carries a day-level `overloadSummary` (counts of exercises moving via each lever, null when the switch is off)**; see the recommendation-engine subsection below), **`GET /api/train/workouts?from&to`** (date-range summary list — see below; **summaries carry `templateSessionId`** since `mezo-p7rp` so the FE can map template day → completed instance this week), **`GET /api/train/workouts/{id}`** (single-instance detail for the done-day review — see the done-state subsection below), `POST /api/train/workouts` (resumes the open instance, never duplicates; **cross-day guards `mezo-p7rp`:** another day's workout open → **409 `TRAIN_WORKOUT_OPEN_ELSEWHERE`** (D6), template day already completed this Mon–Sun week → **409 `TRAIN_DAY_DONE_THIS_WEEK`** (D5) — the same-template resume branch runs BEFORE the guards so resuming never conflicts), `POST .../{id}/sets`, **`PUT /api/train/workouts/{id}/sets/{setId}`** (`mezo-l3on` — egy logolt szett teljesítmény-mezőinek felülírása;
