from the now-deleted `WeeklyDayRow.test.tsx`/`LoadTiles.test.tsx`/`ZoneMiniGrid.test.tsx` by the T12 sweep), `weeklyLoad.test.ts`, `trainHooks.test.tsx`, `trainHooks.deriveGym.test.ts` (the gym-slot join), `trainData.test.tsx`, `train.emptyStates.test.tsx` (the ghost-guards), `train.nav.test.tsx`, `runningDraft.test.ts`, `runningHooks.test.ts`, `runningAgenda.test.ts`, and the morning-training nudge (`mezo-67rb`): `logic/morningWindow.test.ts` (window math, `time > end` offenders, content-key snooze) + `components/MorningTrainingCard.test.tsx` + two new `TrainTodayPage.test.tsx` cases (the card mounts on an offending slot and snoozes/applies).
  ```bash
  cd frontend
  pnpm test                          # REAL mode
  VITE_USE_MOCK=true pnpm test       # MOCK mode — both must be green
  pnpm build
  ```
- **Visual baselines (S8, `mezo-mifi`):** `train` (the pre-redirect `/train` route, now retired with the hub — see [`_platform-design-system.md`](_platform-design-system.md) §8 for which goldens survived the visual-suite retirement), `train-heti`, `train-gym` (the alias, so it renders Heti) and `train-session` are among the self-baselined goldens in `frontend/tests/visual/visual.spec.ts` (both themes; `pnpm test:visual`) — harness mechanics in [_platform-design-system.md](_platform-design-system.md) §8. **`pnpm build` is its own gate**, and `shared/ui/mozaik/prototypeCssStructure.test.ts` (added by the F8 cleanup, `mezo-d20.9.1`) is a CSS-aware structural scanner over `styles/prototype.css` — balanced braces, no conflict markers, terminated comments with no stray comment-close, no runaway string, each failure naming the line. It exists because a multi-way union merge swallowed a `@media (prefers-reduced-motion)` closing brace four times during the redesign, and once a comment body containing `*/` terminated its own comment and broke the build with an unrelated-looking "Unterminated string" — vitest caught none of it.
- **Backend (integration-first, `@SpringBootTest` + Postgres, AssertJ, populators — `docs/references/testing_standards.md`, `integration_test_framework.md`):** service ITs `TrainServiceIT`, `WorkoutServiceIT`, `SportServiceIT`, `ExerciseRecordServiceIT`, `ExerciseCatalogLoaderIT`, `ProvenanceRoundTripIT` (the typed-jsonb risk item), `TrainSeedDataIT`, `SetRecommendationServiceIT` (the prescription engine over real history, extended for RIR/deload in `mezo-5pfe`), `WorkoutTodayProgressionIT` (RIR-aware lever matrix + deload + `overloadSummary` over `getToday`, `mezo-5pfe`); a **pure unit test** `ProgressionDeciderTest` (`feature/train/service/`, no Spring — every lever branch incl. grind-hold, null-RIR neutrality, deload); HTTP contract ITs `TrainContractIT`, `WorkoutContractIT`, `SportContractIT`, `GymScheduleContractIT` (gym-schedule GET/PUT round-trip, replace-all, ownership isolation, 401), `RunningContractIT`, `ExerciseCatalogContractIT`, `ExerciseCatalogPermissionIT` (the S5 USER/OWNER matrix + per-viewer flags, via `registerUser`), `ExerciseCatalogSlugRaceIT` (two threads, one name), `ExerciseCatalogSoftDeleteIT`, `ExerciseRecordContractIT`. Populators: `support/populator/TrainPopulator.java`, `RunningPopulator.java`. **Plan generator (mesocycle wizard redesign, §4 `#### Plan generator`):** pure unit tests `MesoPlanSkeletonTest` (split-per-day-count, ≥2×/week by construction, tier week-1/ceiling wiring, phase curve), `PriorityTierTest` (`service/`, no Spring — tier normalization, week-1-start and ceiling math per tier), `MesoPlanFillerTest` (compound-first/stim-desc ordering, 1-vs-2-exercise threshold, rotation on the second weekly occurrence, empty-pool never fabricates), `MesoPlanMergerTest` (id/group/day validation drops a bad pick and falls back to the deterministic fill, set renormalization to the frame, the `MesoPlanFiller`-parity exercise-count cap, `MergeResult.appliedPicks`); `MesoPlanPropertiesBindingIT` (`config/`, `mezo.meso-plan.*` binds); `MesoStartTierSeedIT` (the tier-aware MEV+2 seed start, above); `MesoPlanGenerateContractIT` (the AI switch off via `@TestPropertySource`, deterministic-only response shape); `MesoPlanGenerateAiIT` (`companion-fake` profile — the fake's default `{"days":[]}` answer changes nothing so `llmUsed` stays false and the deterministic rationale is kept, a scripted `[fake-meso-plan:{…}]` sentinel planted in `goalText` drives an exact accepted pick and marks `llmUsed` true, `[fake-fail]` falls back to the deterministic fill).
  ```bash
  cd backend
  docker compose up -d
  ./mvnw clean test                  # ITs against fixed mezo_test DB (always use clean)
  ```

---

## 9. Decisions, gotchas & deferred

**Key decisions** (see linked specs): mock parity vs clean-slate real mode (`2026-06-11-train-write-clean-slate-design.md`); running as a mesocycle-mirroring slice with typed-jsonb plan structure (`2026-06-14-train-running-slice-design.md`); on-the-fly records aggregation (no materialized table) over soft-deleted rows (`2026-06-12-exercise-records-design.md`); catalog as master content loaded in every profile (`2026-06-12-exercise-catalog-design.md`).

**Design 2.0 decisions** ([ADR 0032](../decisions/0032-five-tab-ia-dissolved-section-shells.md) for the IA, [ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md) for the visual language — the latter supersedes ADR 0026): the nine-tab shell dissolved into one hub + full-page siblings; a tile opens a *page*, never a nested shell; clay SVG sprites replaced emoji; the entrance choreography plays once and then the page is still. **The redesign was recompose, not reinvent** — the axiom that let seven pages be re-faced in one wave without a single contract change.

**Gotchas:**
- **`WorkoutWindowQueryService.windowsFor` is RANGED — call the ranged overload from any multi-day read (`mezo-jcpt.6`).** `DayScoreService.rhythmFreeInputs` called the single-date overload once per date across its 14-day window (7 rendered + 7 rhythm-prior), repeating the user-global gym-slot and running-block queries every time — **~90 statements for one week's read**. `windowsFor(userId, from, to)` batches every underlying query once for the whole range (gym slots, sport slots, the active running block user-globally; done gym instances, active meso sessions, sport events, sport sessions and slot skips each through their existing range finder) and groups in memory per date; the single-date overload now **delegates** to it, which also fixed a sort-order parity slip between the two paths. Same days, same windows, same done signal. `DayScoreServiceWindowFetchCountIT` pins the ranged call count to 1 and the per-date overload to 0 for a week's `scores()` read — reverting to the per-date loop turns it red — and a `WorkoutWindowQueryServiceIT` parity test asserts the ranged result equals per-day calls across a week mixing every window kind.
- **A skipped sport slot must be honoured on the FE too (`mezo-cq06`).** `sport_slot_skip` is a backend concept, but five date-specific FE read paths composed the day themselves and kept rendering a skipped slot's sport block while the backend already called the day „not a training day": `data/fuel/fuelWeekHooks.ts`, `data/fuel/stackDayHooks.ts` + `timelineHooks.ts`, `data/notification/notificationScheduleWriter.ts`, `data/ritual/recapHooks.ts`, `data/today/todayHooks.ts`, `features/fuel/logic/buildProtocol.ts`, `features/today/logic/useDayOrbFill.ts` and `features/train/logic/weekAgenda.ts`. Any NEW date-specific FE read that derives „is this a training day" must filter skips the same way — there is no endpoint that does it for you.
- **The Train parity P1 sweep emptied the prep drawer (`mezo-e1ii9`, Task 7).** Every module below was grep-verified on the FINAL tree (an import-specifier scan over every module in the domain, plus a class-by-class scan of every `className` the P1 commits deleted) before deletion. **Deleted with their tests:** `components/PrepExerciseTile.tsx` (the mosaic's Gyakorlatok row), `components/WeekZoneCard.tsx` (the prep „Heti zóna-kontextus" card), `components/ZoneTrack.tsx` (WeekZoneCard's last importer went with it — the Titanium Terhelés face reads `weekZoneRows` directly and draws its own bars) and `logic/prepBriefing.ts` (every export — `prepStats`/`pseudoDayFromPlan`/`prepForecast`/`oneRmByIdentity`/`identityKeyOf`/`startWeightOf` — had a prep surface as its only production caller). **Narrowed:** `logic/weekZone.ts` lost `selectPrepRows` + `prepSegments`; `weekZoneRows` (three live pages), `selectGymRows`/`gymSegments` and the `ZoneSegment` type stay — the two GYM helpers were already test-only BEFORE this slice, and a pre-existing orphan is not this slice's to judge. **CSS deleted:** the `.tp-*` prep-mosaic family (hero/title/cta/anchor/badge/niggle, `mezo-d20.3.8`), the `.gx-*` prep exercise-tile family, and the `.cer-chal*` ceremony challenge-outcome rows (the prototype's own ceremony carries no challenge block, so nothing regresses toward it). `.mz-panel` stays — six live hub pages wear it. **Named deaths, recorded on purpose:** the prep niggle page's `Értem · jó így` acknowledgement (a prep gesture with nothing left to confirm, §2); the ceremony's küldetés rows (their remaining home is the review page's `Kihívások` strip) and its mock-mode streak/level-up toast (the ceremony IS the reward surface and owns the screen alone); and the pre-workout XP *forecast* (answered honestly by the ceremony's real `+XP` instead). **Two more modules died in the same slice — corrected in the fix wave, `mezo-e1ii9`, which found §10 still listing both as live:** `logic/warmupProtocol.ts` (`WarmupRow`/`WARMUP_ROWS`, the session-level 3-block warm-up protocol's data) is **DELETED** — the sentence above that called it "still live, deliberately" described a tree that no longer existed after `de303e92d`. Its capability is a RECORDED DEATH, not a rehoming: the cards' amber B-rows are a per-exercise warm-up, not the session-level 3-block protocol the Bemelegítés page showed, so the protocol itself is simply gone from the app. And `logic/challengeOutcome.ts` (the FE pre-finish preview of the backend `ChallengeOutcomeEvaluator`) is **DELETED** with the pre-finish `closing` screen it fed — the challenge outcomes the server computes are unaffected and still land post-finish; only the client-side guess before the POST is gone.
- **Three prep components are dead but not deleted (`mezo-d20.3.8` leftovers).** `components/PrepHero.tsx`, `components/PrepExerciseCard.tsx` and `components/ChallengesCarousel.tsx` have **no production import** — the prep mosaic replaced them with the `pages/prep/*` tile pages + `PrepExerciseTile`, and `mezo-e1ii9` then deleted that whole generation too (the bullet above), so these three are now the residue of a residue. Their test files still exist and still pass, which is exactly why the F8 sweep (which deleted `GymDayCard`/`GymStat`/`PhaseDots`, all three test-free) did not catch them. `components/LastWeekStat.tsx` is a fourth, older instance of the same thing. Deleting them is a cleanup follow-up, not a behavior change — but do not read them as documentation of the live screen.
- **The Train parity P2 catalogue rebuild took one sheet with it (`mezo-lf3cv`, Tasks 4–5).** **Deleted with its test:** `sheets/ExerciseRecordSheet.tsx` — the pre-Titanium per-exercise **REKORDOK** modal a record card used to open (records + `repRecords` list + the demo still + the `▶ Demo` chip). `pages/ExerciseStoryPage.tsx` is its replacement — a far richer story, but **NOT a superset: three of the sheet's blocks have no successor anywhere in the app.** (a) the **demo still** (§2 `Gyakorlatok`: the story has no image section, and `ExercisePickerSheet` is now the FE's only still surface); (b) the **`recentTopSets` sparkline** („Utolsó N alkalom · top szett"); and (c) the **`totalSets · totalReps` pair** („Szett · rep ALL-TIME") — of which `totalReps` survives (the hero foot states it for a bodyweight row, where volume would be a fake 0) while **`totalSets` now renders NOWHERE in the app**. The prototype's story has no section for (b) or (c) — the strength curve is the story it tells about recent sessions, and the hero's foot states volume rather than a set tally — so these are **deliberate deaths, declared** (matrix §21 row 26), not oversights. **The wire still carries both fields** on every record, and `firstSeenDate` still reads `recentTopSets` for its dates (a date min, not a rendered sparkline), so a later slice can surface them with no server work. It had **no other caller** — grep-verified on the final tree — and the top-5 `.excat*` catalogue markup it belonged to went with the Task 4 rewrite. **No CAPABILITY was orphaned:** the three the old shell owned (per-exercise edit/delete, the demo-video authoring sheet, the `Saját`/`Közös · {név}` stamps) all have a home on the story page, which is why the rebuild is a rewrite and not a feature death. **One primitive was** (Task 6's sweep, an import-specifier scan of the final tree, not a word grep): **`shared/ui/PageTitle.tsx` is DELETED with its case in `shared/ui/text.test.tsx`** — the pre-Titanium `ExercisesPage` head was the app's last caller (every other page had already moved to a Titanium poster), and its `.page-title` rule went with it. So did the whole `.excat*` CSS family **except `.excat-tag`**, which the Futás session card and the running page still stamp descriptors with, and `.gy-curve-will` / `.gy-curve-cap .is-will` — Task 3 ported the prototype's dashed projection, but production deliberately does not draw it (§2 `Gyakorlatok`), so the rules had no renderer; the two tests asserting the class is ABSENT stay as the guard. Pre-existing orphans (`MESOCYCLE_PHASE_COLORS`, `phaseBarHeight`, `workoutDetail*Mock`, `MiniStat`, `SportStat`, …) were left alone — they were already orphaned on `main` and belong to `mezo-3iuoe`, not to this branch.
- **The Train Titanium T10 sweep closed the T9 residue (`mezo-88iwa.11`).** Each item was grep-verified against the tree before deletion. **Components deleted** (no consumer left once the library pages were refaced): `components/ActiveMesoCard.tsx` (superseded by `MesoTervPage`'s `.pl-poster` in T9), `components/PlannedMesoCard.tsx` and `components/ArchivedMesoCard.tsx` (+test) — the landing and the closed-run list draw `.pl-lib-card` rows themselves now — and `components/MesoTemplateCard.tsx` (+test) with `logic/templatePoster.ts` (+test), replaced by the `Sablonjaid` list card + the story page. The four stale prose mentions that still named `ActiveMesoCard` went with them. **CSS deleted:** the whole `.tpl-*` template-poster family and its `--tpl-ink-*` tokens (light + dark); the dead `.mz-wtile`/`.mz-wnums`/`.mz-wspark*`/`.mz-wstat` week-mosaic block T9 orphaned, with the `.mz-arr`/`.mz-arclbl` strays inside it (the live `.mz-arr-up`/`-dn`/`-eq` rules are a different selector and stayed); the ported-but-never-consumed `.pl-dhero-constel`, `.pl-ex-move` and `.pl-add`; and the inert `.pl-lib .pl-day-facts*` pair (`.pl-lib-card .pl-day-facts` already does that job). **Kept, with evidence:** `.pl-poster-foot` (five Titanium pages consume it), and `.pl-eyebrow`/`.pl-whero`, which were never rules at all — only prose recording why the house `.tr-eyebrow` is used instead. Two behavioural fixes rode along: `MesoDayPage`'s per-muscle bar had its 10-set scale and `80%` cap pin written inline and now derives both from `SESSION_MUSCLE_CAP` (`logic/setBudget.ts`'s `SESSION_BAR_MAX_SETS`/`SESSION_CAP_PIN_PCT`/`sessionBarPct`), and `MesoMusclePage`'s two gauge captions no longer overlap mid-scale — `nudgeFor` only clamps at the track's ENDS, so `spreadCaptions` opens a merged-geometry pair to a minimum gap around its own midpoint first (the marks keep their true positions). Also fixed: `.pl-lib .pl-lib-new` stopped being inert once three pages gained the `.pl-lib` ancestor and, at equal specificity, silently ate `.pl-lib-new.is-start` — it now sits above the modifiers.
- **`GymPage` renders, it does not redirect.** `/train/gym` returns `<TrainWeekPage />` directly, so the URL stays `/train/gym` while the content is Heti's — deliberate, so bookmarks and the three in-app callers are not bounced through a history entry. Anything keying off `location.pathname` (the `train-gym` visual golden, for one) sees the Gym path with Heti's content.
- **Mock parity contract is the single most important rule:** mock returns byte-identical Phase-1 statics (synchronous `initialData`); real has no fallback → views MUST ghost-guard.
- **`useTrain` mocks no-op; `useRunning` mocks emulate** (`setQueryData`). Asymmetric on purpose.
- **Real-mode run-block create inserts synchronously into the cache** (`runningHooks.ts:52-63`, **mezo-11m**): on a `create` success the new block is written into `['running','blocks']` directly (no async invalidate that would race the read-after-write), so navigating straight to the builder no longer crashes on an `undefined` structure. Updates + mock mode still invalidate. The builder also guards `!draft.structure` with a `Betöltés…` placeholder (`RunningBlockBuilderPage.tsx:97-99`).
- **Run-builder edits auto-save (no Save button):** a 600 ms debounce (`RunningBlockBuilderPage.tsx:65-69`) plus a flush-on-back (`backToList`, `:71-74`); `dirty` is a `JSON.stringify` diff of the draft vs the loaded block (`:57-60`). The re-seed effect keys on `block?.id` only so a refetch never clobbers in-progress edits.
- **Template vs instance** both live in `workout_session`: template = `template_session_id IS NULL`; instance carries date/status/back-link. `listMesocycles`/`getToday` filter to templates; `logSet`/`finish` operate on instances.
- **`meso_template` (the plan) vs `mesocycle` (a run) is a DIFFERENT, higher-level split** (`mezo-meyc.1`, ADR [0027](../decisions/0027-mesocycle-template-run-history.md)) — don't conflate the two: a template's `days` is a jsonb plan document with no `workout_session` rows at all; only `start`ing it stamps real `workout_session`/`exercise` rows for a run. **Known deferred edges, accepted for S1:** `POST .../rerun` on a run whose materialized template was later soft-deleted still returns that (now-invisible) `templateId`, and the follow-up `start` call 404s (`TRAIN_MESO_TEMPLATE_NOT_FOUND`) — there is no re-materialize-on-404 retry yet; a full template `PUT` regenerates every exercise's server id, so a client must not assume ids are stable across saves. **Close now freezes a report (`mezo-meyc.2`, superseding the S1 bare-flip state above):** see the `MesocycleReportService` bullet in §4 for the computation + idempotency contract. **S3 (`mezo-meyc.3`) filled in the AI half:** the `mezo.feature.meso-review.enabled` switch (`MesoReviewGate`), the `MesocycleClosed` event, and the companion's listener/assembler/generator that write `context` + `ai_eval` back (§5 *Meso AI review*, [`companion.md`](companion.md) §5.6). **Known deferred edges, accepted for S3:** a `failed` narrative is never retried automatically — the only re-trigger is `POST .../report/regenerate` (which resets the status to `pending` and republishes the event), so a transient provider outage needs one manual tap; the generator is idempotent purely **by status**, so two closes racing the same run could in principle both pass the `pending` check and burn two smart-tier calls (single-user app, one close per run — not worth a DB lock); the LLM sees the context field names as-is, so the **metric legend** prepended to the payload ([`companion.md`](companion.md) §5.6) is the only thing stopping the narrative from overstating `gymRpeAvg` (sport+run RPE, no gym data) and the weight deltas (consecutive-measured-day sums) — if the assembler's metric→field mapping changes, that legend must change with it or the model will confidently overstate. A run auto-archived by starting the next one (the single-active invariant) has no report until someone visits it and hits „Riport generálása" — nothing backfills it proactively; mock mode's `mockRegenerate` can "generate" a report for a still-active run (no archived-only guard in the offline demo, real mode enforces the 409). **Three context fields are computed but never rendered:** `MesoContextJson.Week.sleepQualityAvg`/`waterAvgMl`/`mealCoverageDays` (and the totals-level `mealCoverageDays`) round-trip through `getReport` and ride the AI payload (so the narrative can reference sleep quality or fuel coverage), but `MesoReportPage.tsx`'s context block only ever reads `sleepAvgH`/`kcalAvg`/`energyAvg`/`stressAvg`/`weightDeltaKg`/`sportMinutes`/`sportSessions`/`runSessions`/`gymRpeAvg` — a FE-only gap, not a data gap, and remained open through S4 (the compare view reads only the context TOTALS, never the weekly rows, so it never needed these three fields either). **S4 (`mezo-meyc.4`) shipped the compare view and closed out the epic — see below for its own deferred edges.**
- **Compare view aligns volume by week NUMBER, not phase (`mezo-meyc.4`):** `alignVolumeWeeks` (`frontend/src/features/train/logic/mesoCompare.ts:72-97`) faces W1 against W1, W2 against W2, … regardless of each run's own phase curve — a 4-week MEV block and a 6-week block with its deload in a different slot still line up by raw index, so "week 3 of run A" and "week 3 of run B" can land in different phases. Phase-aware alignment (MEV-to-MEV, deload-to-deload) is a real design question the spec left open, not solved here — a candidate for a future compare-view revision if two differently-phased runs turn out to be the common case. `betterSide` (now exported from `frontend/src/features/train/logic/mesoCompare.ts`, moved there from the page in the mezo-meyc.4 fix wave) and the library's max-two selection cap (`frontend/src/features/train/pages/MesoKonyvtarPage.tsx:62-65`) both carry dedicated unit tests as of that wave (`mesoCompare.test.ts`'s `betterSide` block; `MesoKonyvtarPage.test.tsx`'s third-tap-refused test) — the one-sided-null branch was in fact a real bug (a regression on the only-measured side used to win by default) fixed in the same wave.
- **S4 history cards ship a presence chip, not the spec's „key results" (scope narrowed from spec §4/§8, mezo-meyc.4 fix wave):** the design spec's `Történet` section promised cards carrying "key results — adherence %, top strength jump" per closed run; what actually shipped is `ArchivedMesoCard`'s `hasReport`-driven footer chip (`riport` / `nincs riport`) plus a `closedAt`-based eyebrow date — narrating a run's outcome on the card would mean batch-fetching every archived run's report just to render the list (the compare view deliberately fetches one report per request instead, §4 above), so it was YAGNI-cut for this wave rather than solved. A bd follow-up is filed to revisit a lightweight batched summary if the card ever needs to show adherence/strength at a glance.
- **Single-active invariant** for both mesocycles and running blocks (enforced server-side on activate AND on meso create-as-active).
- **Records resolve over soft-deleted exercises** via native query — day-edits soft-delete template rows but must not erase history.
- **Warmup sets (`kind='warmup'`) are excluded from every "real work" reader** (hypertrophy drive, `mezo-dhdr`; unified onto the shared gate `mezo-88iwa.3`): the shared Epley e1RM formula (weight × (30 + reps) / 30, `REP_CAP = 12`) is now single-homed in `OneRepMax.estimate` (`feature/train/service/OneRepMax.java`), and `OneRepMax.eligible` (working kind, not skipped, positive weight, `1..REP_CAP` reps) additionally gates most e1RM readers — `MedalEvaluator`, `GymSignalCalculator` progression XP, `MesocycleReportService`'s strength block, and (cross-feature) the goal engine's `GuardEvaluationService` strength guard, which previously hand-rolled its own filter (no warmup exclusion, cap 10, skipped sets not excluded) and is now on the same gate. `ExerciseRecordService` is the one exception: it still does not exclude skipped working sets from e1RM/best-set/session-volume records and never calls `OneRepMax.eligible` (open, `mezo-za09c`). `WorkoutService.lastWeekRefs` counts working sets only via its own separate query. So a warmup ramp never inflates a PR, a "Múlt hét" reference, earned XP, or a goal-engine strength trend. Correspondingly the recommendation engine reads **completed-instance history only** — the open live session is invisible to its own prescription, which is what makes the prescription **stable across a mid-workout hard-reload resume** (§4 engine subsection).
- **`ProgressionDecider` is a pure, Spring-free static method** (`decide(RefSet ref, repMin, repMax, targetRir, inc, plateStep, deloadWeek)`, `feature/train/service/ProgressionDecider.java`, `mezo-5pfe`) — no DB, no Spring wiring, unit-tested directly (`ProgressionDeciderTest`) rather than through an integration test; `SetRecommendationService` is the only caller, and the decider's precondition (`ref.weightKg() != null`) is enforced by the caller — the weightless/first-session/anchor-only branches never reach it. **A grind beats reps-in-range in the lever priority**: an athlete who hit `rep_min..rep_max` reps but logged a harder-than-target RIR (`slack = loggedRIR − targetRIR < 0`) gets **HOLD**, not the naive reps-only rule's **+rep** — RIR silently overrides the rep count in exactly this one branch. **A null logged RIR is neutral (`slack = 0`), never a grind** — an un-rated set can still earn a `+rep` or `+weight` lever exactly as if it had hit the target RIR.
- **Ownership errors:** foreign/missing → **404** (indistinguishable); `logSet` on a completed instance → **409**.
- **`phase_curve text[]` is `List<String>`** (not `String[]`) for Hibernate dirty-checking.
- Day-edit save (`MesoExercises.tsx`) fires `saveDayExercises` only when the day carries a real row `id`; mock fixtures have no id → local-only. **Exercise reorder is real** (the `SortableList` primitive, drag + ▲▼): the builder remaps the day's exercises and persists via the same `saveDayExercises` full-list PUT (array order = the contract); the planner (`MesocyclePlannerPage`'s terminal `Program` step) reorders its local draft `program` only (saved wholesale at the end).
- **The wizard's draft still lives in one reducer; generation is now a single explicit call, not a per-step guard (`mezo-yty6`, superseding the 3-step `mezo-d20.14` description in prior revisions of this doc).** `wizard/wizardState.ts`'s `wizardReducer` owns everything — **`step: 'interview' | 'editor'`** (was a numeric step index), `daysOfWeek`, `weeks`, `priorities`, `goalText`, `name`, `proposal`, the editable `program: MesoDay[]`, a `dirty` flag, `activeDay`, and the new **`renameDay`** action (day-name edits inside `MesoDayEditor`). `MesocyclePlannerPage.tsx` is still a pure dispatcher. The generator is called once, from the interview's `onGenerate`, never from a step-entry guard — there is no step to round-trip through any more, so the old `step === 2 && !state.proposal` gate and the **`inputChanged(state)`**/`proposalInput` signature-diff guard it protected were **deleted as unreachable**: the one-screen interview asks every generator input (days, weeks, priorities, goal text) before the CTA unlocks, so there is no longer a path back to change an input after generating without also going through the `dirty` regeneration confirm below. **Every `editProgram` dispatch still sets `dirty: true`**; `dirty` remains the sole regeneration guard — the editor's `↺ Újragenerálás` footer button checks it and opens an inline confirm strip instead of calling the generator directly when true (§2 above), except its own failed-regeneration retry (`Újra`), which bypasses the check (follow-up filed, §9 below). Added-exercise ids still use `crypto.randomUUID()`, minted once per proposal by `mesoPlanHooks.ts`'s `toProposal`/`seedDays`.
- **Gym time is a *standalone* schedule, not a meso-day field** (Option Y, `2026-06-15-gym-schedule-times-design.md`): `GymScheduleSlot` persists across mesocycles; the meso decides *whether* you train that weekday, the slot decides *when*. `deriveGymSchedule` joins them by weekday — a slot with no meso gym day shows nothing; a meso gym day with no slot renders with `time: null` (graceful). PUT is **replace-all** (delete-then-insert the whole week).
- **`Mai` orders by time-of-day, not modality** — `daySessions` (`features/train/logic/agenda.ts`) is the ordering helper `TrainTodayPage`'s heroes use (historically also shared by `WeeklyDayRow`'s weekly rows, before that component retired with the Train Titanium T12 Terhelés reface, `mezo-88iwa.13`); untimed (null/`''`) sort last (`'99:99'` sentinel), ties stable by modality. `TrainTodayPage` builds today's hero from a `daySessions` pass, with run-only-today handled via a **synthetic** agenda day (no gym/sport but a running session) so the run still gets a hero; a fully empty today shows "Ma pihenőnap".
- **The "Időpontok" entry is always visible** (since `mezo-4t43`; on Heti's `PageHead` since the Gym fold): in mock mode `saveGymSchedule` no-ops, so a local optimistic `gymOverride` keeps the edit visible in-session (not persisted across reload); real mode persists via `PUT` + `['train','gymSchedule']` invalidation. Mock mode renders gym times from the static `gymScheduleMock` slots (`data/train/train.ts`). The **mesocycle planner Step 2** is the primary editor of the same standing schedule.
- **`Mai` done-state matching is asymmetric** (`TrainTodayPage.tsx`) — there is **no schedule↔log link** for sport, so "logged" is matched by **the shown day's date AND sport, per slot** (`mezo-05v6` generalized this from a volleyball-hardcoded match; `mezo-9bbc` then generalized "today's date" to "the shown day's date" so the same match works on any past day): `loggedSportOn(iso, k) = sport.sessions.find(s => s.sport === k && s.date === huMonthDayDow(iso))` for the hero (called with `shownIso`, today's date when today is shown), `sportDoneOn(date, k)` for the weekly rows (the mapped `SportSession.date` is the HU display string, so we compare against the target date formatted the same way) — a mixed day (e.g. a TRX slot + a volleyball slot) flips each slot independently since each carries its own `k`. Running **does** carry the prescribed tuple back, so it matches on `blockId + weekNumber + sessionKey` (`runLoggedFor(key)`), ignoring date. **Gym** has no client-readable per-day completion, so the backend computes it: `GET /api/train/workouts/today` returns `weekDoneDates` — the ISO dates this Mon–Sun week whose gym instance is **`status = 'completed'`** (`WorkoutService.doneDatesThisWeek` → `WorkoutSessionRepository.findDoneInstanceDates`; **completed-only since `mezo-cd8s`**, not the old ≥1-logged-set — a started-but-unclosed session is settled by the lazy auto-close, §4). `TrainTodayPage` derives `loggedGym = weekDoneDates.includes(today)` for the hero, and per-row `gymLogged`/`isSportLogged(slot)` (matched against each weekly row's computed calendar `date`, the latter also against the slot's sport) so **past** completed days are marked, not just today. **The done-state stores nothing new — it is a pure read-derivation over already-persisted sessions/sets.** In real mode the log itself is persisted to Postgres (`POST /api/train/sport-sessions` → `SportService.create` → `sport_session` table, §4); the flip then happens after `logSportMutation`/`logRunSession` **invalidate→refetch** the session query from the backend (the DB is the source of truth — the same data the Sport `Napló` reads). The `setQueryData` cache-append is **mock-only** (the `mock ? … : …` arm in `trainHooks.ts`): it exists so the UI flips without a backend in tests/prototype, was a silent no-op before, and **never** substitutes for the DB in real mode — mock `sport.week` stats are also not recomputed on append (real mode re-derives them). A timezone edge exists: the backend stamps the log date server-side, so the flip relies on the server "today" matching the browser's local date.
- **Retroactive logging (`mezo-9bbc`, Task 9) is client-date-only.** A past-day "Pótold" log changes exactly one thing over a normal today-log: the sheet sends the selected day's ISO `date` in the request body instead of omitting it (`SportLogSheet`)/instead of today's `localDateString()` (`RunLogSheet`) — see the date-wiring paragraph above. Nothing else about the write path is date-aware: ownership (`created_by`) is still stamped server-side from the security principal exactly as on any other write, and XP/level-up resolution runs through the same `onSuccess: r => showLevelUp(r?.levelUp)` handler regardless of which day the log is dated to. There is no backend distinction between "logged live" and "logged retroactively" — the DB just stores whatever `date` the client sent.
- **The sub-nav lineage is over — three layers, all retired.** Generic `.subnav-item` underline → the coral `.np-pills`/`.np-pill` pill nav (Napiv S4, `mezo-8141`; Train first, Fuel/`--sage` and Me/`--lav` after) → a `SubNavDropdown` popover fed by `TRAIN_TABS` off `AppHero`'s `utilities` prop (compact-header redesign, `mezo-ugqb`) → **nothing**: `TrainSection`, `TrainSubNav`, `tabs.ts`/`TRAIN_TABS` and `AppHero` are all deleted (`mezo-d20.3.1` + `mezo-d20.9.1`). Navigation between Train surfaces is the global `TabBar`'s four tabs (Train Titanium T4, navigation subsection above) plus each page's back chip; the hub's six tiles are gone. The S4 `GYM` → `Gym` copy rename outlived its own tab: the label survives only in prose.
- **`weeklyLoad` (`logic/weeklyLoad.ts`) headlines the Futás load tile with the week's dominant running style by intensity** — `sprint` > `pyramid` (`piramis`) > `steady` (`tempó`). Each `RunPrescribedSession.kind` (`sprint|pyramid|steady` per `train.yml`) maps to its own short lowercase Hungarian label; a `steady`-only week now reads `tempó` rather than collapsing into `piramis` (fixed in S8, `mezo-mifi`; the raw `.value` string used to render verbatim on `LoadTiles` — that tile itself retired with T12, the summarizer module is untouched).
- **`WeeklyDayRow`'s gym block used to title itself off `gym.type`** (historical note — the component is deleted, T12): `GymScheduleDay` has no separate workout-name field, so the type string (e.g. `Push`) was the only label available. It used to be printed twice (title **and** meta line) to disambiguate same-time gym days; since the stacked restructure (`mezo-lruy`) the pills carried only the schedule facts (time, duration) and the type appeared once.
- **The Napiv S4 accent migration is partial, not domain-wide** (and Mozaik 2.0 layered `--mz-*` on top of it rather than replacing it — the re-faced pages speak both). `TrainTodayPage`, the then-`GymPage`, `SportPage`, `RunningPage`, and (before both retired with T12) `WeeklyDayRow`/`LoadTiles` were re-skinned onto `--tag-*`/`--wash-*` (`TrainSubNav` was S4's pill-nav consumer; it's since deleted, compact-header redesign `mezo-ugqb`); `MesoKonyvtarPage`/`MesocycleBuilderPage`/`MesocyclePlannerPage`/`RunningBlockBuilderPage` only picked up the `.pghead-np` page-head (body content still runs on `--coral`); `ExercisesPage` since moved onto the muscle-family tokens (`logic/muscleColors.ts`, `mezo-kaui`). **Fixed in the final-review wave (`mezo-8141`):** `TrainTodayPage`'s own hero "MA"/"Kész" status chips (volleyball + run heroes, both the corner chip and the log CTA) now consume `--tag-sport`/`--tag-run` instead of the pre-S4 `--cat-tendency`/`--info`. The final legacy-token holdouts — `SportLogSheet`/`SportScheduleSheet`/`RunLogSheet`, `ChallengeCard`, and the run builder/editor (`RunningBlockBuilderPage`/`WeekdayGrid`/`RunWeekEditor`) — were migrated off `--cat-tendency`/`--info` onto `--rose`/`--sky` in the S8 cleanup (`mezo-mifi`), leaving `features/train` free of the legacy accents (the un-re-skinned Builder/Planner body content still runs on `--coral`, not the legacy tokens). **Napiv S5 separately finished the `--brand-glow` → coral migration for the whole active-workout surface** — the warmup rows, exercise list, prescribed-list accent, note pill, challenge tint, the `Kezdjük el` CTA (now `.np-cta`, was `CtaPrimary`), and the `FeedbackModal`/`ExerciseActionSheet`/add-set-prompt/note-editor eyebrows, plus `WorkoutSummary`/`PRToast` (both coral; `WorkoutSummary` replaced the deleted `WorkoutComplete` in `mezo-cd8s`; `PRToast` itself is since deleted too, replaced by `MedalToast`/`MedalChip`, `mezo-wp6n`) — these are no longer part of the "unmigrated" set above. Candidate for an S8 cleanup pass (see the S4 plan's out-of-scope note). Residual source-comment prose naming retired tokens was reworded at the vocabulary-sweep close (`mezo-x3x0`, comments only; two of the three files it touched — `ChallengesCarousel.tsx` and `PhaseDots.tsx` — are now dead or deleted).
- **Rest duration is hardcoded, not data-driven** (`restSecondsFor`, `features/train/logic/restTimer.ts`, Napiv S5): 150s for `compound`, 90s otherwise — no rest-duration field exists anywhere in the model or DB. A configurable per-exercise rest is a Phase 2 backlog candidate (needs a new data-model field), not a Phase 3 item.
- **`⊘ Kihagyás` clears a live rest first** (`handleSkip` calls `rest.skip()` before the model transition — the guard originates in the `mezo-8141` final-review fix) — abandoning the current exercise mid-rest must not leave the rest bar counting down toward it.
- **The active-workout `90s`/Voice tool chips were removed outright, not left inert** (Napiv S5, `mezo-8141`): the old always-present-but-disabled chip row is gone from the UI entirely (superseded by the real rest countdown for timing). Voice logging itself is still Phase 3 — it just no longer shows even a disabled chip.
- **Per-set note and per-exercise note are two different data paths — don't conflate them.** The transient `.setnote` input (`SetLogRequest.note`, ≤500 chars, cleared after every logged set) rides the `POST .../{id}/sets` body (`ActiveWorkoutPage.tsx`); the durable per-exercise note (F4, above) is a separate `PUT /api/train/exercises/{exerciseId}/note` call that persists on the exercise template, survives across sessions, and pre-loads next time. Both inputs coexist on the same `.wo-card` — though the card's OWN cursor-row input has no note field of its own, see the per-set note wire gap in §2 Active workout (`mezo-azzcq`).
- **`components/LastWeekStat.tsx` is orphaned dead code** (Napiv S5) — no import anywhere in `src`; the "múlt hét" comparison it used to render a column of now lives once, inside the `⚡ Progresszió` strip's `ProgressionBanner` (`mezo-d20.3.9` removed the `.wkx-subrow` that briefly carried it). Left in place, not deleted — see the dead-prep-components gotcha above.
- **`TrainMapper.toSetResponse` was silently dropping `setIndex` from every `ExerciseSetResponse`** (found + fixed while building set edit/delete, `mezo-l3on`): the entity property is `setIndex` but the generated DTO's builder method is `index(Integer)`, so MapStruct's implicit by-name matching never wired it — an explicit `@Mapping(target = "index", source = "setIndex")` (`TrainMapper.java:77`) was needed. Every real-mode `logSet`/`updateSet`/workout-detail response had been shipping with no `setIndex` until this fix; nothing client-side depended on it (the FE tracks its own positional cursor), so the bug was latent, not user-visible.
- **Set edit/delete follow-ups still open (`mezo-l3on`):** editing or deleting a logged set only works on the **active** instance — a finished workout (`/train/review/:id`) stays read-only, with no retroactive correction path. The slot count (`extra`/`removed`) is still **client-only** state, exactly like the pre-existing `＋ Szett`: a mid-workout hard reload restores the template's planned count, even though the logged sets' own edits/deletes are persistent. A server-side slot count is a candidate follow-up, not yet built.
- **`durationEst` (the ESTIMATE column) is permanently NULL in production — a completely separate gap from the estimator/measurement machinery both being live** (see §4 `Actual-duration measurement`): `WorkoutService.startWorkout` copies a template's `duration_est` onto its instance, but nothing anywhere ever writes a *template's* `duration_est` in the first place, so the copy always copies `NULL`. `WorkoutSummary`'s "terv" clause and the gym push-notification title (`AnchorResolver.gymTitle`) both silently degrade instead of breaking (**`mezo-lhmh`**, P3, open). Do not confuse this with the unrelated, actually-live `estimateSessionMinutes`/`SessionTimingCalculator` pair — one is a dead DB column, the other two are the app's real duration sources (planned and measured, respectively).

**Deferred / Phase 3 (🟣 do NOT assume live):** `niggleWarning`; the live **cross-load → volume recompute engine** (Sport AND Running cross-load are still static; Heti's group glass (`GroupGlassBody`, formerly `MuscleWeekSheet`, T12) static `sportMuscleLoad.ts` heuristic is the FE interim, `mezo-ly27` — this is distinct from the weekly **performance** ramp, which shipped, see §4); `shoulderLoadTrend` (constant `'stabil'`); sport `jumpCount`/`intensity`/`team`/`season`/`weeklyHours` capture; gym-schedule **`duration`** only (gym **time** is now live via `GymScheduleSlot` — §4); copy-week ("Heti terv másolása" inert); the active-workout **Voice** tool (no UI, ever since Napiv S5 removed the old inert chip — §9 gotcha above); Progressive Overload **Plan 3** (the daily-challenge overload tie-in, `mezo-gj42` — Plan 1 and Plan 2 have both shipped, see §1/§2/§4). The rest-timer **duration** stays out of scope too (hardcoded 150/90s, no data field) but the countdown itself is real, not deferred.

**Deferred by the Design 2.0 wave itself (real gaps, honestly open):**
- **Four pages never got a Mozaik face** — `Mai` (`TrainTodayPage`), `Sport`, `Futás`, `Sablonok` — plus the two builders (`MesocycleBuilderPage`/run page, `RunningBlockBuilderPage`). They work, and their `mezo-d20.3.4`/`.3.5` slices fixed what was load-bearing, but the tab reads as two visual generations at once. **The mezo editors' own deep design round shipped as `mezo-yty6`** (spec [`2026-09-07-mezo-szerkeszto-redesign-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-09-07-mezo-szerkeszto-redesign-design.md), plan [`2026-09-07-mezo-szerkeszto-redesign.md`](../superpowers/plans/2026-09-07-mezo-szerkeszto-redesign.md)) — the planner and the template editor are now both on the Mozaik `MesoWeekEditor`, superseding the "design first, dev issue after" placeholder this bullet used to carry as **F7.2/F7.4**. Five of the round's own known gaps got dedicated follow-up issues rather than a silent drop: the generator can still schedule the same muscle on consecutive days while the editor only warns (`mezo-4neq`), the running mesocycle's day editor (`MesoExercises`/`MesoDayPage`) still renders the older `MesoEditor`/`ExerciseAccordionRow` pair instead of `ExerciseCard` — explicitly out of scope this round, see the Day page bullet above (`mezo-ntva`), a **saved** template lost its `Fókusz` tier picker, moved into the wizard interview only, §2 above (`mezo-9kto`), the wizard dropped the hero's `llmUsed` provenance line and the primary save CTA's `· {startDate}` suffix (`mezo-bhrm`), and the draft editor's failed-regeneration retry (`Újra`) bypasses the `dirty` confirm it would otherwise require, a pre-existing behaviour carried over from the old wizard (`mezo-ra9v`).
- **The `Történet` mosaic tile has no route.** It scrolls the already-rendered history section into view instead of navigating — the only tile in the app that does, and a placeholder until a dedicated history route exists (F2.7 scope).
- **Heti shows no weekly RPE average.** The prototype has the stat; no cross-domain aggregate (gym RIR + sport/run RPE) exists to compute it, so the cell was dropped rather than fabricated. Same call as `Gyakorlatok`' dropped `PR e héten` cell (no dated week-boundary contract).
- **The three dead prep components + `LastWeekStat`** (gotcha above) still ship in the bundle.
- **NOT deferred, for the avoidance of doubt:** the pyramid `completedRounds` scoring fix landed on both sides — `mezo-d20.3.5` made the FE capture and send the value honestly for every run kind, and the backend half (F6.3) shipped with the F6 backend slice.

---

## 10. Key files

**FE — data / hooks / api**
- `frontend/src/data/hooks.ts` — single boundary, re-exports `useTrain`/`useRunning` (`:202-203`)
- `frontend/src/data/train/trainHooks.ts` — Train queries + mutations, mode branching
- `frontend/src/data/train/runningHooks.ts` — Running queries + server-emulating mock mutations
- `frontend/src/data/train/train.ts` — Train mock fixtures, label maps, presets
- `frontend/src/data/train/running.ts`, `frontend/src/data/train/runningDraft.ts`, `frontend/src/data/train/runningAgenda.ts` — running fixtures + pure structure editors + agenda helpers
- `frontend/src/data/train/trainApi.ts`, `frontend/src/data/train/runningApi.ts` — REST clients + re-exported gen types
- `frontend/src/data/train/challengeApi.ts`, `frontend/src/data/train/challengeHooks.ts` — **workout challenges (`mezo-hbwi`, proactive-owned surface consumed here)**: `useChallenges`/`useChallengeActions` over `/api/proactive/challenge` (dual-mode; re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`); backend lives in [proactive.md](proactive.md)
- `frontend/src/data/train/workoutDetailHooks.ts` — **done-day review data (`mezo-cd8s`)**: `useWorkoutDetail(id)` (`GET /api/train/workouts/{id}`, mock serves a static `workoutDetailMock` fixture) + `useWeekWorkouts()` (this Mon–Sun week's `listWorkouts` summaries → the weekly-row date→instance-id map); both re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`
- `frontend/src/data/train/mesoArcHooks.ts` — **volume-arc data (Progressive Overload Plan 2 Phase B, `mezo-hi9m`)**: `useMesocycleVolumeArc(id)` (`GET /api/train/mesocycles/{id}/volume-arc`, dual-mode; mock derives `mesoVolumeArcMock` from `data/train/train.ts`); re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`; domain types `MesoVolumeArc`/`MuscleVolumeArc`/`VolumeArcWeek` live in `data/types.ts`
- `frontend/src/data/train/mesoTemplateHooks.ts` — **template/run split (`mezo-meyc.1`)**: `useMesoTemplates()` — dual-mode list (`useDualQuery`, mock `mesoTemplatesMock` from `data/train/train.ts`) + `createTemplate`/`updateTemplate`/`deleteTemplate`/`startTemplate`/`rerun` mutations; real mode invalidates both `['train','mesoTemplates']` and `['train','mesocycles']` on success, mock mutates both caches directly via `setQueryData` (a started/rerun template touches both); re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`
- `frontend/src/data/train/mesoReportHooks.ts` — **run report + close flow (`mezo-meyc.2`)**: `useMesoReport(id)` (exported `mesoReportQueryKey`, `GET /api/train/mesocycles/{id}/report` resolving a 404 to `notFound`, `regenerate()`/`regenerating` over `POST .../report/regenerate`, a 3s poll gated on `aiEvalStatus==='pending' && aiEvalEnabled`); `useTrain().closeMesocycle` (in `trainHooks.ts`) posts the extended `{selfEval?}` close body; both re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`
- `frontend/src/data/types.ts` — Train domain types
- `frontend/src/data/train/{medalTypes,medalApi,medalHooks,medalMock,medalEvaluator}.ts` — **medal collection (`mezo-wp6n`)**: FE-side `Medal`/`MedalType`/`MedalTier` types, `medalApi.list()` (`GET /api/train/medals`), `useMedals()` (`useDualQuery`, key `['train','medals']`, re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`), a seeded mock cabinet, and `medalEvaluator.ts` — the §6-rules port for mock mode, seeded from the mock plan's `lastWeek` per exercise (`SESSION_VOLUME` deliberately not evaluated in mock mode)

**FE — progression level-up (P5, `mezo-te8k`; consumed by the 3 finish flows)**
- `frontend/src/features/progression/{LevelUpScreen,LevelUpProvider,levelUpMeta}.{tsx,ts}` — the full-bleed overlay, the single `useLevelUp()` host (mounted in `AppLayout`), and the skillKey → HU name+emoji map (muscle names reuse `MUSCLE_LABELS`)
- `frontend/src/data/progression/progressionMock.ts` — deterministic seeded `gymLevelUpMock`/`sportLevelUpMock`/`runLevelUpMock` fixtures (mock-mode finish/sport/run return these)
- `frontend/src/shared/hooks/useReducedMotion.ts` — shared reduced-motion gate; `.levelup*` styles + keyframes in `frontend/src/styles/prototype.css`

**FE — rest timer (`mezo-xt65`, replaced the S5 Live-Activity island; T6 moved the render surface off-card onto the fixed dock — feature-local, no shell seam)**
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/useRestTimer.ts` — page-local countdown state hook (`idle | running | paused`; `start/pause/resume/skip`, 500ms self-tick, self-clears at 0)
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/WorkoutDock.tsx` — the fixed session-level dock (T6 Task 6): idle progress ring + `n/m szett` + the 3-state finish CTA; resting the SAME ring fills as the rest elapses + `+30s`/`Kész` — replaces the deleted `RestTimerBar.tsx`'s in-card `.restbar` CTA-morph (T7 sweep, `mezo-88iwa.7`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/restTimer.ts` — pure `restSecondsFor(type)` (150s compound / 90s else, hardcoded) + `fmtMMSS` — the only place the rest duration is decided

**FE — real-anatomy body geometry (Train Titanium T3, `mezo-88iwa.4`; no consumer yet — T12)**
The artwork is vendored, not drawn: `scripts/vendor/musclemap/{MaleFrontPaths,MaleBackPaths}.swift`
are the **MIT**-licensed path data from [MuscleMap](https://github.com/melihcolpan/MuscleMap)
(Copyright (c) Melih Colpan — see [NOTICE.md](../../NOTICE.md) at the repo root); openGym's own
converted paths are **AGPL-3.0** and were never copied — full provenance + the two bbox-math bugs
found while building the generator are in
[`docs/research/musclemap-body-geometry.md`](../research/musclemap-body-geometry.md).
- `scripts/gen-body-geometry.mjs` — turns the two Swift files into the generated
  `frontend/src/features/train/logic/bodyGeometry.gen.ts` (`BODY: Record<'front'|'back', {vb, p, b}>`,
  `// Generated … — do not hand-edit.` banner): `parse()` kebab-cases each `BodyPartPathData` slug
  and collects its path strings, `bbox()` walks the path cursor through both absolute AND relative
  commands (many shapes draw their whole outline in lowercase relative curves) to derive each
  shape's viewBox/crop box. `node scripts/gen-body-geometry.mjs --check` regenerates to a temp
  buffer and diffs against the committed file — 0 exit means generator and output agree; run it
  after touching the vendored sources or the generator itself.
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/bodyMapShapes.ts` — the tested 21-token→shape table
  (`shapesFor(token)`, legacy keys `chest`/`lats`/`back`/`shoulder`/`rear-delt`/`biceps`/`triceps`
  remapped to the nearest live token): `chest-upper→front/upper-chest`, `chest-mid→front/chest`,
  `chest-lower→front/lower-chest`, `back-wide`/`back-mid→back/upper-back`, `back-lower→back/lower-back`,
  `traps→back/trapezius`, `shoulder-front→front/front-deltoid`, `shoulder-side→front/deltoids`,
  `shoulder-rear→back/deltoids`, **`biceps-long`/`biceps-short`/`biceps-brachialis→front/biceps`**
  (all three heads collapse onto MuscleMap's one arm shape), **`triceps-long`/`triceps-lateral`/
  `triceps-medial→back/triceps`** (same collapse), `quad→front/quadriceps`+`inner-quad`+`outer-quad`,
  `ham→back/hamstring`, `glute→back/gluteal`, `calf→back/calves`,
  `core→front/abs`+`upper-abs`+`lower-abs`+`obliques`. Where several tokens share one shape, **a
  renderer combines them per its own semantics, not the table** — `bodyMapShapes.ts` itself takes no
  position on sum vs max. An unmapped token is dropped, never guessed.
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/BodyMap.tsx` — two-view (`front`/`back`) heat silhouette:
  one faint ink outline of the whole body plus one lit `<g>` per drawable shape carrying heat
  (`OPACITY` keyed on `weekZone.ts`'s `'none'|'below'|'entering'|'in'|'over'` vocabulary); a shape
  shared by several tokens takes their **MAX** opacity (heat is a scale, not additive — see the
  table note above). `views:'auto'` shows whichever side (front/back) sums more heat (`viewLoad`),
  `views:'both'` shows both. The root reserves its `aspect-ratio` box (`66/64` single, `132/64` duo)
  before geometry arrives, so nothing below it jumps on load.
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/MuscleChip.tsx` — one-token zoom-crop icon: union bbox of
  the token's shapes → 30% air on every side (`side = max(w,h) * 1.6`), squared, centered (the
  prototype's `muscleIcon` math). First paint uses the **generated** `BODY[view].b` boxes as a
  fallback crop (works in jsdom, which has no `getBBox`); in a real browser the first mounted
  instance per shape-set **refines** the crop once via `groupRef.current.getBBox()` and caches the
  result module-wide (`cropCache`, keyed `"<view>/<slug1>,<slug2>,…"`) so later instances and
  re-renders skip remeasuring. An unknown token (`shapesFor` → `[]`) renders `null` immediately; a
  known token still waiting on geometry/crop renders a **reserved-space placeholder**
  (`<span style={{width,height}}>`) instead of `null`, so a chip never collapses its layout slot.
- **Dynamic-import rule:** both components load `bodyGeometry.gen` via `import('../logic/bodyGeometry.gen')`
  inside `useEffect`, never a static top-level import — the module is ~90kB of path data and must
  never land in the main bundle. **Both now have real production consumers, wired by Train Titanium
  T12 (`mezo-88iwa.13`, the `Heti` subsection above):** `BodyMap` renders in `TrainWeekPage`'s hero
  and map-doorway card and in the full `TrainWeekMapPage`; `MuscleChip` renders on `TrainWeekPage`'s
  group-card heads and `GroupGlassBody`'s per-muscle rows. The dynamic import still keeps
  `bodyGeometry.gen` out of the main bundle — it now lands in its own split chunk instead of being
  tree-shaken away entirely (re-verified against `pnpm build`'s output as part of the T12 sweep).

**FE — the ⓘ explain layer (Train parity P3, `mezo-b516k`; §2 „The ⓘ explain layer")**

The hit-area test guards the click WIRING (jsdom has no layout); the geometry is guarded by the `elementFromPoint` probe in `frontend/tests/layout/layout.spec.ts`
