`frontend/src/features/train/logic/offDay.ts` — shared `isOffDay` off-day predicate (ported verbatim from the deleted `MesoDayTabsEditor`); `frontend/src/features/train/logic/exerciseDefaults.ts` — `libraryToGymExercise`/`addExerciseWithDefaults` (`mezo-dq60`; since `mezo-d20.14` a **single hypertrophy scheme**, the `goalPreset`-keyed `SCHEMES` table and `planner.ts` are retired) keyed by the picked exercise's `type` alone: plyo always gets `PLYO_SCHEME` + is exempt via `type !== 'plyo'`; `addExerciseWithDefaults` re-runs `suggestedWarmupSets` after insertion to refine the type-seeded `warmupSets`; `frontend/src/features/train/logic/musclePriorities.ts` — `TIER_GROUPS` (the 9 landmark groups), `TIER_LABELS`, `EMPHASIZE_CAP` (2), `tierOf`/`setTier`/`tierTargetOf` (`mezo-3m5m`, §4 `#### Volume progression` + `#### Weekly bands`); `frontend/src/features/train/logic/peakWeekFit.ts` — `peakWeekFit`, the peak-week session-time fit signal, a FE mirror of `WorkoutService.effectiveWorkingSets` (`mezo-3m5m`, spec GD6, §4 `#### Weekly bands`); `frontend/src/features/train/components/MusclePriorityPicker.tsx` — the Fókusz tier picker (`mezo-3m5m`); since `mezo-yty6` mounted **only** in the wizard's `InterviewStep` — the template editor's own `Fókusz` `<details>` block is gone, so a saved template's tiers can no longer be edited from the UI (follow-up filed, §9); `frontend/src/features/train/components/PeakFitCard.tsx` — renders `peakWeekFit`'s out-of-band days ("Csúcshét · időbecslés", `mezo-3m5m`); `frontend/src/features/train/logic/mesoLoad.ts` — the unified editor's load derivations (`weekMuscleLoad`/`dayMuscleLoad`/`adjacentDayConflicts`, plus `dayTone`/`DayTone`, moved here from `wizard/dayTiles.ts`, `mezo-yty6`, §2/§4); `frontend/src/features/train/components/MesoWeekEditor.tsx` (the unified editor both the planner's draft mode and the template editor's `template` mode render), `MesoDayEditor.tsx` (the per-day page-state view it opens — renameable day, `LoadTile`, always-open `ExerciseCard`s, one-shot entrance choreography), `ExerciseCard.tsx` (the always-open, all-fields-writable exercise row — `ExerciseAccordionRow`'s successor in this surface only), `ZoneBar.tsx` (the MEV/MAV/MRV zone gauge with a tier-target line + value marker), `LoadTile.tsx` (the compact week/day load summary tile), `DayStripTile.tsx` (the horizontally-scrolling day-mosaic tile), `WeekLoadPanel.tsx` (the Heti terhelés full page — per-muscle `ZoneBar` cards + the adjacency lint + `peakWeekFit`), `DayLoadPanel.tsx` (the Napi terhelés full page — per-muscle session-cap cards)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/sportMuscleLoad.ts` — static sport/run-kind → muscle load-level heuristic over the live schedule + run week (`mezo-ly27`; Phase-3 cross-load engine's interim)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/growthForecast.ts` — planned week → estimated athletic/muscle XP + level-up flags; hand-mirrors the backend progression economy (`mezo-ly27`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/summaryStats.ts` — **`WorkoutSummary` redesign derivations (`mezo-w943`)**: `deriveSummaryStats(exercises, medals)` → done/planned sets+exercises, `volumeT`, `avgRir` (null with 0 logged sets), region pills (`muscleRegion()` aggregation, off-flagged), RECORD/TARGET medal split (`targetGroups` per-exercise counts) and the per-exercise set-chip view (`topSetIndex`/`recordChipIndex` — a RECORD medal's `setIndex` wins, else a weight+reps value match); table-tested, no hooks
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/morningWindow.ts` — pure wake-anchored morning-training window (`mezo-67rb`): `morningWindow(wake)` = [wake+60′, wake+6h] (Train-only circadian window; **since `mezo-u6jx`** no backend mirror — the habit engine's tick is date-presence), `offendingSlots`/`rescheduledSlots` (`time > end`), `snoozeHash`/`isSnoozed`/`snooze` (content-keyed `mezo-morning-training-snooze`) — §2 Mai
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/MorningTrainingCard.tsx` — the presentational sage `.mtr` nudge card (offending slots → apply/snooze), mounted on `TrainTodayPage` (`mezo-67rb`, §2 Mai)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/medalLabels.ts` — the shared Hungarian medal vocabulary (`MEDAL_TYPE_LABEL`, `MEDAL_UNIT_LABEL`, `medalValueLabel`, `formatMedalNumber`), consumed by `MedalToast`/`MedalChip`/`MedalsPage`/`WorkoutSummary` (`mezo-wp6n`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/{MedalToast,MedalChip}.tsx` — **medal collection UI (`mezo-wp6n`), replaces the deleted `PRToast.tsx`**: `MedalToast` fires only for a `RECORD`-tier medal (~4.5s, amber) with the achieving set + the prior value/date it beat; `MedalChip` is the gold row mark for `RECORD` and renders nothing for `TARGET` tier (a `TARGET_HIT` drives no visual of its own since the v4 table redesign — its rep-range status cell already conveys hit/miss, `mezo-8xmf`, §2 Active workout)
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/` — sheets/cards (`SetStepper` (Napiv S5 giant stepper — `.stepper`/`.steprow`, `mezo-8141`; ± + tap-to-edit value via `useEditableNumber`, `mezo-o7ds`), `PrepHero`/`PrepExerciseCard`/`ChallengesCarousel` (**unreferenced since the prep mosaic, `mezo-d20.3.8` — tests only, §9**; their live successors were `pages/prep/*` + `PrepExerciseTile`, all of which are themselves deleted now, §9), `ProgressionBanner` (the in-workout RIR-aware progression signal — label + delta chip + `Múlt hét → Ma a cél` comparison + rationale, 3 lever tones — renders in the card's cue slot when a `progression` exists, `mezo-5pfe`, §2 Active workout), `GymScheduleSheet` (standalone weekly gym-time editor → PUT `/gym-schedule`), `ExercisePickerSheet` (`ExerciseRecordSheet` — the pre-Titanium per-exercise REKORDOK modal — is **DELETED**, `mezo-lf3cv`; `pages/ExerciseStoryPage.tsx` replaced it), `VideoDemo` (tap-to-reveal demo player, used in workout/browser/picker — `mezo-52zg`; `videoEmbed()` resolves the stored url to an embed target: YouTube → youtube-nocookie 16:9, Instagram reel/post/tv permalink → `instagram.com/{kind}/{code}/embed` 9:16 portrait, `mezo-4g4i`), `ChallengeCard` (live challenge card, now stacked by `PrepKuldetesekPage` — „tanulom"/tools-hidden/outcome-chip states, `mezo-hbwi`), `FeedbackModal`, `WorkoutSummary` (the explicit-finish + done-day review screen — `closing`/`closed` modes, `mezo-cd8s`; replaced the deleted `WorkoutComplete`; **colorful pill/chip redesign `mezo-w943`** — hero+halo, region pills, 4-cell strip, RECORD/TARGET medal split, per-exercise set-chip map, closing-only note; the `showSetLines` prop is deleted, see §2 Active workout for the full anatomy), `SportLogSheet`, `SportScheduleSheet`, `CrossLoadRow`, `RunLogSheet`, `RunWeekEditor` (two-zone session cards: Menetrend = `WeekdayGrid` + time, Terhelés = load — still on `CompactStepper`/`NumberStep`), `WeekdayGrid` (single-select 7-day picker), `RunSessionCard`, `RunCrossLoadCard`, `TodaySessionCard` (`.todaycard` sport/run/non-today-gym session card — tag + time + title + fact pills + full-width log CTA or `DoneBar`, tone-tinted per modality, `mezo-lruy`; `ctaLabel` omitted ⇒ read-only, `mezo-9bbc`; **since `mezo-jyua` a thin wrapper over `shared/ui/ItemCard`** — the card language is shared with Today, so a change to it is cross-domain), `DoneBar` (the shared "session is done" bar — check + summary + optional detail line, worn by `TodaySessionCard`'s logged state and the gym hero's Kész state, `mezo-9bbc`; **also imported by `shared/ui/ItemCard`** since `mezo-jyua`, so a `shared/ui` file reaches into this feature — an inherited compromise with a follow-up filed to move `DoneBar` into `shared/ui`), `DayStrip` (Mai's `role="tablist"` week navigator — one `.daychip` `role="tab"` per weekday over `dayStripItems`, `mezo-9bbc`; centres the selected chip on mount via `scrollIntoView({ inline: 'center' })` — skipped under reduced motion, mount-only — since 7 chips ≈ 536 px do not fit a 440 px viewport, and its `aria-label` spells out everything the chip shows: `„Hétfő · 18. · 2/2 kész"` / `„… · nincs naplózva"` / `„… · pihenő"`, because the label replaces the chip's content as its accessible name), `MesoEditor`/`MesoEditorHero`/`WeeklyBandsCard`/`DayBreakdownCard`/`ExerciseAccordionRow` (the unified day-tabbed meso day editor, `mezo-7rdg`, §2/§4 — **survives only for the running mesocycle's day editor**, `MesoExercises`/`MesoDayPage`, since `mezo-yty6` moved the wizard/template-editor surfaces onto `MesoDayEditor`/`ExerciseCard` instead (follow-up filed to bring this third surface onto `ExerciseCard` too, §9): `MesoEditor` composes day tabs with session-cap warning dots + `MesoEditorHero` (gradient hero, day/week stats — week-level `warningCount` is session-cap breaches only, the % overage alarm retired with `SetBudgetCard`) + `DayBreakdownCard` (the active-day "Ma · izmonként" per-group bars, `mezo-smhn`, §4 `#### Weekly bands`) + `frontend/src/features/train/components/WeeklyBandsCard.tsx` (`current → ceiling` per tier, no percent text — **replaced `SetBudgetCard` everywhere in `mezo-d20.14`**: this component and the wizard's `Program`-step week band; its third mirror, on `MuscleWeekSheet`'s Gym-page detail, retired with that sheet in the T12 Terhelés reface) + a single-expand `ExerciseAccordionRow` list (🔥/🌿 toggle, 2×2 stepper grid, `Finomhangolás` disclosure, a `↺ javaslat: n` adaptive-warmup chip fed by `logic/warmupSuggest.ts`, `mezo-dnln`) — **supersedes the deleted `MesoDayTabsEditor`** (builder day-tabbed recipe editor) **and `PlannerDaySection`** (the old 5-step planner's per-day exercise-list component, also deleted in `mezo-d20.14`), which themselves had superseded `ExerciseEditRow`/`DayExerciseSection`), `ExerciseRecipeRow` (the older always-open recipe row — **retained only for `CustomWorkoutBuilderPage`**, no longer used by the meso builder/wizard), (**deleted:** `ZoneTrack`, the shared zone-bar primitive, and `WeekZoneCard`, the live zone-context card, are **DELETED** — `SetBudgetCard`, then `ZoneMiniGrid`, then the prep mosaic took their consumers one by one, §9), `DerivationSteps` (the `MesoMusclePage` provenance-derivation card, mesocycle pages v2 Task 4 — successor to the deleted `MesoOverview`/`MesoVolume`, and still standing after Train Titanium T9's re-face; **`VolumeBar.tsx` and its `FinalStat` cell are now DELETED too**, along with `PhaseCurveBars`/`MetaStat`, whose last importer went with `MesoOverviewPage` — `DerivationSteps` owns the whole 01…04 provenance body; its sibling `VolumeBand` — the old per-tile MEV/MAV/MRV bar `MesoWeekPage`'s mosaic and `MesoMusclePage`'s gauge used to share — is itself DELETED as of T9, once both pages' own `.pl-list`/`.pl-scale` faces left it with no consumer), `MesoExercises` (seeds local day-state from `meso.days`, hosts `MesoEditor`, fires the background `saveDayExercises` PUT; day-scoped via its `day?` prop since mesocycle pages v2 Task 3 — its old in-cycle `Fókusz`/`MusclePriorityPicker` `<details>` and `PUT .../muscle-priorities` call are gone), `LastWeekStat` (orphaned dead code since Napiv S5, no imports — §9), …). **`GymDaySheet`/`GymExRow` are deleted (`mezo-bxpg` direct-start flow)** — superseded by `GymPage`/`TrainTodayPage`'s own tap-handler resolution (§2).
- `frontend/src/features/train/sheets/CatalogExerciseSheet.tsx` — user-exercise authoring form (create/edit/delete; name/muscle/type/stim/fatigue/video), opened from the `Gyakorlatok` page `+ Új gyakorlat` chip (`mezo-52zg`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/sheets/VideoUrlSheet.tsx` — one-field demo-video URL editor (attach/replace/remove) for rows the viewer may re-mediate (`mediaEditable` — own rows; master rows for the OWNER only, `mezo-qw37.5`), opened from the per-row `▶ Videó` action → `setExerciseVideo` (`/video` endpoint, `mezo-bnsk`). The contract `pattern` on `videoUrl` accepts a YouTube watch/short url OR an Instagram `reel`/`reels`/`p`/`tv` permalink (profile-scoped form and `?igsh=` tracking query included); anything else is a 400 surfaced as the generic „Mentés sikertelen" toast (`mezo-4g4i`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/{WorkoutCard,WorkoutMenuGlass,WorkoutRecordsGlass,WorkoutDock,FinishConfirmGlass}.tsx` — the Titanium card list (T6, `mezo-88iwa.7`, §2 Active workout): one card per exercise, its per-card `⋮` menu glass + video glass, its per-card records glass, the fixed session dock, and the pending-sets finish-confirm glass. **`ExerciseActionSheet`/`ExerciseOverviewSheet` are both deleted** (T7 sweep) — the menu glass and the records glass replaced their jobs; there is no more session-wide jump-to list, since every exercise is already on screen.
- `frontend/src/features/train/sheets/SetEditSheet.tsx` — edit-or-delete-one-set sheet (`mezo-l3on`), opened by tapping any row of the active-workout v4 set-list table (§2 above, `mezo-8xmf`): reuses `SetStepper` + the RIR/Side pill rows; `mode: 'logged' | 'pending'` (a not-yet-logged slot shows its target read-only, delete-only); the delete action is floored at one slot per exercise (`canDelete`, disabled + explanatory copy at the floor)

**API contract**
- `api/feature/train/train.yml` — the FE↔BE source of truth for Train

**Backend** (`backend/src/main/java/io/mrkuhne/mezo/feature/train/`)
- `controller/TrainController.java` — implements generated `TrainApi`; the meso-template CRUD/start/rerun endpoints delegate straight to `MesoTemplateService` (`mezo-meyc.1`)
- `service/{TrainService,WorkoutService,SportService,GymScheduleService,ExerciseCatalogService,ExerciseRecordService,RunningService}.java` — `ExerciseCatalogService` now has the catalog **write** methods `create`/`update`/`delete`/`setVideo`/`setImages` (author-or-OWNER gated, master media OWNER-only, 403 `EXERCISE_CATALOG_NOT_EDITABLE` / 409 `CATALOG_MASTER_READONLY`; `uniqueSlug` pre-probe + retry loop — S5 `mezo-qw37.5`) alongside `list` (`mezo-52zg`, `mezo-8xdl.1`). **`TrainService.stampRun`** (package-visible; `mezo-meyc.1`) is the extracted "materialize a run from a plan document" method every start funnels through — its `StampSource` record decouples the stitching from where the plan came from (today: `MesoTemplateEntity` via `MesoTemplateService.start`); `TrainService`'s own direct-create method is deleted along with the endpoint. `TrainService.closeMesocycle` (`mezo-meyc.2`, `:256`) now freezes the report in the close transaction, `hasReport` batching (`:110`).
- `service/MesocycleReportService.java` (`mezo-meyc.2`) — the deterministic report computer + reader (`computeAndStore`/`getReport`/`regenerate`); `mapper/MesoReportMapper.java` maps between the persisted jsonb records and the contract DTOs (report ⇄ `MesoReportJson`, arc ⇄ `MesocycleVolumeArcResponse`). `VolumeArcService.arc(createdBy, mesoId, effectiveCurrentWeek)` (package-visible 3-arg overload, `:65`) is the report-freeze entry point; the public 2-arg overload is unchanged. **`MesoReviewGate.java` + `MesocycleClosed.java`** (both `feature/train/`, `mezo-meyc.3`) are the AI half's two seams — the switch marker bean `getReport` reads for `aiEvalEnabled`, and the record the close/regenerate transactions publish for the companion's AFTER_COMMIT generator.
- `service/MesoTemplateService.java` — the template/run split's write half (`mezo-meyc.1`, ADR [0027](../decisions/0027-mesocycle-template-run-history.md)): template CRUD (full-replace `update`), `start` (hands the plan to `TrainService.stampRun`), `rerun` (materializes+links a template for a legacy run on first call, plain lookup after); all finders owner-scoped, missing/foreign → `TRAIN_MESO_TEMPLATE_NOT_FOUND` 404
- `service/CatalogMediaResolver.java` — the ONE batched `catalog_id → CatalogMedia(videoUrl, imageStartUrl, imageEndUrl)` resolve shared by `WorkoutService.getToday` and `TrainService.toDay` (renamed from `CatalogVideoResolver` in `mezo-8xdl.1`)
- `service/{MedalEvaluator,MedalService}.java` — the medal-collection engine (`mezo-wp6n`, §2 `Medálok` + §4): the pure rule surface + the replay/read service, derived-not-materialized (ADR 0016)
- `service/{SetRecommendationService,Prescription}.java` — hypertrophy-drive double-progression engine + its `(sets, rationale, progression)` result record (`mezo-dhdr` + `mezo-5pfe`)
- `service/ProgressionDecider.java` — pure, Spring-free RIR-aware + deload-aware double-progression decider (`mezo-5pfe`, §4 engine subsection); the only caller is `SetRecommendationService`
- `service/{VolumeProgressionService,VolumeDecider,MuscleGroup,MesoWeeks,VolumeArcService}.java` — Progressive Overload Plan 2 (`mezo-hi9m`, §4 `#### Volume progression`): the weekly per-muscle set-rollover engine (`VolumeProgressionService`), its pure decider (`VolumeDecider`), the 21-zone→coarse-group collapse (`MuscleGroup`), shared `startDate`-anchored week math (`MesoWeeks`, package-visible), and the read-only volume-arc endpoint's service (`VolumeArcService`). **`service/PriorityTier.java`** (`mezo-3m5m`, §4 `#### Volume progression`) — the Emphasize/Grow/Maintain enum, its sparse-map resolver (`of`) and `ceiling`, consumed by both `VolumeProgressionService` (the rollover) and `VolumeArcService` (the planned scaffold); `db/changelog/1.0.0/script/202608251200_mezo-3m5m_muscle_priorities.sql` — the `mesocycle`/`meso_template` `muscle_priorities jsonb` columns, no backfill.
- `config/HypertrophyProperties.java` — `mezo.hypertrophy.*` tuning (plate step, per-type increments, the count-keyed `warmupLadders`, `mezo-dnln`); `HypertrophyDriveGate.java` — `@ConditionalOnProperty` switch bean (`mezo.feature.hypertrophy-drive.enabled`)
- `config/VolumeProperties.java` — `mezo.volume.*` tuning (`step`/`deloadFraction`/`grindRirGap`); `VolumeProgressionGate.java` — `@ConditionalOnProperty` switch bean (`mezo.feature.volume-progression.enabled`, default true), mirrors `HypertrophyDriveGate`
- `service/{MesoPlanSkeleton,MesoPlanFiller,MesoPlanMerger,MesoPlanLlm,MesoPlanGeneratorService}.java` — the plan generator (mesocycle wizard redesign, §4 `#### Plan generator`): the pure split/set-frame builder (`MesoPlanSkeleton`), the pure deterministic exercise fill (`MesoPlanFiller`), the pure LLM-pick validator/merger (`MesoPlanMerger`, returns `MergeResult{days, appliedPicks}`), the train-owned port interface (`MesoPlanLlm`, Gemini half in `feature/companion/llm/MesoPlanLlmAdapter.java`), and the `@Service` orchestrator behind `POST /api/train/meso-plans/generate` (`MesoPlanGeneratorService` — deliberately NOT `@Transactional`, so the slow-tier LLM round-trip never holds a DB connection open). `config/MesoPlanProperties.java` — `mezo.meso-plan.*` tuning (`@Validated` record: `maxExercisesPerGroupPerDay`/compound+isolation rep ranges/`targetRir`/compound+isolation warmup counts).
- `entity/*` — `MesocycleEntity` (now `templateId`/`closedAt`), `WorkoutSessionEntity`, `ExerciseEntity` (recipe fields `warmupSets`/`workingSets`/`repMin`/`repMax`/`targetRir`/`anchorWeightKg`), `ExerciseSetEntity` (`kind`), `ExerciseFeedbackEntity`, `MuscleGroupVolumeLogEntity`, `ProvenanceEnvelope`, `VolumeRecomputeJson`, `SportSessionEntity`, `SportScheduleSlotEntity`, `GymScheduleSlotEntity`, `ExerciseCatalogEntity`, `RunningBlockEntity`, `RunningBlockStructure`, `RunSessionLogEntity`. **`MesoTemplateEntity`** (`mezo-meyc.1`) — the plan document, `days`/`volumePerMuscle` typed jsonb; `entity/json/{MesoDayJson,GymExerciseJson,VolumeBaselineJson}.java` are its record shapes. **`MesocycleReportEntity`** (`mezo-meyc.2`) — the frozen report row, `report`/`context` typed jsonb; `entity/json/{MesoReportJson,MesoContextJson}.java` are its record shapes (the latter is filled in by the companion's `MesoContextAssembler` since `mezo-meyc.3`, but stays **train-owned** — the companion computes it, train persists and freezes it). **`SportSlotSkipEntity`/`WorkoutDayAdjustmentEntity`** (S5, `mezo-d58h.5`, §4 above) — the two read-time overlay rows the advice card's mutation ports write; `service/{SportSlotSkipService,WorkoutDayAdjustmentRepository}` are their owning service/repository, called both from `feature/proactive`'s adapters and from every read path listed in §4.
- `repository/*` — notably `ExerciseRepository.findIdentityRowsIncludingDeleted`; `repository/MesoTemplateRepository.java` (`findByCreatedByAndDeletedFalseOrderByCreatedAtAsc`, `findByIdAndCreatedByAndDeletedFalse`); `repository/MesocycleRepository.java:25` — `countByTemplateIdAndCreatedByAndDeletedFalse`, the library's `runCount`. **`mezo-meyc.2`:** `repository/MesocycleReportRepository.java` (`findByMesocycleIdAndCreatedByAndDeletedFalse`, the `hasReport` batch existence query); `WorkoutSessionRepository.findCompletedMesoInstancesInWindow`; `ExerciseSetRepository.findWorkingSetsInSessions`. **`repository/{SportSlotSkipRepository,WorkoutDayAdjustmentRepository}.java`** (S5) — identity/date lookups behind the two overlays; both tables are in `support/ResetDatabase`'s TRUNCATE list.
- `mapper/{TrainMapper,RunningMapper}.java`
- `{TrainSeedData,RunningSeedData,ExerciseCatalogLoader}.java` — seed + master content loader
- `backend/src/main/resources/content/exercise-catalog.json` — catalog master data, **166 rows** (incl. the 2 plyo entries `db-jump-squat` / `single-leg-plate-hop`, the 22-row `kb-*` kettlebell block, `mezo-18g3`, and the 5 program gap-fillers of `mezo-41ei`); 124 rows carry `imageStartUrl`/`imageEndUrl` (`mezo-8xdl.2`)
- `scripts/data/exercise-image-map.json` + `scripts/import-exercise-images.mjs` — the hand-curated slug → free-exercise-db map and its by-hand importer
- `frontend/public/exercises/` — 248 vendored demo stills (public domain; precache-excluded, `CacheFirst` at runtime)
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/ExerciseImage.tsx` — the two-frame demo still (`hero` crossfade / `thumb`), `.exdemo*` classes in `styles/prototype.css`
- Migrations: `backend/src/main/resources/db/changelog/1.0.0/script/{202606111400_mezo-n5q_create_train, 202606120900_mezo-tod_t2_workout_execution, 202606121000_mezo-0ae_t3_sport_schedule, 202606121400_mezo-7ot_exercise_catalog, 202606141200_mezo-b4n_create_running_block, 202606141210_mezo-b4n_create_run_session_log, 202606151500_mezo-auk_create_gym_schedule_slot, 202607072000_mezo-dhdr_prescribed_sets, 202607082000_mezo-52zg_catalog_write, 202607141200_mezo-05v6_sport_schedule_slot_sport, 202607301900_mezo-wp6n_exercise_set_target, 202608161200_mezo-meyc.1_create_meso_template, 202608161210_mezo-meyc.1_mesocycle_template_link, 202608161600_mezo-meyc.2_create_mesocycle_report, 202609041000_mezo-d58h.5_sport_slot_skip, 202609041100_mezo-d58h.5_workout_day_adjustment}.sql` — the last two are S5's read-time overlays (§4 above), each owning a table `AdviceMutationPort` adapters write into from `feature/proactive`.

**Tests**
- FE: `frontend/src/features/train/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}` (incl. `logic/restTimer.test.ts`, `logic/useRestTimer.test.ts`, `components/WorkoutDock.test.tsx` (`mezo-88iwa.7` T6 — `RestTimerBar.test.tsx` deleted with it, T7 sweep), `components/SetStepper.test.tsx`, Napiv S5; `components/ProgressionBanner.test.tsx`, `components/PrepHero.test.tsx`, `components/PrepExerciseCard.test.tsx` (`mezo-5pfe`)), `frontend/src/data/train/trainHooks.test.tsx`, `train.emptyStates.test.tsx`, `train.nav.test.tsx`, plus the `hideChrome` case in `frontend/src/app/navigation.test.tsx` (Napiv S5); **mesocycle pages v2 (`mezo-d20.15`, deletes `pages/MesoOverviewPage.test.tsx` + the `DayDetailSheet`/`MesoOverview` tests):** `logic/mesoBands.test.ts`, `logic/mesoWeek.test.ts`, `pages/MesoDayPage.test.tsx`, `pages/MesoWeekPage.test.tsx`, `pages/MesoMusclePage.test.tsx`; `data/train/mesoTemplateHooks.test.ts`, `pages/MesoTemplateEditorPage.test.tsx`, `sheets/MesoStartSheet.test.tsx`, `logic/mesoDays.test.ts` (template/run split, `mezo-meyc.1`); `data/train/mesoReportHooks.test.tsx`, `pages/MesoReportPage.test.tsx`, `sheets/MesoCloseSheet.test.tsx` (run report + close flow, `mezo-meyc.2`) + the `test/msw/handlers.ts` fixtures for the report/close/regenerate endpoints; `logic/mesoCompare.test.ts` (exact-value helper tests incl. the null-volume, week-padding, no-overlap and `betterSide` tie/one-sided-null/regression edges), `pages/MesoComparePage.test.tsx` (the two mock fixture reports side by side + the missing-param / self-comparison / one-side-`notFound` states), and the compare-view cases (`mezo-meyc.4`: `riport` vs `nincs riport`, the third-tap-refused + toggle-pressed-state pair), which moved with the closed-run list onto `pages/MesoFutamokPage.test.tsx` in Train Titanium T10; **`mezo-tlwa`:** `logic/runToTemplate.test.ts` (field-for-field mapping, rest-day passthrough, `''`→`null`, empty days, baseline collapse + the skip/null fallbacks), `pages/MesoTemplatesPage.test.tsx` + `pages/MesoTemplateStoryPage.test.tsx` (the card's use-line, the story page's spelled-out week, duplicate → `(másolat)` + editor, the two-tap delete, real-mode skeleton + empty state), plus the runs-only guard in `pages/MesoKonyvtarPage.test.tsx` (no template cards / no dashed CTA, the `Sablonok · n` hub-mosaic tile → `/train/templates`, `Sablonná` → the new editor), the `Sablonná` + both-actions-hidden cases (now in `MesoFutamokPage.test.tsx`), `MesoReportPage.test.tsx`'s save-as-template case, and `train.nav.test.tsx`'s `Sablonok` navigation case; **the unified mezo-editor redesign (`mezo-yty6`):** `logic/mesoLoad.test.ts` (`weekMuscleLoad`/`dayMuscleLoad`/`adjacentDayConflicts`/`dayTone`), `components/ZoneBar.test.tsx`, `components/ExerciseCard.test.tsx` (the buffered-input contract), `components/LoadTile.test.tsx`, `components/DayStripTile.test.tsx`, `components/MesoDayEditor.test.tsx` (day-name buffer contract + the entrance-choreography-fires-once assertion), `components/MesoWeekEditor.test.tsx` (incl. the external-change buffered-name-input case), `components/WeekLoadPanel.test.tsx`, `components/DayLoadPanel.test.tsx`, `wizard/wizardState.test.ts` (updated for `step: 'interview' | 'editor'` + `renameDay`), and `pages/MesoTemplateEditorPage.test.tsx`'s `goalPreset`-coverage + debounced-rename cases
- BE: `*IT.java` (service + contract) incl. `WorkoutTodayProgressionIT` + the pure `ProgressionDeciderTest` (`mezo-5pfe`) + `support/populator/{TrainPopulator,RunningPopulator}.java`; Progressive Overload Plan 2 (`mezo-hi9m`): the pure `VolumeDeciderTest`, `VolumeProgressionServiceIT`, `VolumePropertiesIT`, `VolumeEffectiveSetsIT` + `VolumeEffectiveSetsSwitchOffIT` (the effective-set distribution, on/off), `VolumeArcContractIT`; **`MesoTemplateIT`** (`mezo-meyc.1`) — template CRUD/ownership, stamp correctness (days/exercises/volume rows copied), single-active invariant, planned start, legacy rerun materialization, the jsonb round-trip of `meso_template`'s `Map<String, record>` `volumePerMuscle` column — + `support/populator/MesoTemplatePopulator.java`; **`MesocycleCloseReportIT`** (`mezo-meyc.2`, 9 tests, mutation-checked) — report math (adherence/strength/records) on populated data, idempotent close, self-eval capture + fill-on-reclose, legacy backfill via regenerate, the `TRAIN_MESO_REPORT_NOT_FOUND`/`TRAIN_MESO_NOT_CLOSED` error paths — over the extended `TrainPopulator`; **`MesocycleCloseReportIT` grew the `MesocycleClosed` publish/no-publish matrix** (`mezo-meyc.3`, `@RecordApplicationEvents`: real close publishes, an idempotent re-close and the fill-if-null self-eval branch do NOT, every accepted regenerate does) and runs with **`mezo.feature.companion.enabled=false`** so the companion's async review listener cannot race its `context`-null / status-`pending` assertions; **`MesoReviewSwitchOffIT`** (`mezo-meyc.3`, own `@TestPropertySource` context) covers `aiEvalEnabled: false` + "context written, AI half left pending". The generator itself is tested from the companion side — `feature/companion/MesoReviewGeneratorIT` ([`companion.md`](companion.md) §10). **Plan generator (mesocycle wizard redesign):** pure `MesoPlanSkeletonTest`/`MesoPlanFillerTest`/`MesoPlanMergerTest` (`service/`), `MesoPlanPropertiesBindingIT` (`config/`), `MesoStartTierSeedIT`, `MesoPlanGenerateContractIT`, `MesoPlanGenerateAiIT` (`companion-fake` profile — exercises the `MesoPlanLlmAdapter` end-to-end through the port, there is no adapter-only unit test) — see §4 `#### Plan generator` and §8 above for detail

**Related docs** — specs/plans in `docs/superpowers/specs|plans/` (dated `2026-06-10`…`2026-06-14`, plus the `2026-07-25` Progressive Overload design spec + the Plan 1 (`2026-07-25-progressive-overload-p1-intensity-signal.md`, `mezo-5pfe`) and Plan 2 (`2026-07-25-progressive-overload-p2-volume-overview.md`, `mezo-hi9m`) implementation plans, and the `2026-08-01-set-budget-unified-editor-design.md` set-budget + unified-editor design (`mezo-7rdg`, §2/§4 above)); house standards in `docs/references/*.md`. **Mesocycle history — epic COMPLETE** (template/run split S1 + deterministic close report S2 + cross-domain context/AI evaluation S3 + two-run compare view S4, `mezo-meyc`, all four slices shipped): design spec [`2026-08-16-mesocycle-history-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-08-16-mesocycle-history-design.md), ADR [0027](../decisions/0027-mesocycle-template-run-history.md).
